# App Context Changelog

Newest first. One entry per merged change that alters behavior, schema, process or backups. Keep entries short; the detail lives in the module dossier and the PR.

Format: `YYYY-MM-DD · PR · what · migrations (version → apply state)`

## 2026-11-23 — Modules launcher: four big blocks you choose, and a count on every tile (PR #985 open, not merged) · migration `20270821000000` APPLIED to production 2026-11-23 14:02 IST (before the merge)

- **Why.** Owner: redesign the Modules page with four big blocks, show the notification number on every smaller tile as the big blocks do, and let the owner change which modules are big. A finished-looking mock-up (1920, 1366, phone, Edit order) was approved first; the owner chose Sales as the fourth block and "save to account".
- **What.** `src/app/modules/page.tsx` + `modules.module.css`: up to four big blocks (default Task, Order, Finance, Sales; four across from 1040 px, two by two below), chosen in Edit order with a "Make big / Big block" button per card (at four the rest are disabled; Reset restores the default order and four). Every tile says its count under the name: red "N unread" (Quotations "N waiting"), "Nothing new" for a known zero, "No alerts" for a module with no notifications. The red number on a small tile's icon is removed; on a phone's list the chip sits at the row's right end. Counts are now read for Sales, Assets & Access, Inventory, Review Workflow (verifiers only), Attendance & Payroll and Quotations as well as Task, Finance, Order and Sample Tracking, each only for a module the person may open, through the same one batched request. `src/lib/modules/moduleOrder.ts` (rules + reducer) and `useModuleOrder.ts` (one read of both lists, a save that writes the big blocks only when they changed).
- **Migration.** `20270821000000_launcher_big_module_keys.sql`: `user_module_order.big_module_keys text[]` (nullable, no default; NULL = never chosen, `{}` = none) and check `user_module_order_big_keys_valid` (the shared shape validator, at most 4). No policy, grant, trigger or function touched; self-asserting. **APPLIED 2026-11-23 14:02 IST** on the owner's yes, after a `backup-all` (279 tables, 473,013 rows, 0 errors, pg_dump of the three restricted finance tables OK, `verify-backups` "All required layers are fresh") and a rehearsal on the live database inside `begin … rollback` (its own assertions passed, no residue); applied with `supabase db query --linked --file` in one transaction (`lock_timeout` 5 s) and recorded with `migration repair --status applied`, so `migration list --linked` shows `remote` populated. After the apply: column and check present, 3 saved card orders untouched, every `big_module_keys` NULL (the default four apply). Rehearsed in an in-memory Postgres (PGlite) on top of the real `20261228000000`: 23 checks (cap, empty, NULL, five/duplicate/bad/null/path refused, upserts, RLS read/write/forge, anon and delete refused, second apply, rollback) all pass. Ship order was migration, then merge (the launcher falls back to the order alone if the column is missing, but a Save of the big blocks would fail until it exists); the migration is now in place, so the merge can go ahead.
- **Checked.** `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; eslint 0 on the changed files; the 41 test files that name the changed code plus the new `launcherBigBlocks.test.ts` (32 tests). Not checked: a signed-in look at the real page (owner tests before commit), the full `npm test` and `next build` (CI runs them), the page-load timing with up to ten counts for an administrator (counts do not hold up the page).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; the column is harmless unused (`alter table public.user_module_order drop column big_module_keys;` removes it with its constraint).

## 2026-11-23 — Orders and Finance notifications: major activity only (PR #979 merged as `586aaf2e` by the owner 2026-11-23 12:08 IST, production deployment succeeded 12:11 IST) · no migration

- **Why.** Owner: the Orders and Finance Notifications pages were always full of routine notices. Keep a notice only when a named person must act, is stuck until they hear back, or something went wrong; routine progress already has a home (dashboard queues, the Order's Activity trail, the "New update" mark on Confirmed Orders). A plan page with the per-event table was approved first.
- **What.** `src/lib/notifications.ts`: `FINANCE_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` (now 5) and `ORDER_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` (now 18) are the SHOWN lists, so the feed, the sidebar unread count, Mark all read, Delete all and phone alerts (`pushPolicy.ts`) all follow them. New `FINANCE_ROUTINE_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` (8) and `ORDER_ROUTINE_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` (10) hold the hidden types. New `MAJOR_ONLY_NOTE`: one line under the filters in `NotificationsView` (Orders and Finance only) saying where routine updates went. Control Center → Order notifications has a note on what its switches now decide.
- **Not changed.** Nothing is deleted and nothing stops being written: old rows of a hidden type stay in `notifications`, hidden, and come back if the type moves to the shown list. The `order_update_*` rows still feed the "New update" mark on Confirmed Orders, so a later "stop writing hidden types" step must not drop them. No migration, no new route.
- **Checked.** `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors, eslint 0 on the changed files, 1001 tests across the 32 test files that name the changed code plus the push and navigation guards, 0 failed (new `majorOnlyInbox.test.ts`; `hiddenOrderTypes` and `orderUpdateNotifications` updated). Not checked: a signed-in look at both inboxes, the full `npm test` and `next build` (CI runs them).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR. No data was changed.

## 2026-11-23 — Sales: the Lead Board's columns start with the page, and what the three speed PRs measured live (PR open, not merged) · no migration
- **Why.** After #977, #978 and #980 went live (10:21, 10:30 and 10:36 IST) the same timing as the baseline was taken in the owner's signed-in Chrome (admin, desktop, repeat visits, reads only). It showed one gap in #977: the Board's totals started at about 0.3 s, but its five column reads at about 1.0 to 1.2 s, after the access answer, because the columns are children of a body that was still held back for `ready` (`if (!ready) body = <BoardBlocks />`).
- **What.** `BoardScreen` no longer waits for `ready`: the guard (`ModuleGuardStartsEarly`) builds the page hidden and shows it only on a yes, so the columns and their reads start with the page and each column shows card-shaped blocks until its first page arrives. On a phone only the open tab is read: the page is built after hydration, so the screen-width check is right from its first render.
- **Measured live after #977 / #978 / #980 (repeat visits, one account, run-to-run noise is large; the baseline is in memory `project_sales_module_speed_audit_23nov`).** A Sales page's own first request now starts at about 0.17 s (it started at 1.0 to 1.5 s). Data complete: My day 0.39 to 0.93 s (was about 1.5 s), Team 0.71 s (was 2.3 s), Lead Master 0.93 s (was 2.4 s), Board cards 1.2 to 1.55 s (was 2.3 to 2.7 s; this PR should take the columns' start from about 1.0 s to about 0.35 s). The lead popup: the quotations and the record are asked together (+98 ms and +104 ms) and both are in by about 0.38 s (was about 0.63 s). A Lead Master period card changes the address at once and makes exactly one request (`sales_leads_page`, 0.25 s), no server page request. Clicking between pages (no reload) did NOT change within noise: Board 1.47 s, Team 1.2 s, Lead Master 0.89 s, My day 0.72 s (before: 1.05, 1.5, n/a, 0.45): those are the database's own times; the next lever is the database. One first load of My day took 5.5 s to DOMContentLoaded and one Notifications load 22 s earlier: not reproduced, treat as network noise.
- **Not measured.** The saved-action path (#978) was not exercised live (it writes); the phone layout; any salesperson's account (an Admin's permission check is a one-row shortcut, so a salesperson is probably slower).
- **Checked.** tsc and eslint clean; the 379 tests of the Sales folders pass (`boardSpeed.test.ts` now pins that nothing on the board waits for `ready`; `loadShape.test.tsx` updated).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR.
## 2026-11-23 — Sales: pages wait in their own shape, the Lead Master's filters stop asking the server, the popup reads quotations beside the record (PR #980 merged as `27e81248` on 23 Nov 10:34 IST, live 10:36 IST) · no migration
- **Why.** Owner, 23 Nov: Sales is slow. Found while measuring: (1) the Lead Master changed its address through `router.replace` on every period card, filter and search, which asks the server for the page again each time (documented on the Lead Board at 0.1 to 3.4 s before a filter took effect; the Board was fixed on 14 Nov, the Lead Master was not); (2) My day, Team, the Lead Master and the Board's first answer waited behind `LoadingScreen`, the 100vh spinner `ModulePageSkeleton` says is wrong inside a module whose shell is drawn; (3) the popup read the lead's quotations only after the record arrived (+0.25 s).
- **What.** (1) `LeadMasterScreen` uses `useListUrlState(LIST_PARAMS, { shallow: true })`. (2) My day, Team and the Lead Master draw `ModulePageSkeleton variant="list"`, the Board draws `BoardBlocks` (the blocks the page frame drew while the check ran, now exported), and a column waits with `SkeletonCards` instead of the word Loading. (3) `LeadQuotes` reads `quotation_for_lead` at once (the lead's id is known from the card) and draws nothing until the record has arrived, as before.
- **Tests.** New `loadShape.test.tsx` (the skeletons, the shallow mode, and a DOM test that the quotations are asked once, beside the record); `boardSpeed.test.ts` (the Lead Master is no longer pinned as non-shallow; the board pins `BoardBlocks`) and `leadQuotes.dom.test.tsx` (asked while filling, still nothing drawn) updated on purpose. tsc and eslint clean; the 402 tests of the Sales folders pass.
- **Not done, on purpose.** A smaller first page for each Board column (20 instead of 40) was considered and dropped: nothing measured says the database cost is in the rows, so it waits for the query-plan measurement.
- **Not verified.** Not seen in a browser by the builder; no timing yet. Expected: Lead Master filter clicks apply at once, pages show a page-shaped placeholder instead of a spinner, the popup's quotations line arrives with the record.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR.
## 2026-11-23 — Sales: a saved action shows on every screen at once and reads only what changed (PR #978 merged as `f7e91f93` on 23 Nov 10:28 IST, live 10:30 IST) · no migration
- **Why.** Owner, 23 Nov: Sales is slow. After every action (No answer, a move, a note, a follow-up date, a heat change, Undo) `useLeadWork.guarded` ran `invalidateQueries([sales-leads])`: every mounted query read again, each Board column replaying ALL its loaded pages one after another, plus the totals, My day and the popup, and the screen caught up a second or two after the toast. The database already answers each action with the lead as it is now, and that answer was thrown away.
- **What.** New `src/lib/salesLeads/cache.ts`: the answer is put into My day (the row is replaced, or taken off when it is sure the lead no longer belongs), into every Board column (replaced where it stands; taken out of the column it left; at the top of the column it moved into when the filter would show it; every page's count kept in step) and into the open popup. Then only what may have changed is read again: My day, the board totals, the columns the lead was in or went to, and that lead's popup; everything else is marked old and is read when next opened. A refused or failed action, or one that answered with no lead, still reads everything again as before. Wired through `guarded(run)` (it now hands `run` a `note(lead, from)`), `doMove`, Undo, and the popup's heat, follow-up date, facts and update box (`work.settle`).
- **Not changed.** The Lead Master and Team actions (allotting changes holders and counts across lists) still read everything again; nothing in the database.
- **Checked.** tsc and eslint clean; 19 new tests in `cache.test.ts` (the rules, and a real query client proving which queries are read again and which are only marked old); the 372 tests of the Sales folders pass. Not seen in a browser by the builder; the gain (fewer requests after a save, the change visible before the database's second round) is expected, not measured.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR (`guarded` goes back to `invalidateQueries` on every outcome).
## 2026-11-23 — Sales: every Sales page starts while the access check runs (PR #977 merged as `9d2ec62e` on 23 Nov 10:19 IST, live 10:21 IST) · no migration
- **Why.** Owner, 23 Nov: Sales is slow. Measured live (admin, repeat visit): a Sales page's own first request started 0.5 to 1.1 s late on every new-tab load, because `ModuleGuard` mounts nothing until the permission answer arrives (the answer itself is 0.2 to 1.0 s, then 0.1 to 0.4 s of render before the page's reads fire). My day content came at about 1.5 s, the Lead Board at 2.3 to 2.7 s, Team and the Lead Master at about 2.4 s.
- **What.** `src/app/sales/layout.tsx` uses `ModuleGuardStartsEarly` (Task Management's guard since 19 Nov) with a new optional `checking` frame: while the answer is pending the page is built hidden and inert behind the existing static `SalesCheckingFrame` (no spinner, as since 14 Nov); yes shows the same page without building it again; no or no session removes it and shows the spinner while the redirect lands. The page's reads no longer wait for the answer: My day, the Lead Master list and people, and the Team reads (the Team reads stop if the answer says the person is not a manager; the link is only offered to managers). Only the held-chats count still waits (`ready && canAssign`).
- **Safe because.** Every Sales read is a database function that checks the caller itself; nothing is drawn or focusable until the guard says yes; `ModuleGuard` is untouched. New audit test `startDuringCheckSales.test.ts` fails if an effect in the Sales tree writes on mount (none does today: notifications are marked read only on a click).
- **Checked.** tsc and eslint clean; the guard and Sales test files (210 tests) pass, including 4 new DOM tests for the frame. Not seen in a browser by the builder; timing to be measured live after the deploy (expected 0.5 to 1 s sooner on every new-tab load; unproven).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR: `src/app/sales/layout.tsx` back to `ModuleGuard` and the three `enabled` lines back to `ready`.
## 2026-11-23 — SEO discussion brief: a short version on top, and a total under every part (PR #981 merged as `97b0fdbc` on 23 Nov 11:56 IST, live 11:59 IST) · no migration
- **Why.** Owner, 23 Nov: the brief should start with what went well, then why it did not reach sales, then how many searches improved and fell with an approximate percentage and why, say the figures are Google's own record averaged over everyone and not one browser, keep the detail after it, show a total wherever there are several results, and use bar and pie charts.
- **What.** A brief with a `summary` (and a report block `{ kind: 'summary' }`) draws the short version first: three sections (what went well, why it did not reach sales, how the searches moved) with a one-line conclusion, figures, charts and reasons, then a "where these numbers come from" box. New `src/lib/seo/moves.ts` counts every search the same way (`up`, `same`, `down`, `out`, `new`, `thin`; first quarter against the newest firm reading in the last two columns), and the page uses it for the donut, the per-category bars, a total above the rankings table, `▲ ▼` on each category heading and the "Since" column (`Out of view`, `Newly in view` replace a dash). The door (`briefProblem`) checks chart kinds and that the counting charts have rankings. Nothing else in the brief changed shape; an older brief draws as before.
- **Data.** Brief v7 (`period_end` 2026-11-23): rankings refreshed to 1 Oct–21 Nov 2026 (Search Console, India, 134 searches in four groups), latest 28-day totals read worldwide, Google's November 2026 core update recorded as still rolling out and not counted as a verdict. Sent at about 12:00 IST on 23 Nov, after the deploy (the old door would refuse the new part), and read live as the owner in Chrome: the short version first with its counts and charts as built, no sideways scroll at desktop width and in a 375 px frame. Not yet seen on a real phone; the screenshot tool timed out, so the check was by page text and layout measures.
- **Checked.** `moves.test.ts` (9), `sixMonth.test.ts` (summary kinds), `seoScreens.test.tsx` (summary order, counts, totals); 140 related tests, tsc and eslint clean; the real brief drawn with the real stylesheets at desktop and phone width (no sideways scroll). Not seen signed in.
- **Rollback.** Revert the code. A stored brief with a `summary` block then just skips that block (older code draws nothing for a block kind it does not know), and the next send of an older-shaped brief passes the door as before.

- **Why.** Owner: clients ask for a quotation with fabric, finish and packing, which the team priced on a separate Excel sheet. The sums become the Fabric and Packing amounts of the totals once any price is typed (the boxes turn read-only) and are written into the two existing columns on every save, so the list value, PDF, Excel and PI draft are untouched. New source "Client's code, we source it" for a fabric. Client copy, versions and the client link carry nothing new (PR 2 shows per-piece figures, PR 3 the PI draft).

No existing row changes.
- **Checked.** `costing.test.ts` (17), `costingPage.dom.test.tsx` (11), `quotations_pglite.mjs` (152 checks; the new block proves apply, store, return, refusals and no leak to the client link or a frozen version). Both migration ledgers in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts` list the new file. Not seen signed in.
- **Rollback.** Revert the code; by hand re-create the two functions from 20270808000100 and drop the column and the checkers (in the migration header). Nothing else depends on the column.

## 2026-11-21 — Orders: the approver's payment chips in one call (PR open, not merged; follows #969, which is merged) · migration 20270818000000 (NOT APPLIED: waits for the owner's go)
- **Why.** After #969 the widest part of the approver's `/orders` was the last round: one `pi_submission_payment_summary` request per PI row on the "New PIs to approve" card, up to 12 at once. Owner said yes to drafting the one-call version.
- **What.** New function `pi_submissions_payment_summaries(uuid[])`: up to 50 PIs in, one row (`submission_id`, `summary`) per PI the caller may view out. It calls the existing single-PI function once per id, so no payment rule is copied and every summary is exactly that function's answer. `loadPiPaymentSummaries` (`lib/finance/piPaymentView.ts`) uses it and lines the answers up by id; a PI with no row has no chip, as a refused single read had none. Where the function is not on the database yet, or the batch fails, it asks per PI exactly as before, so merging before applying breaks nothing (it only costs one failed request).
- **Access.** Runs as the caller (`security invoker`), touches no table itself; the single function is still `security definer`, still needs a signed-in caller and still refuses a PI the caller may not view (error 42501), and only that refusal is turned into "no row". Any other error fails the whole call and the screen falls back. At most 50 ids. Executable by signed-in users only (not `anon`, not `public`).
- **Before / after.** Requests for the chips: up to 12 → 1. Sequential rounds unchanged (2): the chips were already in round 2, they are just no longer 12 wide.
- **Rehearsed, not applied.** The migration text was run on an in-memory Postgres (PGlite) against a stand-in single-PI function: applies, applies twice, security invoker + stable + fixed search path, anon refused and signed-in allowed, not signed in refused, 51 ids refused and 50 accepted, an unviewable PI and a null id give no row, duplicates once in first-asked order, 12 batch answers equal the 12 single answers, a non-42501 error fails the whole call, drop function reverses it and re-applying works. It does NOT prove the real function behaves like the stand-in; the real database was not touched.
- **Rollout (owner's call).** Needs no backup (adds one function, changes no table or data). Order: `supabase db push --linked --dry-run`, then apply `20270818000000_pi_payment_summaries_batch.sql`, then merge. Rollback: `drop function public.pi_submissions_payment_summaries(uuid[]);` (the screen then asks per PI). Because the code falls back per PI, merging first is harmless, but apply first anyway (merge deploys to production).
- **Tests.** New `piPaymentSummariesBatch.test.ts` (the helper: one call, by id, missing id, empty, fallback; the migration text: wrapper only, invoker, stable, 42501 only, 50 cap, grants, rollback line). `adminDashboard.test.tsx` now expects the one call. The migration is listed in both ledgers of `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`.
- **Not verified.** Not run against the real database or seen on the live site; no timing.

## 2026-11-21 — Expenses: paste (Ctrl+V) or drag and drop a bill (PR open, not merged) · no migration
- **Why.** The owner asked for copy-and-paste of the attachment in the Add Expense form (a screenshot of a UPI payment or a bill is usually already on the clipboard), then for drag and drop too, "as already done on other pages".
- **What.** `useBillIntake` (`ExpenseBills.tsx`) feeds both bill surfaces: `PendingBillPicker` (Add / Complete form, off while saving) and `ExpenseBills` (Edit form and the bills window, when the person may attach). Drop uses the shared `useDragAndPaste` hook and a dashed area (`BillDropZone`) that holds the button and says "or drag a bill here, or press Ctrl+V to paste a copied bill or screenshot" (the same pattern as the payment-proof `ProofDropZone`); paste is a document `paste` listener. A file dropped BESIDE the area is refused by a document `dragover`/`drop` guard, so the browser cannot open it and leave the half-filled form. `pastedBillFiles` / `nameBillFiles` (`lib/finance/expenseReimbursements.ts`) leave any paste that carries text alone (Excel copies a picture beside its cells) and name a nameless screenshot `expense-bill-YYYY-MM-DD-HHMMSS.png` using the proof intake's `isGenericProofName`.
- **Same rules.** The type list and the 10 MB limit are the picker's own (`billFileProblem`); upload path, storage rule and bill row are unchanged.
- **Tests.** New group in `expenseReimbursements.test.ts` (screenshot and dropped-file naming, real names kept, text paste left alone, unsupported file still reported, both surfaces wired, stray-drop guard present).
- **Not done / not verified.** Not tried in a browser (owner tests first), so the drop highlight and the look of the dashed area are unseen. No Paste button for phones. The listeners are document-wide, so two bill areas on screen at once would both take a paste; none exists today.
- **Rollout.** UI only. Rollback: revert the PR.

## 2026-11-21 — Orders: the approver's dashboard reads in two rounds, not three (PR open, not merged) · no migration
- **Why.** The owner reported the app hanging for a few seconds while moving between pages. The database is in Tokyo and the people are in India, so each call costs 200+ ms and what hurts is how many calls wait for the one before. Counted from the code on `main`, the approver's `/orders` waited in a chain: the three dashboard reads, then the five lists behind the cards, then the names, the payment chips, the send-back comments and the salespeople. This does not claim to fix the console errors or the multi-second hang he described: those are not captured yet.
- **What.** (1) The five lists behind the approver's cards (PIs in review, revised PIs, documents, flagged PIs, change requests for an acting admin) now start in the same moment as the page's three dashboard reads, instead of after them. Only a reader who can approve PIs starts them, never under View As. (2) The people's names come back inside those same requests (a join over the foreign key to users), so there is no separate names call afterwards; the Documents list does the same. (3) What is left after the first round (one payment summary per PI, the send-back comments, the flagged orders' salespeople) starts the instant the first round answers.
- **Before / after, sequential rounds for the approver (after the access check, which is shared by every Orders page and unchanged).** Before: 3 (dashboard reads; the five lists; names, payment chips, send-back comments, salespeople). After: 2 (dashboard reads together with the five lists; payment chips, send-back comments, salespeople). Requests: up to two fewer (the two names lookups, one per list, each made only when a row names somebody); nothing else added or removed. Not measured in milliseconds, on purpose.
- **Same rules.** Every read still runs as the signed-in reader under their own rights; a failed PI read is still an error line, never "nothing is waiting"; nothing is read under View As; the newest read still wins (the page's ticket); a list started for one reader or one set of rights is never used for another (it reads for itself instead); the PI Drafts link rule and the revenue rule are untouched (revenue still arrives only for readers who see every order).
- **Left alone, and why.** The two queue-count reads at the foot of the page still start after the first draw (a 22 Oct decision: each extra simultaneous call finishes about 70 ms later, so the first round was kept narrow); the unread-count call for the bell badge (`/api/notifications?count=1&category=order`, the slowest in the owner's Network tab) is already in the first round, because the loading frame shows the same sidebar, so it was not changed. The payment chips are still up to 12 separate calls in the last round: one database function could make that one call (needs a migration, so it waits for the owner's word).
- **Tests.** New group in `adminDashboard.test.tsx` (names come with the rows and `users` is never read; the second round holds only what depends on the first; composing again reads nothing; a failed PI read is null; each open table is read once; an early read is used only for the reader and rights it was started for; page order and wiring). Three pinned tests changed on purpose because the page's first group is now a named promise so the lists can start between sending and awaiting it: `backWithOperations.test.ts` and `ordersDashboardFewerReads.test.ts` (the group is still the same three dashboard reads, still no table read in it). `orderStartupShape.test.ts` is unchanged: the page file still holds exactly five reads.
- **Not verified.** Not seen against live data or on the live site, not timed in a browser; the embeds were checked against the live schema without reading any rows. The first round is now wider (about nine requests at once instead of four), and the 22 Oct measurement says each extra simultaneous call finishes about 70 ms later, so whether the saved round outweighs that is unmeasured: check the Network tab on the Vercel preview before merging. The page's own three reads are sent first on purpose.

## 2026-11-20 — Orders: the approver's dashboard is one "Needs your action" sheet and a "Watching" list (PR #965, squash-merged by the owner as `076b6668` on 20 Nov 2026 at 23:41 IST; deployed 23:44 IST (Vercel production success)) · no migration
- **Why.** The owner (20 Nov 2026): "Orders to watch" is not wanted on `/orders`; he needs the new PIs to approve, the changes on running orders, the clarifications Operations asked him, and what he has passed on and is still waiting for, each with how long and from or with whom, and no value. Previewed and approved as dummy-data pages first (final: version 7 of the preview), then built.
- **What.** `AdminDashboardView` → `DecisionQueue` (reads) → `DecisionCards` (draws). **Needs your action** is one white sheet of three groups (New PIs to approve · Changes on running orders · Clarifications from Operations) sharing one column grid: order or PI number, client, the ask (status as icon and words), from, the wait with its day, one button. The longest wait is the "Start with" row: the only solid button and the only tinted row. **Watching** (what is with Sales or Operations) is the same grid flat on the page, "with Mohit", a quiet chevron, a filter by person (longest-waiting person first). One header row per section, no column-label row; a group's name shows only when the sheet has two or more groups; "Start with" only when there is a choice. **An empty group, section and heading are not drawn**; when nothing waits anywhere one line says so, never when a read failed. Design Files and Client PO submissions are rows of "Changes on running orders" (the app calls them document changes on an Order). Operations' answers on revised PIs (a "cannot do" or "to discuss", the version still in force, answered within 14 days: `OPERATIONS_ANSWER_DAYS`) are rows of "Clarifications from Operations". The revenue strip and "Orders to watch" are no longer drawn (`AdminRevenueCard` and `AdminOrdersBoard` stay in the tree, unused). The page no longer draws `OperationsResponses` for the approver view.
- **Which group a row sits in** is who holds the next move now (`splitCards`, `flaggedRow`): a flagged PI waiting on the approver → Clarifications; Sales replied → Changes; sent to Sales → Watching. Orders waiting on the Operations reviewer come from the page's own alignment list (`awaitingReviewRow`); with nobody assigned the row says "No reviewer" (an administrator must assign one). Orders held up only by payment are no longer on this dashboard.
- **Data.** No migration. One extra read in `loadDecisionRows`: the salesperson of each flagged order (`orders` + `users!assigned_to`), only when something is flagged, in the same burst as the names. The page's first burst is unchanged. `DecisionRow` gained `card`, `who`, `next`, `order`, `round`; `isMixed` was removed. "Sales" is the Order's salesperson: the database records the holder as a team, not a person.
- **Proof.** `adminDashboard.test.tsx` and `flaggedPisScreens.test.tsx` rewritten for the new screen (74 tests: placement, one header, empty things not drawn, failed read never "all clear", order number leads, Start with, Watching, documents as changes, page wiring). Full suite, lint and type check clean at the time of writing. The owner saw an earlier build on a local copy against his own data; the final build has not been seen signed in by the session.
- **Not done.** Line-by-line changes of a revised PI for the approver need a new read (the differences only exist once the handoff is created). An approver asking Sales for a change on a running order outside the flagged-PI chain does not exist. The Operations team's dashboard (Nitish) is PR 2 and still the old one.
- **Rollout.** UI only: merging deploys it, no backup step. Rollback: revert the PR.

## 2026-11-20 — Quotations: typing in a product's Material and Remarks boxes no longer waits on the price model (branch `fix/quotation-typing-lag`, PR #962) · no migration
- **Why.** The owner's Vercel toolbar (Interaction Timing) showed about 138 ms for every keystroke in the two product textareas (`ProductLine.tsx`, the `input` + `t3` classes), above the 100 ms where typing starts to feel laggy.
- **Cause.** Every keystroke sets the lines, which re-runs `modelFor` in `BuilderScreen`: `priceLine` for every product, and each call refitted the price model from scratch (`fitType` re-reads every point of the price pool, up to 2,500 of them, parsing each one's words, then re-solves the regression) although the fit depends only on the pool and the product type, never on the line. Cost = products × pool size on every key.
- **What.** `priceLine(points, line, now, fits?)` takes an optional Map that keeps one fit per product type (a type with too few prices is kept too, as `null`); `BuilderScreen` makes one Map per pool (`useMemo` on `poolQ.data`) and passes it. Same prices, reasons, confidence and ranges: a new test in `model.test.ts` compares a kept fit with a fresh one and proves the pool is not read again. No screen text or layout changed.
- **Measured.** The real `model.ts` on synthetic pools (not the owner's data) with 12 products on a quiet machine: 500 / 2,000 / 5,000 prices cost about 140 / 830 / 2,600 ms per keystroke before and under 2 ms after (the first pricing after the pool arrives still pays one fit per type). The real pool size and product count of the owner's quotation are not known, and it was not timed in the browser signed in.
- **Not done.** Every product row still re-renders on each keystroke (`LineRow` is not memoized and its callbacks are rebuilt each render): not measured; the next step only if typing is still above 40 ms in the toolbar after this is live.

- **Why.** The owner (20 Nov 2026), from the Dashboard: an option to delete a quotation as an administrator (many test quotations, some added by mistake), and everyone able to delete until it is submitted or shared for price approval; after that, modify only. Reverses the 12 Nov default "Archive, never delete" for those two cases.
- **What.** The rule lives in `quotation_delete_blocker` (never a PI-linked quotation; an administrator any other; everyone else only their own unsent draft: Draft, never shared, never sent to a senior, price approval never asked). `quotation_delete`, `quotation_delete_check`, `quotation_list.may_delete`, a record in `quotation_deletions`. - **Proof.** `quotations_pglite.mjs` 146 checks (6 new, including the old refusal before the migration and the same delete working after it); DOM tests for the confirm, the Status card button and the Dashboard bin. Not seen signed in; PGlite proves behaviour, not the real schema.

- **Start from a lead.** `quotation_start(lead, data)` copies the lead's name, phone, e-mail, city, venue and requirement into a new linked draft (Dead lead refused; `data.owner_id` puts it on a salesperson's page, Admin / pricing desk only; the number carries the OWNER's initials); `quotation_makers()` lists who it may be put on. `20270814000100` then replaces `quotation_create` so it needs `lead_id` and is `quotation_start` (APPLY IT ONLY AFTER the code is live). Old Task requests are NOT deleted: they stay readable under "Earlier requests" (and in Task Detail).
- **Report cards.** `quotation_cards(from, to, person)` in one call; `HomeCards.tsx` on the list: your open quotations, with a senior, made / value, conversion; the desk: waiting for you (oldest), answered and how fast; Admin / see all: most quotations, team total, confirmed, conversion, and a candidate-by-candidate table that opens that person's cards. Periods: last month (default), this month, last 3 months (India calendar months).

Always asked, always optional (Skip); stored as `pricing_feedback` rows (kind `price_reason`, status done) by `quotation_price_briefs_save`; read back to pre-fill. - **Why.** The second of three pieces the owner approved on 19 Nov 2026 ("Yes to all above") after the Lead card on the quotation (#949): from the lead, see its quotations and start one.
- **What.** `quotation_for_lead(lead)` returns `{ open, may_create, quotations }`: the quotations linked to the lead that the caller may see (owner, creator, view_all or Admin), newest first, at most 20, each with the latest SENT version's number and value (the saved value before any is sent); `open` is false for somebody with no Quotations access, `may_create` is Quotations create or Admin; a lead the caller cannot see answers nothing. `LeadQuotes.tsx`: ONE slim line in the popup's right card, between the update box and the activity: the newest quotation (a link, "v2 · Sent", value), "+N more" (opens the rest as rows, newest first), and "Make quotation" (shown only for somebody who may work the open lead and may create quotations). Make quotation: `quotation_create` (client name = the lead's name or number, contact name and phone, source = the lead's source label), then `quotation_link_lead`, then the builder opens; if the link fails the quotation still opens and its Lead card offers the same lead by phone. The lead's requirement and venue are NOT copied (`quotation_create` takes neither): they come with the contact-role piece. - **Proof.** `quotations_pglite.mjs` 112 checks (8 new: an empty list, the owner sees only their linked ones with the sent version's number and value, newest first, the desk and Admin see all, a lead the caller cannot see answers nothing, no Quotations access answers `open: false`, an unlinked quotation drops off, closed to signed-out callers), `leadQuotes.dom.test.tsx` (9, the real band in jsdom), tsc, eslint, the existing Sales tests.

The popup hides the line while the function is missing, so the code and the migration can go out in either order; apply before the merge anyway. Rollback: `drop function public.quotation_for_lead(uuid);`. One more small request when a lead opens (after the record is in, with `retry: false`).

## 2026-11-19 — Quotations: a quotation can be linked to its Sales lead, and a Lead card offers one tap on that lead (PR #949, squash-merged by the owner as `1bf5169d` on 19 Nov 2026 at 22:44 IST; deployed 22:47 IST (Vercel production success)) · migration `20270811000000_quotation_lead_link.sql` (one nullable column + index on `quotations`, two functions; APPLIED 19 Nov 2026 about 22:35 IST after the 22:01 IST backup, before the merge; recorded in the ledger)
- **Why.** A quotation and its lead were two pipelines nobody joined: a sent quotation had to be logged by hand on the lead to unlock Negotiation, Confirmed did not mark the lead Converted, Lost did not mark it Dead. Owner, 19 Nov 2026: "Yes, build PR 1" (plan: never automatic, one tap).
- **What.** `quotations.lead_id` (nullable, `on delete set null`; one lead per quotation, many quotations per lead). `quotation_link_lead(quotation, lead | null)`: the caller must be allowed to edit the quotation and see the lead (Sales module open and `sales_lead_visible`); null unlinks. `quotation_lead_info(quotation)`: the lead's number, name, stage, holder, follow-up, whether the caller may work it (holder, Admin or Sales `assign`, the rule of `sales_lead_for_work`), when this quotation was last logged on it, the latest sent version and its value, and a suggestion (the lead whose phone equals the quotation's contact phone, by the shared phone rule). It shows nothing about a lead the caller cannot see. The builder gets a slim Lead card under the top row: link or unlink, and ONE button for where the quotation is (Sent or Negotiating: "Log on the lead", a quotation update with a follow-up 3 days on, a Sunday moved to Monday; Confirmed: "Mark lead Converted"; Lost: "Mark lead Dead" with the lead's eleven reasons and the lost note). The buttons call the lead's OWN functions from the browser (`add_sales_lead_comment`, `move_sales_lead`), so the lead's permission rules, checks (Converted still asks for what it needs) and Undo apply unchanged. No lead function, no Orders object and nothing client-facing changed.
- **Proof.** `quotations_pglite.mjs` 104 checks (16 new, with the REAL `sales_lead_visible`, `sales_lead_sees_all` and `normalize_lead_phone` taken from their migrations over a small lead stand-in): linking, the phone suggestion, a lead the caller cannot see (same answer as one that does not exist), no Sales access, read-only people, `assign`, a lead moved to somebody else, a lead nobody holds, "logged" recognition, the latest version and value, the client link never carrying the lead, unlink, a deleted lead, closed to signed-out callers. `leadLink.test.ts` (10) and `leadCard.dom.test.tsx` (15, the real card in jsdom). Both migration ledgers account for the file.
- **Rollout.** Backup first (the migration alters `quotations`), then apply, then merge: the change is additive and the old code ignores the column. Rollback: drop the two functions, the index and the column (header of the migration).
- **Not done.** PR 2 (the lead popup lists its quotations and offers "Make quotation") and PR 3 (Lost uses the lead's reasons). Not seen signed in.
- **Verified live (19 Nov 2026, from outside, no sign-in).** The production domain serves the new code: the Lead card's words ("Log on the lead", "Mark lead Converted", "Mark lead Dead") and both function names are in the JavaScript `boeone.vercel.app` sends. Before the merge the migration was rehearsed on the live schema as a real Admin in a transaction that always aborts, then applied in one transaction and recorded with `migration repair`; afterwards the column exists, 0 quotations are linked, both functions exist, signed-out callers cannot run either and the table is still closed to the browser. `db push` was NOT used: nine older migrations (20270624 to 20270803) are unapplied on purpose and it would have applied them. The card was NOT seen on screen, and no quotation is linked yet.

## 2026-11-19 — Task Management pages start while the access check runs (PR #948, squash-merged by the owner as `3bf9a015` on 19 Nov 2026 at 22:15 IST; deployed 22:17 IST; "Backup on merge" green; verified live) · no migration
- **Why (measured in the owner's Chrome, 19 Nov 2026, single samples, noisy).** A warm click from the launcher never waits on the guard: the permission answer is already in the session, so the page shows at once. A HARD load (new tab, bookmark, the first open of the day) waited for it: on `/tasks/my`, scripts 0.5 to 1.2 s, then the guard's two reads (permissions and profile) 1.16 s in the slowest sample (0.22 to 0.88 s in others), a 0.2 s gap, and only then the page's own reads (0.6 s), rows at about 3.0 s. The page's reads need nothing from the answer: they run as the signed-in person and the database refuses what they may not see.
- **What.** New `ModuleGuardStartsEarly` (`src/components/layout/ModuleGuardStartsEarly.tsx`), ModuleGuard's sibling, used by `src/app/tasks/layout.tsx` and `src/app/dashboard/layout.tsx` ONLY; `ModuleGuard` and the other modules are untouched (pinned: its "children render only when allowed" tests still pass unchanged). Until the context has answered the first time, the page is built hidden and inert next to the spinner (browser only, after hydration, so the server HTML is unchanged); yes -> the same page is shown, not built again; no or no session -> the page is removed and the spinner stays, the same redirects. After the first answer a return to "checking" (View As switching person) unmounts the page as before. The same decision (`canAccessManagementModule` over the display subject). Orders has done this since #897/#898 with its own guard.
- **Held until the page is on screen** (`useModuleShown`, `src/lib/moduleShown.ts`): the once-per-session "app open" ping in `DashboardLayout` (the only write on mount found: a person the guard turns away is never recorded as having opened Task Management), and the New Quotation Request form, whose first field is `autoFocus` (a field focused while hidden stays unfocused).
- **Audit of the Task Management tree** (every effect in the Tasks pages, the Dashboard, the shared shell and the Tasks components, found by a script that extracts effect bodies): 26 effects flagged, one write. The rest are login redirects (same target as the guard), View As exits (they need a non-null profile), `/tasks/quotation-requests` (already waits for `ready`), the new-request init effect (its own reads, a read-only rpc), focus effects that run only when a menu is opened, and no mark-read on open. `useListScrollRestore` needs no change: it restores only after Back inside a running app, whose permission answer is already cached, so a hidden phase cannot coincide.
- **Proof.** New `moduleGuardStartsEarly.dom.test.tsx` (11, real components in jsdom): hidden, inert and next to the spinner while pending; nothing built on the server render; yes keeps the SAME wrapper and page element and builds the page once; no removes the page; a warm yes has no hidden phase; the page is told it is not on screen until shown; the hidden build is for the first load only. New `startDuringCheckTasks.test.ts` (7): only the two Task layouts use the new guard, ModuleGuard is untouched, the same decision function, the ping and the form wait for shown, and an audit that fails if a new effect in the tree writes on mount without being listed with its reason. Mutation checks: removing `inert`, showing the page while pending, or letting a denied person keep the page each fail tests. A first version of those tests took 113 s to fail because comparing two jsdom nodes printed their whole object graphs; the comparisons are booleans now. The guard and navigation pins (`moduleParentGate`, `salesCheckingFrame`, `ordersFinanceUsability`, `comingSoonModules`) pass unchanged.
- **What CI found (19 Nov 2026).** The first push failed one test: `taskDetailOpenFromDrawer.test.ts` pinned that the Tasks layout contains `<ModuleGuard ...>` "because the guard resolves the shared caches before the page mounts". That is the very assumption this change removes, so the pin was stale, not the code: it now pins the new guard and the join that keeps the profile row at one read (`profileReadInFlight`); the page still waits for the profile before drawing gated actions (pinned beside it). It passed locally only because the one-file runs skipped it; the full run is what finds such pins.
- **Live check (19 Nov 2026, new build serving from 22:18 IST, the owner's signed-in Chrome; each page loaded cold in a fresh same-origin document, the browser's own resource timing).** All 15 Task Management pages (`/dashboard`, `/tasks/my`, My Completed, Assigned By Me, its Completed and Cancelled, Cancelled, Quotation Requests and its new form, Team Today, Monthly Review, Create, Create Self, All, and a task page): the page ends up `shown`, not inert, no crash, no spinner left, exactly ONE permissions call and ONE app-open ping per load. On 11 of the 15 the page's first read started before the guard's answer arrived; the other 4 (Quotation Requests and its new form, Team Today, Monthly Review) start their reads from their own first step (`getSession`, or their own permission check) as before: no gain, no harm. THE METRIC: on `/tasks/my`, the list arrived +110, -416 and -272 ms after the guard's answer on three cold loads (the last two before the guard had even answered), against +646 to +825 ms in the three samples before this change, so the page's data arrives about 0.75 to 1.2 s sooner relative to the permission check. Total time from navigation is NOT claimed: those older samples were top-level loads with a slow guard wave and a different cache state. On `/tasks/my` the rows themselves show at about 0.45 s from the saved copy kept on the device, which this change does not touch. The profile row (8 columns, one row) is read ONCE on the task page, My Tasks and the new-request form (an earlier count of 2 to 3 was a loose counter that also matched Team Today's own fuller profile read; the other `users` reads are a directory read and a 4-column position read, as before). The New Quotation Request form's first field is focused (the held autofocus works). A top-level hard load of `/dashboard`: shown, badges 13 / 8 / 3, Top 3 intact, no console message. The console tool does not see inside the test frames, so the 15-page console state is not proven.
- **Not proven, said plainly.** The denied path cannot be tried live (no second account); it is covered by the DOM tests. A phone look is owed. A warm click from the launcher is unchanged by design (it never waited on the guard).
- **Rollout and rollback.** No SQL. Live check after the deploy: a hard load of EVERY Tasks page (`/tasks/my`, `/dashboard`, assigned-by-me, completed, cancelled, the task page, create pages, quotation requests and its new form, team today, monthly review) for no crash, no stuck spinner, the first reads starting with the guard's, the app-open ping once, and on the task page (`/tasks/<id>`) the profile row read ONCE (the page now asks for it before the guard does, and the guard joins that read: `profileReadInFlight`); any failure means revert at once. Rollback is one revert: the two layouts go back to ModuleGuard.

## 2026-11-19 — Task Management sidebar: the three badge counts in ONE call (PR #946, squash-merged by the owner as `616efe75` on 19 Nov 2026 at 20:15 IST; deployed 20:17 IST; "verify" green in 7m28s, "Backup on merge" green) · migration `20270810000000_task_nav_counts.sql` (read-only function; **APPLIED to production 19 Nov 2026 at 20:00 IST**; no backup needed, it changes no table or data)
- **Why.** After #942 the sidebar's three badges (My Tasks, Assigned By Me, Quotation Requests) were still three head-only `tasks` requests fired beside the page's own reads on every Task Management page. On `/tasks/my` that is 3 of the 7 data calls leaving at once; by the page-speed model (about 70 ms per extra simultaneous call) two fewer is roughly 0.1 s sooner, an estimate, not measured.
- **What.** `task_nav_counts(p_user uuid)`: three counts over `public.tasks` with the SAME rules as the three reads (my active: assigned to the user, pending/started/working, not a quotation request; assigned by me: created by the user, assigned to someone else, not completed or cancelled; quotation: assigned to the user, a quotation request, not completed or cancelled). SECURITY INVOKER (row-level security decides, as for the three reads; View As passes the viewed person's id and gets what the administrator may see), `stable`, execute for `authenticated` only, no table, row, policy or grant touched. `src/lib/tasks/navCounts.ts` `fetchNavCounts` tries it once and, on ANY failure or an answer that is not three non-negative numbers, runs the three original reads unchanged; `DashboardLayout` uses it (same query key `['nav-counts', id]`, so every invalidation still works).
- **Rehearsed on the live database** (19 Nov 2026, migration body plus checks in one explicit transaction that ends in a deliberate error, so nothing could commit; the CLI's `db query --file`): the function equalled the three direct counts, run as each person under row-level security, for all 17 people who have tasks (7 with My Tasks, 8 with Assigned By Me, 1 with Quotation Requests, 1 with all three), and for 12 administrator-views-another-person cases; anon is refused (`insufficient_privilege`). Afterwards the function, and the ledger row, were absent. Thin coverage to remember: only one person holds quotation requests.
- **Proof.** New `src/lib/tasks/navCounts.test.ts` (9, with a fake client that records every request): one `rpc` and no table read on success; the exact three filter chains on fallback (an error, an empty answer, a negative, non-numeric or missing number); no request for an absent or non-UUID id; `DashboardLayout` no longer holds the reads; the SQL is invoker, read-only, anon-refused. A deliberate filter typo in the fallback fails 2 of them. The migration is in both ledger lists of `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`.
- **Rollout.** Apply the migration BEFORE merging (`db query --linked --file` from the linked checkout, then `migration repair --status applied`), merge, then count `/tasks/my` live: one `rpc/task_nav_counts` instead of three `tasks?select=id` requests, badges unchanged. Rollback: `drop function public.task_nav_counts(uuid)` (the sidebar falls back by itself) or revert the PR.
- **Applied (19 Nov 2026, 20:00 IST), on the owner's "Check, then apply" after all three checks on #946 were green (verify 7m28s).** The file was unchanged since the rehearsal (one commit, sha256 starts `78b972d8a171a40b`); the live database had neither the function nor the history row beforehand. Applied with `db query --linked --file` from the linked checkout (no error, the self-check blocks passed), then `migration repair --status applied 20270810000000` from this worktree with the link files copied in and removed again. No backup (a function only: no table or data change). Verified on production: SECURITY INVOKER, anon cannot execute it, `authenticated` can, and run as the owner under row-level security it returns 13 / 8 / 3, exactly the sidebar badges. 
- **Live check (19 Nov 2026, new build serving from 20:18 IST, the owner's signed-in Chrome, the browser's own resource timing).** `/tasks/my` hard load: ONE `rpc/task_nav_counts` request and none of the old `tasks?select=id` reads (checked on two loads); the badges read 13 / 8 / 3 (My Tasks, Assigned By Me, Quotation Requests), the tab counts are unchanged (In Progress 13, Today 3, Overdue 9, Future 1, Waiting 0, Awaiting Approval 4; 13 rows), no crash. The request count is the firm result: the first wave went from 12 to 10 data requests. Timings are noisy and NOT a controlled A/B: across the two loads the list took 0.58 s and 0.28 s, the sidebar call 0.59 s and 0.23 s, Top 3 0.56 s and 0.55 s (after #942 alone, one load: list 0.71 s, Top 3 0.73 s, the three count reads 0.3-0.7 s), so the speed effect of this change is below the noise of these samples. NOT measured: a client-side navigation sample (the test tab was throttled in the background) and a phone look.

## 2026-11-19 — Payment window: no already-matched payments offered as duplicates, and a different client name no longer blocks Verify and link (PR #943, squash-merged by the owner as `006ad8fe` on 19 Nov 2026 at 18:44 IST; Vercel Production deploy succeeded at 18:46 IST; "Backup on merge" green; no SQL)
- **Owner report (19 Nov 2026):** on P-AA-0105 (₹2,00,000, request from "Aryan Ji") the window led with "This may already be in Confirmed Payments" showing P-AA-0098 and P-AA-0095 (another client, already on Order 0547 and linked to BS-01326), told the person a merge was impossible, and only far below showed the real match BS-01564 (₹2,00,000, same account, same day, another name). The card also said "Check first: the name does not match" and made Not this one the main button.
- **Duplicates (`lib/finance/paymentDuplicates.ts`):** the look-alikes found by details (verified, same account, exact amount, within 21 days) drop any payment whose linked bank credits already cover it (`bankHasAccountedFor`), unless it holds the credit this request looks like. The links are read with their amount; 30 payments are read, the covered ones dropped, then at most 5 listed. A missing amount never hides a payment. The database merge door is unchanged and still judges every rule.
- **Name (`lib/finance/bankMarks.ts`, `bankMarkUi.tsx`):** `candidateDoubts` no longer counts a name; the comparison says "Different name" and one plain line that it is normal when a company pays. The date (more than 3 days, unless a UTR matches) and the other HDFC account still stop a person. The Bank Statements page keeps its own name check (`matchDoubts`), not changed.
- **Proof:** unit and DOM tests (`paymentDuplicates.test.ts`, `bankDuplicate.dom.test.tsx`, `bankProofBeforeVerify.test.ts`, `bankMarkUi.dom.test.tsx`); each fix was broken on purpose and the new tests failed. `tsc` and eslint clean. Not seen signed in on the live page (the owner's own look at a pending HDFC request is still owed). Rollback: revert the PR (no SQL, no data touched).

## 2026-11-19 — My Tasks opens sooner: the list reads open work only, and Top 3 is one request (PR #942, squash-merged by the owner as `5efcad37` on 19 Nov 2026 at 18:12 IST; "verify" green in 6m32s, "Backup on merge" green) · no migration
- **Owner question (19 Nov 2026):** why did My Tasks take so long to open from the launcher. A DevTools capture of `/tasks/my` showed about ten database calls leaving at the same instant (permissions, profile, unread count, three sidebar badge counts, the task list, the Top 3 pins), each taking about 1 s; the list (78.8 kB on the wire, 366 kB decoded, 1.32 s) is the one the first paint waits for, and Top 3 needed a second call after its first (pins, then tasks). Measured in the owner's signed-in Chrome too: the calls leave within about 20 ms of each other.
- **Changed.** (1) `useMyTasks` no longer reads completed tasks (`.neq('status', 'completed')`): no tab, badge or row on My Tasks draws one, and `/tasks/my/completed` pages the history itself. (2) A stale `?tab=completed` link goes to `/tasks/my/completed` behind the skeleton, never "No Completed tasks". (3) `useTopTasks` is one request, `user_top_tasks` with the task embedded (`tasks!task_id(...)`, same status filters on the embedded task, no `!inner` so every pin still counts); a failed read now rejects instead of being cached as "nothing pinned". Row access is unchanged: the caller's own JWT and the same two policies. The embed shape was checked against the live schema with an anon-key request (HTTP 200, a wrong table name gives PGRST200).
- **Visible side effect.** The "Assigned by" filter on My Tasks is built from the loaded list, so someone whose only tasks for you are finished no longer appears in it.
- **Not changed.** The three sidebar badge counts (`nav-counts`) stay three requests; folding them into one database function is the planned PR B (a function only: no table or data change). The background permission refresh, the profile read and the unread count are shared by every page and untouched.
- **Region note (no code):** a keyed call that reaches Tokyo takes about 190-220 ms warm against 42-45 ms to the Cloudflare edge, so about 150 ms of each call is the edge-to-Tokyo leg. The earlier "Mumbai would save about 50 ms per step" (part 7) was measured on a keyless call the edge answers itself; a Mumbai project is now estimated at 130-150 ms per sequential step. Not tried on a real Mumbai project; Supabase cannot change a project's region in place.
- **Proof.** New `src/lib/tasks/myTasksOpenWorkOnly.test.ts` (9): every tab, badge and Task Type count is identical with and without completed rows; nothing links to `?tab=completed`; the redirect; `shapeTopTasks` keeps every pin and skips a filtered task; the hook makes one request with no `!inner`. 6 of the 9 fail on the old source. Pins updated in `myTasksLoadState.test.ts` and `topTasksApproval.test.ts`. Full local suite 21,848 tests, 0 failures, 9 skipped.
- **Live check (19 Nov 2026, new build serving from 18:15 IST, the owner's signed-in Chrome, the browser's own resource timing).** The list request carries `status=neq.completed`. The owner has **636 completed tasks** (the Completed page: 636, 32 pages), so the old list carried them beside 13 open ones. Top 3 is ONE `user_top_tasks` request on the Dashboard and on My Tasks, with no second `tasks` read after it. The tab counts are unchanged (In Progress 13, Today 3, Overdue 9, Future 1, Waiting 0, Awaiting Approval 4; 13 rows), and the Dashboard still shows the pinned task in slot 1 ("1 of 3 slots active"). `/tasks/my?tab=completed` as a hard load ends on `/tasks/my/completed`, no crash and no "No Completed tasks". Timings, one cold run each and NOT a controlled A/B: the list 0.71 s (hard load) and 0.52 s (client-side from the Dashboard) against 1.1-1.3 s before (the owner's capture 1.32 s; my own run in the same Chrome on the old build 1.09 s, in a throttled background tab); the Top 3 answer 0.73 s, where pins then tasks took about 1.07 s in chain. A repeat inside the 30 s stale window asks for nothing at all. NOT done: pin and unpin (would change the owner's data; that code is untouched), a phone look, the response size (cross-origin timing hides it).

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: Client choices, step 2 of 3 (the client's copy, the PDF page, the Excel sheet) (PR #936, squash-merged by me on the owner's "Merge when green" 18 Nov 2026 21:10 IST as `31e7fbdf`; Vercel deploy succeeded; migration APPLIED and repaired just before the merge, `quotation_client_doc` checked live) · migration 20270809000000 → APPLIED (function replaced only, no backup needed)
- **Owner decisions:** layout 2 (one table, a row per product); print BOTH confirmed and to-confirm rows.
- **Client copy:** `quotation_client_doc` (the client link and every sent version read it) adds `client_choices` to a line that has any: source, name, code, supplier, used_for, photo, status, confirmed_on. The choice's **note** ("anything the factory should know") and the internal note are left out; a line without choices gets no key, so a quotation without choices gets exactly the document it got before. Versions already sent are frozen and unchanged: a choice reaches the client with the next version sent. Function body only: no table or row changes, no backup needed.
- **PDF and client link:** a "Client choices" annexure after the products (and the notes page) and before Terms and acceptance, only when a product has a choice (`choicesDoc.ts`: `choiceRowsOf`, `planChoicePages`; the terms page number and "sign on page NN" follow). Columns Product / Fabric / Finish / Stone / Rope weave; each choice shows its swatch, name, "code · supplier", "For: ...", the source (From our catalog / Client's own) and a chip (Confirmed 18 Nov 2026 / To confirm); "Not used" otherwise; signature lines for the client and for Best of Exports on the last page. Several pages when there are many products; on a phone each product is a block of labelled lines. Checked in headless Edge with 1, 6 and 25 products: no overflow.
- **Excel:** a second sheet "Client choices" with the same table and the swatch pictures, only when choices exist; the first sheet is untouched. Swatches are loaded with the product pictures and counted in the "pictures left out" warning.
- **Pictures:** swatches go through `pictureUrl`, so the quotation's logo switch applies to them like a product picture. The picture-file route (switch OFF) now also accepts a swatch as "a picture this quotation uses", in live lines (`linePictureFilter`) and in sent versions (`PICTURE_HOLDERS`).
- Builder: the panel's footer now says what is printed. Tests: `clientChoicesOutput.test.tsx` (11), the PGlite suite (88 checks: the next version carries the choices, an older version does not, no note, no key without choices, the function stays closed), both migration ledgers.

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: Client choices, step 1 of 3 (the builder records them; nothing client-facing yet) (PR #934, squash-merged on the owner's "Merge when green" 18 Nov 2026 20:22 IST as `5bd9569d`; Vercel deploy succeeded; renumbered from 20270808000000, which the picture wallet had taken; a backup-first export was taken (all 277 tables, 469,653 rows; one storage image timed out, left for the next nightly run) then the migration applied and repaired before the merge) · migration 20270808000100 → APPLIED
- **Owner decisions (18 Nov):** layout 2 (one table, a row per product); everything is picked by the client, either from our catalog or sent by them; no lock after sign-off; no bulk import.
- **What it is:** under each product a collapsible "Client choices" panel (status chip on the row: "N to confirm" or "N confirmed"). Four optional kinds: fabric, finish, stone, rope weave. Each is "From our catalog" (name, code / shade, supplier, where used, note, swatch photo) or "Client's own" (what the client chose, who supplies it, where used, note, photo), with "Confirmed by the client" stamping today's date (IST). A choice never changes a price and is never locked. Swatch photos use the quotation picture upload (`uploadPicture`).
- **Stored ON the line**, not in a table of its own: `quotation_lines.client_choices jsonb not null default '{}'` (one object keyed by kind), checked by `quotation_line_choices_ok` (kinds, source, status, field names, lengths, photo path, date). `quotation_save` deletes and re-inserts every line, so a table keyed by line id would be orphaned and one keyed by position would follow the wrong product after a move; `reference_images` already works this way. `quotation_save` is re-created from 20270806000100 with the new field; a line sent without the key (an old browser tab) keeps what the line at the same position with the same name had.
- **Not exposed:** `quotation_get` returns it (staff only); `quotation_client_doc`, `quotation_public`, frozen versions, the PDF, the client link and the Excel are untouched (they list their fields by name). Steps 2 (annexure page on the PDF and client link, sheet in the Excel) and 3 (PI draft customisation text, Order Fabric and Finish card) are not built.
- **Migration:** adds a column with a default to `quotation_lines` (no existing row changes) and replaces `quotation_save`: per the owner's rule, back up first. Apply before the code that sends the key is merged. Tests: `clientChoices.test.tsx`; PGlite suite 83 checks; both migration ledgers.

## 2026-11-18 — Picture credits: a wallet and a tracking page for every paid picture press (branch `feat/image-credit-wallet`; PR number added at the stamp) · migration `20270808000000_picture_wallet` (three closed tables, four service-role-only functions; NOT applied)

## 2026-11-18 — Picture credits: a wallet and a tracking page for every paid picture press (PR #932, squash-merged as `c7f03dcd` on 18 Nov 2026; Vercel deploy succeeded; "Backup on merge" green; the new routes answer 401 without a session and the page loads; limits are OFF, nothing is blocked) · migration `20270808000000_picture_wallet` (three closed tables, four service-role-only functions; APPLIED 18 Nov 2026 by the owner with `db query --file` then `migration repair --status applied`, verified: tables and 4 functions exist, limits off, ledger empty, browser roles refused)
- **Owner request.** Keep picture spending in check and see it: what is left in fal, spend by day, who uses the most, how many pictures. Measured first, limited later, from real numbers (the owner expects 60 to 80 pictures a day and will adjust).
- **What.** `spendCredits` runs just before the paying call in Studio Image (1 credit), Edit PI Customize image and the quotation Clean up / Change it (2 credits each), and records the press in `image_credit_ledger`. **Limits ship OFF** (`enforce = false`): every press is recorded and nobody is refused except by the pause switch. With limits on: a person needs an allowance, and is refused past their monthly credits, a daily cap or the team pool (India time). Credits return only when fal refused the request before running it. A wallet that cannot be read refuses the press.
- **Tracking page** `/image-editor/usage` (administrators only): fal balance (needs a separate BILLING-preset key, `FAL_BILLING_KEY`, else "Not connected"), spent today and this month in rupees, days of balance left, the last 14 days with the top user each day, spend by person and by tool, the switches, the pool, the daily cap and each person's monthly credits. Staff see a one-line note ("Uses 2 credits · 140 left this month · 30 left today") only once limits are on; credits only, never rupees or model names.
- **Rollout.** Owner applies the migration first (nothing uses it until the code ships), then merge; set `FAL_BILLING_KEY` if the balance is wanted; watch about two weeks; set allowances, then switch limits on. Switching limits on with no allowances blocks everyone.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; the tables stay unused.
- **Not changed.** The Anthropic "Understand" step, the per-minute limiters, the prompts, the models, who may open each screen.
- Proof: `picture_wallet_pglite.mjs` (13 checks on the real migration), `imageCredits.test.ts` (16), the real Studio route behaviour test with the wallet; full suite 21807 pass / 0 fail / 9 skipped; tsc and eslint clean; the first version, `20270807000000`, collided with `quotation_payment_and_desk` already applied on the live database (the CLI matches by version only, so it was skipped and the tables did not exist), hence `20270808000000`; apply it with `db query --file` then `migration repair`, not `db push` (nine older local-only migrations would need `--include-all`); `next build` compiles and prerenders every page when the public Supabase variables are set (a worktree without `.env.local` fails on /my-attendance, which this change does not touch). Not run against the real provider from the app, not applied to the live database, `permissions:check` not run (it needs the live service key; no permission was added or changed).

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: nothing waits for price approval, price suggestions for the pricing desk, bank account + UPI QR on the quotation (PR #933, squash-merged on the owner's "Merge when green" 18 Nov 2026 19:09 IST as `8a0b1f21`; Vercel deploy succeeded; migration APPLIED and repaired before the merge, checked live; the UPI QR was stored by SQL the same evening and the PDF terms page checked in headless Edge) · migration 20270807000000 → APPLIED
- **Owner decisions:** (1) Sales need no approval before the client link; approval stays an optional ask to Nitish and the owner; Confirm → PI is not held either. (2) Price suggestions only for the owner and Nitish for now, with a backend option to widen it once the suggester is trained. (3) One bank account and a UPI QR on the main quotation; the Customer ID is not shown to clients.
- **No gate:** `quotation_price_state` returns `blocked = false` always (the one value `quotation_share`, Confirm → PI and version create read). Limits, the "to ask" state, the ask and the desk queue are unchanged; the card now says it is optional.
- **Suggestions:** `quotation_suggestion_settings.shown_to`: NULL = everyone, an EMPTY array = the pricing desk (administrator, or quotations edit + view all: today the owner and Nitish), otherwise the listed people. The migration sets the row to the pricing desk. Control Center › Sales › Quotation price approval: "Pricing desk only" / "Show to everyone" (administrators only).
- **Payment details:** new one-row table `quotation_payment_details` (closed to direct reads), QR kept in the row as a PNG/JPG data URL (400 KB cap, shrunk to 600 px in the browser). `quotation_payment_details(p_token)` for the print page and the client link (valid token or Quotations access; no Customer ID); `quotation_payment_get/_set` administrators only; edited on the same Control Center page. Printed as a "Pay by bank transfer or UPI" block on the Terms and acceptance page (not frozen into sent versions: it always shows today's account) and below the totals in the Excel. Seed: Best of Exports, HDFC Bank, Jodhpur; the QR is uploaded from Control Center.
- Tests: `paymentAndDesk.test.tsx`; PGlite suite extended (77 checks); two migration ledgers; issuedAt pin. No existing table or data is altered, so no backup is needed first.

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: a styled, editable Excel with a picture beside each product, the standard terms, and one fixed GST line "GST @18%" everywhere (the GST-rate picker is gone) (PR #931, squash-merged on the owner's "merge done" 18 Nov 2026 18:18 IST as `ff8dedc3`) · no SQL
- **Owner request:** the Download Excel was "not professional"; make it appealing with product images, and still usable when a client asks for an Excel to edit. Then, from his own look at samples: nothing frozen, GST only in the totals, plain quantities, Remarks last in its own colour, narrower Name / Material / Remarks; the standard terms in the Excel; GST as "GST @18%" with no CGST/SGST split.
- **New workbook** (`src/lib/quotations/clientWorkbook.ts`, ExcelJS, loaded only when the button is pressed): the logo and a QUOTATION title band; the details (quotation no., prepared for, project, date, valid until, the salesperson's name, role, phone and e-mail as on the PDF); a terracotta header row; room types as heading rows; per product a No., a picture (the one the client sees, so the logo mark follows the quotation's switch), name, size, material, quantity, rate, amount, "Counted in total" and Remarks (last column, its own colour); the totals; transport and notes; then "Terms and conditions" (the owner's sentence "Given prices are ex-factory. GST and Packing will be extra as applicable." first, then every term of `terms.ts`) and the clients' expectations. Landscape A4 fitted to one page wide, no gridlines, nothing frozen, Indian digit grouping, rows as tall as their words need.
- **Editable:** Amount, Subtotal, "Less: Discount", each GST line and the Total are live formulas that repeat the app's rounding (editable: Qty, Rate, the discount %, "Counted in total" Yes/No). GST is not on the product rows. Every formula cell also carries the app's value, so a viewer that does not calculate shows the PDF's figures; Excel recalculates on open. Verified with a formula engine (HyperFormula, one-off, not in the repository): 0 differences over 6 setups after the 18% change.
- **Everything is billed at 18% (owner, 18 Nov 2026: "no need to divide it into GST slabs").** `computeTotals` returns ONE GST row, `round(taxable × 18 / 100)` (`GST_RATE`), whatever a line stored; the "GST rate (all products)" picker and "Mixed rates" are removed from the builder; `toLines` reads every line as 18% (an older 12% or 0% line is saved as 18% on the next save). Shown in the builder's summary, the printed quotation, the client link and the Excel: no split into CGST + SGST or IGST, because a quotation (PI) does not know the delivery state yet. The header's `gst_mode` still goes on the PI draft. A quotation that was in CGST + SGST mode can differ by one rupee in its grand total (one rounding instead of two).
- Same guarantees as before: never the Finish, internal notes, a cost or the client's own phone or e-mail. A picture that cannot be loaded is left out and the builder says how many. The browser half is `clientWorkbookDownload.ts`. The old plain grid (`clientSheet.ts`, xlsx package) is gone. Tests: `clientWorkbook.test.ts` (reads the generated file back); GST pins updated in `math`, `clientDocument`, `quotations` tests.

- **Owner request.** Cut the cost of picture generation (the fal balance had run out; volume is expected to be about 100 pictures a day). `PI_IMAGE_EDIT_MODEL` in `src/lib/orders/customizationImage.ts` is now `fal-ai/nano-banana-2/edit` (was `fal-ai/nano-banana-pro/edit`): about US$0.08 a picture at 1K against US$0.15. - **Rollback.** Put the constant back to `fal-ai/nano-banana-pro/edit`. No data, schema or environment change.

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: the Best of Exports logo mark on every picture the client sees, optional per quotation; clean originals move to a private bucket (PR #924, merged by the owner 18 Nov 2026 16:39 IST as `746cf467`, live 16:41 IST; the 423 existing pictures backfilled 18 Nov about 17:20 IST) · migrations `20270806000000_quotation_picture_originals` (a bucket and two policies, APPLIED 18 Nov) and `20270806000200_quotation_picture_mark_switch` (one new table, two functions)
- **Owner request and choice (review page, 18 Nov 2026):** a black translucent strip THROUGH the product, a little below the middle, with the small white icon-and-name logo repeated across it. Numbers in `src/lib/quotations/pictureMark.ts` (`MARK`: strip 6% of the height with a 24 px floor, centred at 58%, 45% dark, logo 45% of the strip, gap 1.7 logos); the only place to change the look.
- **Burned into the file** (sharp, server side), so saving or screenshotting the picture keeps it. The CLEAN original lives in the new PRIVATE bucket `quotation-originals`; the public `quotation-images` bucket (client link, PDF, screens) holds the MARKED copy at the SAME path, so no stored path changes.
- **New pictures:** `uploadPicture` stores the clean file in the originals bucket, then `POST /api/quotations/picture-mark` (uploader only, create/edit access) writes the marked copy. The image studio (`/api/quotations/picture`) reads the clean original and stores both copies. The PI draft (`toPiDraft.loadPictures`) takes the CLEAN original: the PI is a factory document.
- **Existing pictures:** `scripts/quotations/mark-existing-pictures.ts` (service role, `--env-file`): copies each clean picture to the originals bucket (and to a local safety folder), then marks from the ORIGINAL, so it is safe to re-run. Run once before and once after the code goes live.
- **Optional per quotation (owner, same day):** a "Logo on pictures" switch in the builder's Share card (`ShareCard.tsx`), ON by default, for anyone who may edit the quotation (not an archived one). Stored in the new table `quotation_picture_marks` (no row = on; `quotation_picture_mark_get/_set`), live at once. `GET /api/quotations/picture-file` decides what a viewer gets: ON redirects to the marked public copy; OFF returns the clean original, only for a picture the quotation uses (its lines or a sent version) and only to its own client link (token) or someone who may see it. `pictureSrc` in api.ts builds the address for the builder, the print page and the client link.
- Tests: `pictureMark.test.ts`, `pictureMarkWiring.test.ts`, `pictureMarkSwitch.test.ts`; both migration ledgers.

## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: a size in mm, metres or feet becomes inches, rounded to the nearest 0.5 (PR #909, merged 18 Nov 2026 06:41 IST as `13ae93e7`, live 06:43 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner rule:** a size not in inches or cm is converted to inches by default and rounded to the nearest 0.5 (8.3 → 8.5, 8.2 → 8). ## 2026-11-18 — Quotations: practical fixes from the module review, and a drag on first open draws the crop box (PR #903, merged 18 Nov 2026 02:08 IST as `22f041f1`, live 02:10 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner asks (18 Nov 2026):** "analyze the complete Quotation module for practicality". A read-only review of the module listed 10 problems; the five small ones are fixed here, the rest are in the morning report.

- **Product pictures load as they scroll into view** (`Pic lazy` in `ProductLine`): a big quotation no longer downloads every picture at once.
- **Crop, found live after #901:** on opening Crop & rotate the box is the whole picture and its move layer covered it, so a drag did nothing; now a drag on it draws a new box (crosshair pointer). Test: `cropAndRefine.dom.test.tsx`.

## 2026-11-17 — Page speed: the PI draft page starts reading the PI beside its first group (PR #895, merged 17 Nov 2026 23:59 IST as `e4d923f2`, live 18 Nov 00:02 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process", then step 1 of "start loading during the access check": "Yes, build step 1".
- **Measured live first.** After the Orders guard has the permission answer (~1 s), the PI draft page ran one group (profile and two permission sets from the guard's cache, the salesperson list), and only then `loadDraft()`. `loadDraft` needs only the address and the session (its deps are `[supabase, submissionId]`); the capabilities decide only the controls. Opening a PI draft writes nothing.
- **What.** `loadDraft()` starts right after the session check, beside that group, and is awaited after it. New `capsKnown` (set once the capabilities are applied): the record is not drawn until it is true, so no control appears late; a failed or missing PI still shows its message at once.
- **Proof.** Orders, PI and drafts tests 5,242 pass (two source pins in `draftsAccess.test.ts` updated to the new wording, plus one that the capabilities are applied before the record is awaited); tsc, eslint. The guard is unchanged. Local full suite not run (CI runs it).

Absolute times were lower too (permissions at 0.88 s vs 1.74 s), mostly a warmer connection, not counted.
