## 2026-11-17 — Page speed: putting the saved copy back no longer asks twice (PR #891, merged 17 Nov 2026 21:51 IST as `c5fe668a`, live 21:52 IST) · no SQL
- **Found.** Measured live after #889 on a PI draft page: with the saved copy on, the permissions (`resolve_effective_permissions_for_user`) and the profile were each asked twice (27 requests); with it off, once (25). `invalidateQueries` cancels a read already on its way by default and sends it again.
- **What.** `restorePersistedCache` invalidates with `{ cancelRefetch: false }`: a screen that already started its read keeps it; one that has not still asks at once or on mount, so the Quotations freeze stays fixed.
- **Proof.** `persistedCache.test.ts` 35: a new test (a read on its way when the copy is put back is sent once, and its answer wins) fails without the change; the #889 tests still pass. Related source-pin tests 327, tsc, eslint. Local full suite not run (CI runs it).
- **Live check 17 Nov 2026 21:53-22:00 IST (owner's Chrome, copy on):** a PI draft and an order page each asked once for the permissions and once for the profile; 14 module pages opened (Quotations twice, 0.9 s and 2.2 s), no crash, nothing stuck, no console error; one pair (My Day, Inventory) came back blank (the page request never arrived) and opened fine four times right after.

## 2026-11-17 — Page speed: a put-back saved copy is always asked again (the Quotations freeze), with #886 (PR #889, merged 17 Nov 2026 21:10 IST as `6aa09ddd`, live 21:12 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process" (cause found by reading the code), then "Yes, build it".
- **Cause of the Quotations freeze, found.** A put-back result keeps the time it was fetched. The permission answer is fresh for 30 s (`PERMISSION_STALE_MS`), so a copy saved a few seconds before a page load counted as fresh: React Query did not ask again, `usePermissionContext().ready` (true only for an answer fetched in THIS visit) stayed false, and `ModuleGuard` waited on its Loading screen with no request. Quotations' layout mounts only the session check and ModuleGuard, so nothing else asked; pages with the full shell had other readers that did. The persister assumed a put-back result is always stale; staleTime windows broke that.
- **What.** `restorePersistedCache` invalidates every entry that took the saved data (an open screen asks at once, the rest on mount; a newer answer already in memory is left alone). With it, #886 again: a Set tagged like a Map (My Tasks' pinned ids), `survivesSaving()`, `PERSIST_VERSION` 2 (v1 deleted, never read), and the sign-in change (the copy is put back by the getSession() seed, never inside the auth callback).
- **Proof.** `persistedCache.test.ts` 34: two new tests (a copy saved 5 s earlier is asked again when shown; a screen already open asks again) fail without the invalidation, and a third keeps the #886 freeze as proof (no invalidation = never asked); #886's tests (pinned Set, unsaveable values, v1 deleted) and #880's sign-in guard stay. Related source-pin tests 326, tsc, eslint. Local full suite not run (CI runs it).
- **Live check planned:** EVERY module page, opened one after another within seconds (the case that froze): `/modules`, `/dashboard`, `/tasks/my`, `/sales`, `/sales/board`, `/sales/my-day`, `/quotations`, `/orders/drafts`, `/orders/all`, `/finance`, `/finance/expenses`, `/inventory`, `/vendors`, `/pricing`, `/marketing`, `/seo`, `/samples`, `/exhibition-leads`, `/image-editor`, `/assets-access`, `/showroom-admin`, `/performance`, `/meetings`, `/payroll`, `/attendance`, `/admin/members`, `/admin/control-center/people`; twice; no "ran into a problem", nothing stuck on Loading, no console error. Any failure: revert at once.
- **Live check 17 Nov 2026 21:13-21:30 IST (owner's signed-in Chrome, saved copy on, pages opened one after another within seconds, the case that froze).** Two passes over 27 module pages: every one opened (0.4-2.4 s), none crashed, none stuck on Loading; Quotations 0.87 s and 1.18 s (and on screen, list shown, 3 s after the launcher); no console error. Two loads in pass 2 (SEO, Samples) came back blank (the page request never arrived) and opened fine four times right after; `/tasks` is a 404 with the copy on or off. On/off (2 runs each): launcher 0.40-0.44 s with the copy against 0.62-0.68 s without; PI Drafts rows 0.84 s against 0.86-0.92 s (no real gain now: its capabilities wait for a fresh permission answer, as they must). Not seen on a phone.

- **What happened.** #886 went live 19:10 IST. But `/quotations` stayed on the full-page Loading screen with NO data request for 10 s and more, twice; with the saved copy switched off it loaded. The quotations list itself was not in the saved copy, so something else the copy restores leaves that page waiting. Cause not yet found.

## 2026-11-17 — Page speed: the saved copy comes back in the right shape, then is put back after sign-in again (branch `fix/saved-copy-sets`; PR number added at the stamp) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** After the revert (#883): "Yes, plan it", then "Yes, build it".
- **Cause of the crash, confirmed.** `useTopTasks` (`['top-tasks', uid]`, a saved family) returns `{ pinnedIds: Set, tasks }`; My Tasks calls `pinnedIds.has()`; the saved copy (JSON) brought the Set back as `{}`. The permission context's Map was already tagged; no other Set in the saved families.
- **What.** `src/lib/queryPersist/persistedCache.ts`: a Set travels tagged like a Map and comes back a Set; `survivesSaving()` refuses to save a result holding anything JSON cannot bring back as it was (a Date, a class instance, a function, NaN, an undefined in a list), so a future family with such a value is simply not saved; `PERSIST_VERSION` 2 (key `boe-qcache:v2`), and the version 1 copy (Sets saved as `{}`) is deleted when someone is attached and on every wipe, never read. `src/components/layout/Providers.tsx`: #880's change again (the first SIGNED_IN only adopts the identity; the seed restores after initialisation).
- **Proof.** `persistedCache.test.ts` 31 (3 new fail on the old module: the pinned-ids Set comes back and answers has(), unsaveable values are skipped while the rest are saved, the v1 copy is deleted; #880's guard test back), related source-pin tests 323, tsc, eslint. Local full suite not run (CI runs it).
- **Live check owed after the deploy (owner around):** every restored screen opened twice with the copy on (`/modules`, `/dashboard`, `/tasks/my`, `/sales`, `/sales/board`, `/sales/my-day`, `/quotations`, `/orders/drafts`): no "ran into a problem", nothing stuck on Loading, no console error; then repeated reloads and the on/off timing. Any failure: revert at once.

## 2026-11-17 — REVERT of #880 (the saved copy put back after sign-in): it crashed My Tasks (PR #883, merged 17 Nov 2026 18:27 IST as `08012448`, live 18:30 IST; My Tasks checked live 18:31 IST: loads, no crash) · no SQL
- **What happened.** Found 17 Nov ~18:10 IST while measuring page speed: `/tasks/my` showed "This page ran into a problem" every time, console `TypeError: t?.has is not a function`; with the saved copy switched off (`boe-qcache-off`) the page loaded. Now that #880 put the saved copy back, a value that is a Set in memory came back from the stored JSON as a plain object (`persistedCache.ts` tags a Map for the round trip, not a Set). The live test after #880 covered only `/modules` and `/orders/drafts`, not the task screens.
- **What.** `Providers.tsx` and `persistedCache.test.ts` back to their state before #880 (the first SIGNED_IN attaches without a restore), so the saved copy is again saved but not put back: no crash, and no speed gain. Owner: "Yes, revert".

## 2026-11-17 — Loading screens: lines drawn from a library of 300+ instead of the same eight (PR #882, merged 17 Nov 2026 18:18 IST as `0a7be599`, live 18:20 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "The loading screen shows the same set of texts in a loop ... some fun facts, some trending news in brief, some nice jingles, one liners, motivation quotes, working facts and quotes" for the Gen Z staff. Owner chose live headlines from public RSS feeds, and English with a Gen Z tone.
- **Why.** The BOE One loader (#770) showed 8 fixed lines, one per 4.5 s, always in the same order. Most waits end within ten seconds, so everyone read the same first two sentences every time. The once-a-day quote on the home page was 1 of 50 picked by the day of the year (the same for everyone, back after 50 days).
- **What.** `src/lib/dailySplash/items.ts`: 304 lines in six kinds (Fun fact, Did you know? = trade / wood / India, Work hack, One-liner, Jingle, Motivation with 3 named quotes), each at most 110 characters, original wording or well-established facts. `splash.ts`: `drawLoaderLines` (8 different lines per showing, never the same kind twice in a row, nothing stored) and `chooseTodaysSplash` (the daily splash remembers what this browser has shown, no repeat until all are used, never the same kind two days running). `BoeOneLoader.tsx` is now a client component: the old 8 lines (`CHEER_LINES`) are still what the server HTML and the first render show, and what shows without JavaScript; right after mount they are swapped for a fresh draw (the library, about 13 KB gzipped, is its own chunk loaded by a dynamic import, because this component is in the root layout and a static import would weigh on every page; the small `kinds.ts` is the only static import), and the hidden copy in the root layout draws again each time a slow click reveals it (MutationObserver on `data-boe-nav-pending`). Each drawn line carries a small label. `globals.css`: the line box is `min(44ch, 90vw)` x `6.4em` (was 36ch x 3.1em) so a label and three lines fit; `.boe-loader-kind`, `.boe-loader-by`. `DailyQuoteLoader.tsx`: draws from the same library with the label, stays 2.5 to 4.5 s by length, tap / Escape / Enter / Space skips it, and fetches tomorrow's headline 4 s after mount (at most every 6 h per browser, never before the splash decides). New `GET /api/daily-headlines`: public by design (public headlines only, no input, nothing read from BOE One), five publisher RSS feeds (The Hindu technology, cricket and movies; Gadgets 360; BBC entertainment; a business feed was tried and dropped, it is market ticks), a keyword filter for grim, political, scandal, money-worry and work-unfriendly headlines (tuned on a live read of the feeds), cached at the edge for an hour, each feed read upstream at most hourly, generic user agent.
- **Proof.** New tests: library rules (300+, no repeats, 110 characters, labels), a full lap without a repeat, kind rotation, storage failures, headline storage and expiry, RSS parsing and the filter, the route, and the REAL loader in a simulated browser (fixed lines first, then a draw, a headline appearing with its publisher, the slow-click copy redrawing each time). The existing loader tests still pass. The feeds were read once by hand: 12 clean headlines in 2.2 s.
- **Not verified.** Not seen on a phone or in a browser (the owner tests): the line box is taller, so the logo sits about 20 px higher than before. The keyword filter is not perfect (a banking-delay story passed until two words were added). The publishers' RSS terms were not reviewed. A headline shows the day AFTER it is fetched, by design (the splash never waits on the network).
- **Live check.** After the deploy, `GET /api/daily-headlines` on the live site answered 200 with 12 headlines. No backup was run (no SQL, and the owner rule is none after a merge). The owner's look on a phone is still owed; a headline first shows the day after it is fetched.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR. Nothing is stored server side; the browser keys `boe_daily_splash_v1` and `boe_daily_headlines_v1` are harmless if left.

- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process", then "Saved copy retry" and "Yes, build it", with the owner around for the live test.

Nothing else changes: what may be saved, the 12-hour limit, the wipe on sign-out or another person, display-only use, the kill switch.
- **Proof.** `persistedCache.test.ts`: a new test fails on the old code (no attach inside the callback except the different-person one, which never restores; no setTimeout or hydrate there; the first-event branch touches nothing of the saved copy); related source-pin tests (144) pass; tsc, eslint. Revisit timing (middle value): PI Drafts rows 0.53 s with the copy (19 loads) against 0.88 s without (8); the launcher 0.30 s (12) against 0.76 s (9). ## 2026-11-17 — Page speed: the launcher's For you column asks the database once (PR #875, merged 17 Nov 2026 16:31 IST as `a644cf19`, live 16:33 IST) · migration `20270730000000` APPLIED 17 Nov 2026 ~16:15 IST (adds one function; no table or data change)
- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process", then "For you reads" and "Yes, build it".
- **Measured first.** Production `for_you_item_settings` has one row (machine_stopped = optional, its default) and `user_for_you_preferences` none, so everyone has the defaults; of the six "waiting on you" reads only two ever run (payment requests for verifiers, flagged PIs for Orders users), so batching those alone would save almost nothing. The column's real separate reads on every launcher open: everyone 4 (`for_you_item_settings`, `user_for_you_preferences`, `finance_handover_list`, `finance_unclosed_funds`), Orders users 2 more (`my_returned_pis`, `my_flagged_pis`); five of them again every minute.
- **What.** `for_you_bundle(p_parts, p_choices_user, p_handover_statuses)`, SECURITY INVOKER (every read runs as the caller: the same table rules and each function's own checks), runs the same six reads and returns `{ part: { data } }`; a part that raises is left out. `src/lib/modules/forYouBundle.ts` gathers the hooks' asks for 10 ms and sends one call (same pattern as `batchedGet`); one part alone, the function missing, any failure, or a part left out = that caller's own read, as before. Hooks: `useForYouModes`, `useForYouChoices`, `useOpenHandovers`, `useUnclosedFunds`, `useMyReturnedPis`, the flagged read in `useForYouWork`. The payment-requests read is unchanged. The flagged and returned reads wait for the settings, so on a cold launcher they form a second, smaller call: everyone up to 4 requests to 1, Orders users up to 6 to 2 (counted from the code: a read another screen already made is not asked again).
- **Proof.** `forYouBundle.test.ts` (6: one call, lone part, function missing / failing, part left out, other arguments, the migration's parts and grants), migration ledgers in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`, tsc, eslint, full suite. Rehearsed on production inside BEGIN ... ROLLBACK: for an administrator and two other active people every part equals that person's own separate read, an unknown part is ignored, anon cannot run it, it is not security definer; nothing left behind.
- **Live.** Live recount 17 Nov 16:35 IST (owner's signed-in Chrome, cold `/modules`, administrator with Orders and payment approval): two `for_you_bundle` calls (both 200) and no separate settings, choices, handover, cash, returned or flagged reads, against six before; the payment-requests read is unchanged; no error text in the column. Not seen on a phone.

## 2026-11-17 — Quotations: a PowerPoint's pictures are drawn again when a client file is read (PR #872, merged 17 Nov 2026 15:10 IST as `0b2cc083`, live 15:13 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process", then "Yes, test it": run the one untried door of Start from a client file, a PowerPoint, on the real Brewery deck (`C:\BOE New\Inquiry\659 Brewery\BREWERY FURNITURE SELECTION.pptx`, 18 slides, 31 pictures).
- **Found.** Every slide came out white with only its text: no picture was drawn. A shape's position was read from the first `a:off` / `a:ext` in it, and PowerPoint also writes `<a:extLst><a:ext uri=…>` (no size) in the shape's name, before the real one, so every picture had zero size (and text boxes were squashed to a few letters wide).
- **Fix.** `slideFrame` in `scanPages.ts` reads the position inside `a:xfrm` only. A placeholder with no frame of its own (it follows the slide layout, like the slide titles in this deck) still sits at the top-left; its text still goes to the reader as text.
- **Proof.** The app's own `filePages` / `surveyJpeg` drawing the deck in headless Edge (esbuild bundle, not the signed-in app): before, 10-16 KB white slides; after, all pictures in place. Read with the app's prompts and the owner's spend-capped key (harness): 34 rows from 18 slides, every slide's designs as one row each with the others marked as options, every quantity right ("6 or 12" marked doubtful), 38 pictures of the right pieces; 7 calls, 60k input and 7.5k output tokens (about Rs 18). New `scanPages.test.ts` (jsdom, the deck's XML shape), tsc, eslint.
- **Not seen** signed in, in a browser or on a phone.

## 2026-11-17 — Payroll: a half paid leave takes half off the earliest absent day (PR #869 squash-merged 17 Nov 2026 13:57 IST as `e055668f`, deployed to production 14:00 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** One employee's October 2026 payslip: why is the paid leave not adjusted against the absences, only the late arrivals? His machine file is final (11 days present, 16 absent), so the data was right; the rule was the cause. Plan and defaults approved ("Approve plan").
- **Cause.** 11-15 days present earns 0.5 leave. A whole absent day needed a whole leave (stage 1), so a half leave could never touch an absence and fell to the late-lines stage: three late lines, Rs 407 saved, while all 16 absences were charged Rs 1,385.
- **What.** New stage 2c in `applyLeaveAbsorption`: when no half day takes the 0.5 leave, the EARLIEST absent day is charged half (`roundRupees(per_day_rate x 0.5)`, Rs 692) and the company pays the other half (Rs 693). The day stays absent; its line carries `waived_by = 'paid_leave'` plus a charge (`isPartlyCoveredByPaidLeave`). Payslip row keeps the name Absent with "Half covered by company paid leave"; the popup shows the Rs 693 as a subtraction. Rules text, the How Payroll Works guide and Ask About Your Salary read the new order. BOE Credits are refused on that day (`part_company_paid`). A full leave, a half day, and the no-leave case behave exactly as before.
- **Effect, that employee, October 2026.** Deductions Rs 22,160 -> Rs 21,874; salary after attendance Rs 13,840 -> Rs 14,126 (+Rs 286): 5 Oct Rs 692, late lines Rs 163 + 81 + 163 charged again. Existing payslips change only when the owner presses Generate for the month (restates everyone in the 11-15 band; back up first; a locked month is never touched). Who else is in the band was NOT read from production.
- **Not fixed (owner decides).** A full leave with one half day and no absence; the late-lines stage being all-or-nothing; settings allowing more than 1 leave day while the engine spends one absent day.
- **Proof.** `engine.halfLeaveAbsentDay.test.ts` (15 tests, 8 fail on the old engine, includes that employee's real punches), `attendanceRedemption.test.ts`, tsc, eslint on the changed files; full local `npm test` 21,502 tests, 0 failed, 9 skipped (live-database suites, no database in the worktree); CI 3 of 3 green, `next build` run by CI only. Not seen in a browser or on a payslip in the app.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; the change writes nothing itself. A payslip already regenerated under it is restated by running Generate again.
- **Still open.** Pressing Generate for October (the owner's call; back up first); who else is in the 0.5 band (needs a read-only look at production).

## 2026-11-16 — Pricing: the watcher, a quoted price against the taught one (PR #863 squash-merged 16 Nov 2026 13:30 IST as `ce3bb873`, deployed to production 13:32 IST; migration 20270729000000 rehearsed in a rolled-back transaction, then APPLIED by the owner after a backup, before the merge (confirmed on the live database, recorded)) · migration `20270729000000_pricing_taught_watcher.sql` (alters one CHECK on a table: backup first)
- **Why.** The last piece of the owner's first brief: study the prices quoted, ask the person about a variation, and let the logic improve from the answers. Plan and defaults accepted on 16 Nov.
- **Limit, said to the owner.** Only a line that PICKED a taught product can be compared. Lines without a pick are not watched; old quotations have none.
- **Ask.** On a quotation line with a picked product, when the price typed is more than 5% from the taught price (in the line wood, with what the line leaves out or adds), the line says how far and which way, and offers "Why?": Quantity discount, Client relationship, Size is different, Material or finish is different, The taught price is wrong, Other, plus an optional note. Never forced: the quotation saves without an answer.
- **Where the answer goes.** Into the line tag (`[Taught case #12; why Quantity discount]`), and as a row of the new kind "Differs from taught price" in Pricing → Price learning → Feedback, with taught price, quoted price, reason and a link to the case. The desk marks it Seen, Done or Declined as with other feedback.
- **Learn.** Pricing → Price logic has a new card, "How each product is being quoted": per taught product, how many priced lines picked it, how many are within 5%, lower, higher, the reasons given, and every line with no reason (linked to its quotation).
- **Never automatic.** No taught price is changed by the system; a person changes the case in Training.
- **Safe in either order.** Before the migration the question still shows and the reason is kept on the line; only the inbox row is refused and the Price logic card stays away.
- **Checks.** `supabase/tests/pricing_training_pglite.mjs` 14 of 14; `match.test.ts`, `TaughtPrice.test.tsx`; `tsc` and eslint clean; full `npm test` 0 failures. Not seen in a browser.
## 2026-11-16 — Sales: a salesperson keeps the lead they add; Potential Clients no longer asks for the project value (branch `fix/sales-self-allot-and-potential-value`; PR #861 squash-merged 16 Nov 2026 12:35 IST as `03b1a061`, deployed to production 12:37 IST, new words string-verified live) · migrations `20270728000000` and `20270728000100` APPLIED 16 Nov 2026 by the owner, by hand, before the merge
- **Owner ask (one message).** "When a salesperson adds its own new lead from Lead Master, it should automatically get assigned to him. Currently it is not happening." and "when we try to shift a lead from Under Process to Potential Lead, it asks for an estimate amount, but many times that is not known at this moment. So want to remove this limitation currently from the Potential Lead status." Plan approved with "Yes, build it" (the Salesperson rule below was the one decision).
- **Cause 1.** `create_sales_lead` saved every new lead Not allotted, and only Assign can allot: a salesperson's own lead sat in the queue (owner's screenshot, lead #14875, added while acting as the dummy Test Sales User).
- **Fix 1 (`20270728000000`, one function re-issued, same signature and grants).** Whoever adds a lead keeps it when they work leads: a Salesperson (active, not Admin, Sales View, no View all, no Assign), whatever the Can be given leads tick says (the dummy account is unticked and keeps what it adds); or anybody ticked Can be given leads. A Manager, Lead manager or Admin who is not ticked adds a lead that stays Not allotted, as before. A kept lead is given in the same insert: holder, first call today, history added then allotted; nobody is notified. `AddLeadScreen` says "It is on your list, to call today" for a kept lead.
- **Fix 2 (`20270728000100`, `move_sales_lead` re-issued from `20270711000000`, one line).** The project value is asked from Negotiation on (`v_rank >= 4`), not from Potential Clients. Potential Clients asks for the requirement only; Negotiation and Converted still ask for the value (owner, 3 Nov). The "Needed for Potential Clients" hint now says Negotiation; `checklistFor` follows.
- **No table, column or data change in either migration**, so a same-day verified backup is enough; apply each by hand (`db query --linked --file`, then `migration repair --status applied`): `db push` refuses (it wants `--include-all`) and would also push the never-recorded 20270624-0628 and 20270705. Applying before the merge is safe: the old screen keeps working (its "Allot it later" words are wrong for a salesperson until the merge). Rollback: re-run the `20270524000000` definition of `create_sales_lead` and the `20270711000000` definition of `move_sales_lead`.
- **Left alone.** Leads already added stay Not allotted (lead #14875 is the owner's to allot or close). Website, WhatsApp and the morning run allot by rule 29 as before.
- **Proof.** The Sales full-flow suite in an in-memory Postgres over the real migrations: 160 checks, eight of them new; with the two migrations left out five of those fail, with them all pass. Applied to the real database by hand on 16 Nov 2026 (both files run with `db query --file`, both recorded with `migration repair`; a definition query then showed the new lines in both functions). A rolled-back rehearsal on the real database was refused by the permission classifier and not run. After the deploy the live bundle on boeone.vercel.app has "It is on your list, to call today" and "Needed for Negotiation" and no longer has "Needed for Potential Clients". Not seen signed in as a salesperson, not on a phone.

## 2026-11-16 — Quotations: "This one is different" on a picked taught product (PR #850 squash-merged 16 Nov 2026 10:00 IST as `fab39501`, deployed to production 10:02 IST; migration 20270727000000 APPLIED by the owner before the merge (function confirmed on the live database, recorded)) · migration `20270727000000_pricing_training_library_parts.sql` (one function re-created, no table change: no backup needed)
- **Why.** Step 3 of "the estimator uses submitted training cases" (plan and defaults accepted by the owner on 16 Nov). With one chair taught, a price cannot be built from nothing; the first real "price from elements" is a taught chair less or plus single elements.
- **What.** On a line that has picked a taught product, "This one is different" opens a tick list: untick an element of the case to leave it out, tick an element taught on ANOTHER submitted case to add it. The price moves by that element's amount; the line says "Taught price, adjusted" and what is different. It still never fills a price by itself.
- **Rules (owner defaults).** Anyone who can edit the quotation may adjust. Only material, labour and other elements can be left out or added; the wooden structure, overhead and margin stay as taught. An element's amount cannot be changed on the line: correct the case in Training and resubmit.
- **Who sees what.** The tick list shows amounts only to people who may open Pricing. The amounts of the removable elements do reach every quoting browser (as `parts`), because leaving one out changes the price by exactly that amount anyway; structure, overhead and margin amounts still go to Pricing people only.
- **How it is kept.** In the same tag in the line internal note, in words: `[Taught case #12; without Back weaving; with #4 Brass caps]`. An element the tag names that is no longer taught changes nothing and is named on the line.
- **Safe in either order.** Until the migration is applied, cases arrive without `parts` and the link does not show.
- **Checks.** `supabase/tests/pricing_training_pglite.mjs` 13 of 13; `match.test.ts`, `TaughtPrice.test.tsx`; `tsc` and eslint clean; full `npm test` 0 failures. Not seen in a browser.
## 2026-11-16 — Page speed, part 14: PI Drafts joins the saved copy, and the saved copy stops losing lists (branch `perf/persisted-cache-c`, PR pending) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "PI Drafts only" (phase C scoped down: Orders lists and the dashboard are NOT converted).
- **PI Drafts.** The list now lives in the shared query cache under `['orders', 'pi-drafts-list']` (`skipToken`: it never fetches by itself, `load()` still reads and every delete and retry is unchanged), so a saved copy can be drawn while the fresh read is on its way. The profile and permissions set the capabilities in a `.then` as soon as they are known, so the saved list has its buttons and groups from the first frame; the page still waits three times (pinned by `orderStartupShape`).
- **A flaw in phases A and B, fixed.** The in-memory cache forgets a result five minutes after its last screen is gone, and the saver wrote only what was in memory, so a list the person had left could vanish from the saved copy before the next day. `savePersistedCache` now keeps what this person saved earlier (under 12 hours, same owner, still allowed) unless a newer result for the same key is in memory. A null result is never saved.
- **Not converted.** All Orders (seven loaders, refresh hook) and the Orders dashboard stay as they were.

## 2026-11-16 — Quotations: the client's swatches and drawings go to the PI draft (PR #848, squash-merged 2026-11-16 09:16 IST as `eaa05402` (on the owner's "merge when green"), deployed 09:18 IST (Vercel success on the merge commit)) · no SQL, no backup needed
- **Owner ask.** "Carry the next process": the first follow-up of Start from a client file, owner chose all three kinds (swatch, drawing, reference).
- **What.** Confirm → PI draft and Download in PI format put each line's other pictures (`reference_images`, 20270721000000) in the Customization column (K) of its PI row, beside the main picture in E. The PI reader already takes any picture anchored in K as a customization picture of that row, and Orders already stores and shows them (amber "customization" tag), so nothing changes there. Up to 4 across; more on a second line of a taller row.
- **Size.** The PI upload limit is 10 MB. Extra pictures go on the PI at 800 px (`toJpeg` gained an optional size; default unchanged); main pictures always go; extras are added in product order while all pictures stay under 9 MB (`fitExtras`), and a missing or unreadable extra is left out, never a reason to fail the PI. When any were left out, the builder says how many (and stays on the quotation instead of opening the draft). The quotation keeps the full-size originals; the client's copy still never shows them.
- **Proof.** `piWorkbook.test.ts` (8, through the real PI parser): two products with 2 and 8 extras read as customization pictures of exactly their rows, the main pictures untouched; a missing extra leaves the PI readable; `fitExtras` keeps main pictures, extras in order while they fit, counts the rest. Quotation tests 228, tsc, eslint. Not tried signed in or on a real PI draft.

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing Training: submitting is what counts, and a case can always be changed (branch `feat/training-submit-counts`, PR #843 squash-merged 16 Nov 2026 00:37 IST as `cd8d6358`, deployed 00:39 IST; migration 20270724000000 APPLIED by the owner before the merge (history table, new column and status conversion confirmed)) · migration `20270724000000_pricing_training_submit_counts.sql` (alters tables and data: backup first, apply before the merge)
- **Owner, after pricing the first real case.** He wanted to edit the prices and submit them for the automatic estimator, with editing always available, also after submitting, and the current state always shown. Asked, he chose: **submit is enough** (no approve step) and **he and Nitish may both edit any case**.
- **Rules now.** Two states, draft and submitted. Anyone holding Create on Pricing may change ANY case at ANY time. Saving a change to a submitted case puts it back to "changed, not resubmitted" until it is submitted again, so a half-changed price never counts. Submitting again with nothing changed is refused.
- **What the screen says.** "Price saved, not submitted", "Price submitted on <date> by <name>", "Price changed on <date> by <name>, not resubmitted. Last submitted on <date>", "Price resubmitted on <date> by <name> (submitted N times)". Buttons: Save, and Submit / Resubmit to the price estimator. A History card lists every save and submission with who, when and the total.
- **Removed.** Approve, Send back and Reject; `pricing_training_decide`; the action `approve` on `pricing` (levels are Viewer and Contributor again). Old cases: approved became submitted, sent back and rejected became draft.
- **Database.** `pricing_training_cases`: `submit_count`, `submitted_by`, `updated_by`, status check of two values. New closed table `pricing_training_events`. `pricing_training_save`, `_submit`, `_get`, `_list`, `_access`, `_message` re-created. **Apply before the merge**: the new screens read fields the old functions do not return.
- **Honest limit.** "Submit to the price estimator" records the case as counting. The estimator itself does not read training cases yet; that is the next piece of work.
- **Tests.** `pricing_training_pglite.mjs` (1 new block: conversion of old statuses, change by another person, resubmit, history), `doc.test.ts` (3 new), the action lists, both ledgers.

## 2026-11-15 — Quotations: Start from a client file (scan any requirement file) (PR #835, squash-merged by the owner 2026-11-16 00:04 IST as `17a37990`, deployed 00:06 IST (Vercel success; signed out, POST /api/quotations/scan answers 401 and the old /api/quotations/import 404)) · migration `20270721000000_quotation_line_reference_images.sql` APPLIED by the owner 2026-11-15 about 23:00 IST, after a backup and before the merge (ledger shows it remote; post-check: column 1, check function 1, 0 of 15 lines changed)
- **Owner ask.** The quotation should be made from whatever the client sends (PDF, photos; floor plan, drawings, reference pictures, material specs, quantities), "be prepared for any kind of sheet", so the sales team moves to the app. Plan approved; then the partner's Base44 tool was studied and four ideas were added ("Yes, add all four").
- **What.** "Start from a client file" on the Quotations list (creates the quotation: client, project, delivery state, venue, rooms, products, pictures) and a rebuilt "Import client file" in the builder (also reads the quotation request's attached files in one tap). Several files at once: PDF, JPG / PNG / WebP, PowerPoint (.pptx), Excel / CSV. Pages are turned into pictures ON THE DEVICE (pdf.js, new dependency `pdfjs-dist` 6.4.299, loaded only when a file is read; PowerPoint slides drawn from their pictures and text boxes), so no file-size limit and the file itself never leaves the device. New route `POST /api/quotations/scan`: a survey of every page (kind of page, headings, units, rooms, project) then the pages in batches of three, three batches at a time; a batch cut short or too slow is read page by page. Each product comes with picture boxes; the browser cuts them from the page drawn large.
- **From the Base44 study.** (1) Every picture is typed: the product, a swatch, a drawing, another reference; the extra ones are kept on the line (new column) and shown under the main picture. (2) What the client WANTS is kept apart from what the reference shows: "Client wants: …" in Remarks, and Improve picture opens on "Change it" filled in with it and the client's swatch. (3) Pictures on no product wait as spares; tapping a product's picture chooses one. (4) Their crop details: page drawn large, 2% margin, slivers dropped.
- **Rules.** The client's item code goes in front of the name; a range ("32-49") quotes the larger number and keeps the printed text in the Internal note; options become optional lines; a price printed in the file (a supplier deck) goes to the Internal note only, never the price; no person's name, phone, e-mail or address is read.
- **Removed.** The one-call PDF/photo reader (`/api/quotations/import`, `importPrompt.ts`, `parseBoqReading`): 4 MB cap, no pictures, never run on a real file.
- **Migration.** `quotation_lines.reference_images` (JSON list, at most 8 of {path, kind, note}, checked by `quotation_line_refs_ok`); `quotation_save` stores it and keeps a line's pictures when an old tab saves without the key. Not on the client's copy or any version. Rollback: re-create `quotation_save` from 20270717000000, drop the column and the function.
- **Reader request.** Structured output (`output_config.format` through `src/lib/ai/structuredOutput.ts`, #830), never a forced tool. The read schema has every field required (with optional fields the API refused it as "Schema is too complex" after about 3 minutes); an empty value means "not there". Effort: survey `low`, pages `medium`.
- **Real files (15 Nov 2026, owner's spend-capped test key).** The owner's 9 sample PDFs through the same prompts, schemas and parsing (harness outside the repo, pdf.js in Node): 182 pages, 220 products, 335 pictures, 0 failed pages, 5 to 25 s a batch, about Rs 1 a page. Fixes found there: crop margin from the box (`cutRect`), a list of item codes to the Remarks, a bare serial is not a code, a room printed once carries on (shaded). Babylon's suites appear only as renders with no list, so no rows (correct). Not run: the PowerPoint (harness reads PDFs only) and the screens signed in.
- **Proof.** `scanReading.test.ts`, `importBoq.test.ts`, `structuredOutput.test.ts` (34), `quotations_pglite.mjs` 62/62 (4 new), tsc, eslint (only the 4 old warnings in `documentPlan.test.ts`), the full `npm test`.

## 2026-11-15 — Sales: files on a lead's update, by button, drag and drop and paste (branch `feat/lead-popup-attachments`, PR #839, squash-merged 2026-11-15 23:11 IST as `709a747b`, deployed 23:14 IST, string-verified live) · migration `20270722000000_sales_lead_attachments.sql` (additive; APPLIED by the owner on 15 Nov and recorded in the migration history, before the merge)
- **Owner request** (after the polish): "an option to add single or multiple attachments such as jpg, pdf, png or other standard files ... drag and drop, copy and paste of files". A plan with defaults was approved.
- **What.** The update box has Attach files, takes dropped files and pasted files or screenshots; each uploads at once and shows as a chip; Post waits for the uploads; an update of only files says "Shared N files." In the feed, pictures are small squares and other files are chips under the update's words, opened through links signed for five minutes. 10 MB per file, 10 per update; photos, PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, CSV, text (no zip, no program). Dossier 28, rule 43.
- **Database.** A private bucket `sales-lead-files`; `sales_lead_attachments` (closed table, read through the lead's visibility, immutable); three storage policies keyed to the lead folder; three helpers; `add_sales_lead_comment` re-issued with `p_files` (the four-argument function is dropped, a four-argument call still works); `get_sales_lead` re-issued with `files`. The migration is additive (no existing row, table or policy changes), so no backup is needed first; the nightly backup mirrors the new bucket. Rollback is in the file's header.

The migration was applied inside a transaction on the real database and rolled back: it applied, its self-checks passed, and afterwards there was no table, no bucket, no policy and the four-argument function was intact. Sales tests 372 pass (12 new in files.test.ts, 5 new in boardSpeed.test.ts), tsc 0, eslint on Sales 0; the full npm test ran 21,420 tests and its only two failures were the migration ledgers of participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts, which now list the new file (69 of 69 pass); no next build locally, CI builds it.

- **Order, as it happened.** 1. The owner ran the SQL with `supabase db query --linked --file` from the linked checkout (the file lives only on the branch's folder), 2. recorded it with `supabase migration repair --status applied 20270722000000 --linked` from a folder that holds the file (the first attempt, from the linked checkout, failed: repair needs the file in its own `supabase/migrations`), 3. read-only checks of the real database confirmed the table, the bucket, four policies, three helpers, the five-argument function and the history row, 4. merge, 5. the owner tries it signed in. The code sends `p_files` only when there are files, so a plain update never breaks in between.

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing Training: a failed photo reading says why, and can be tried again (PR #828, squash-merged 15 Nov 2026 22:32 IST as `1d7fcac4`, deployed 22:34 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner report (15 Nov, 21:25 IST).** The first real photo was not read: "Could not read the photo right now". That sentence was the catch-all, so the cause could not be seen, and the only way to retry was to upload again.
- **Fix.** `POST /api/pricing/training/read` now returns the reading service's own answer (status, error type, first 200 characters of its message; never a key) in the sentence shown. A new button on the Photo card, "Read the materials from the photo" / "Read the photo again", reads the saved photo without a new upload. Auto-adding the materials as editable rows was already built and is unchanged: it happens whenever a reading succeeds and no element is listed yet.
- **Cause, found since (PR #830, live 15 Nov 21:46 IST).** Sonnet 5.5 and Opus 5.5 refuse a forced `tool_choice`; the route now asks for structured output (`src/lib/ai/structuredOutput.ts`). This change keeps its value: a future refusal names itself on screen, and a draft whose photo was not read can be read without uploading again.

## 2026-11-15 — AI calls: structured output instead of a forced tool (Meta review, Pricing Training photo, old quotation import; WhatsApp judge's backup) (PR #830, squash-merged by the owner 2026-11-15 21:44 IST as `475569ea`, deployed 21:46 IST (Vercel success on the merge commit; the Pricing Training route answers 401 signed out)) · no SQL, no backup needed
- **Found.** Building "Start from a client file", the first real call to `claude-sonnet-5-5` was refused: `tool_choice: type "tool" and "any" are not supported for this model`. Checked live 15 Nov 2026: Sonnet 5.5 and Opus 5.5 refuse a forced tool with a 400; Haiku 5.5 still accepts it.
- **What was broken.** The Meta weekly review (Opus 5.5 then Sonnet 5.5: both refused), the Pricing Training photo reader (Sonnet 5.5 first: refused; shipped earlier the same evening in #826), and the old Quotations "Import client file" PDF/photo reader (Sonnet 5.5 first: refused; never ran on a real file). The WhatsApp lead judge kept working because it tries Haiku 5.5 first; only its Sonnet backup was refused.
- **What.** All four now ask for structured output (`output_config.format`, a JSON schema) and read the JSON from the answer's text: `src/lib/ai/structuredOutput.ts` (`jsonFormat` closes every object with `additionalProperties: false`, as the API requires; `answerJson` returns nothing for a cut-short, declined or non-JSON answer). The schemas themselves are unchanged (the API accepted each one, checked live). Prompts no longer say "call the tool".
- **Guard.** `structuredOutput.test.ts` fails if any non-test file forces a tool again.
- **Proof.** One real WhatsApp judge call through `judgeChat` per model (Haiku 5.5 and Sonnet 5.5: "lead", 2 to 3 s); every schema accepted by the live API; the touched tests (62), tsc, eslint. The Meta review, Pricing Training and import routes were not called end to end (only their schemas were checked live).
- **Trap for later.** A structured-output schema with many optional fields can be refused as "Schema is too complex" after about 3 minutes of compiling (seen on the new scan schema): make every field required and let an empty value mean "not there".

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing: Training, where a product's price is taught element by element (branch `feat/pricing-training`, PR #826 squash-merged 15 Nov 2026 20:44 IST as `431cd0c2`, deployed 20:46 IST, the page and the photo route verified live without sign-in; migration 20270720000000 APPLIED by the owner before the merge (tables and bucket confirmed)) · migration `20270720000000_pricing_training.sql` (new tables: backup first, apply before the merge)
- **Owner request.** A manual training section, apart from the automatic prices: upload a product photo, the app says what it can see, the person edits, adds or deletes the elements and gives each its cost with the reasoning, then sends it for checking: the owner or Nitish approves it or sends it back. The owner chose to teach inside the app instead of in a sheet.
- **What it is.** Pricing → Training (`/pricing/training`, `/pricing/training/new`, `/pricing/training/[id]`). A case is a photo, a name, a variant, a size, the price on the PI, and free-form elements: element, kind (material, labour, overhead, margin, other), material, quantity × rate or a typed amount, and "how you worked this out". Deliberately no fixed model: the first real cases decide what the price logic looks like.
- **Rules.** Create (teach) and Approve (check) are new actions on `pricing`, off by default, Admin holds both. Only its maker changes a case, and only as a draft or after it is sent back. Anyone holding Approve (the owner as Admin, Nitish when granted) may check ANY submitted case, their own included (owner decision, 15 Nov: "either Nitish or me can approve"), so a case the owner teaches does not wait for Nitish. Sending back or rejecting needs a note. An approved case is locked.
- **Photo reading.** `POST /api/pricing/training/read`: one product photo to the Anthropic API (owner decision D4, 15 Nov), forced schema, access checked in the database before the paid call, nothing stored or logged by the route, never a price. A failed reading never blocks the case.
- **Database.** Three closed tables (`pricing_training_cases`, `_lines`, `_messages`), nine SECURITY DEFINER functions, public bucket `pricing-training` (JPEG, 4 MB, own folder only). Rollback is in the file's header.
- **Not in it.** No effect on any price or quotation, no notification when a case is sent (the list shows "Waiting for you"), no suggested quantities, no Price logic page, no questions on quotations. Those wait for real cases.
- **Tests.** `supabase/tests/pricing_training_pglite.mjs` (8 checks on the real migration), `src/lib/pricing/training/doc.test.ts` (10), the action lists in `comingSoonModules.test.tsx` and `accessCatalog.test.ts`, both migration ledgers.

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing: heights alone are not a footprint, and a labelled size in feet is read (branch `fix/pricing-heights-feet`; PR number and merge SHA are added at the stamp) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "Fix the heights-only and labelled feet faults too", the two faults left open by #811 and #817.
- **Heights only.** In `labelledPlan()` (`src/lib/pricing/features.ts`): when every number in the text is labelled and none is a side ("Seat height 18, arm height 24"), there is no footprint. A marked height beside plain numbers ("24 x 18 x 30h") still leaves the footprint.
- **Labelled feet.** `feetAsInches()` writes feet out as inches ("6 ft" → 72, `6'6"` → 78, `4'-6"` and `7'.6"` too), then the labelled reading runs with the unit fixed to inches (so 360 inches is not taken for millimetres). Feet with no label keep the old plain reading. In feet, a lone length never names the other numbers as breadth and height (`7'.6" Length Should Be divided -3'+4'.6"` stays without a footprint).
- **Measured on the real PI lines** (the same 3,876 priced rupee lines as #817): 16 lines read differently. 15 gain a correct footprint (all feet: `7'L*4'W*3'H`, `L-6'-10" D -2'-6"`, `3'-6" dia`, and the like); 1 loses a wrong one ("length of side 18inch height 30inch" was 18 × 30). Footprints found 1,599 to 1,613; the fit's overall error 53.7% to 53.6% (Coffee table 41.0% to 40.5%, Lounge / arm chair 30.8% to 30.4%, Table 63.0% to 62.8%, every other type unchanged). A first, wider version of the heights rule lost 5 right footprints on these lines and was narrowed before this entry.
- **Found, not fixed.** A unit word between a number and its label is not skipped for millimetres ("600 MM Dia Height - 750 MM" is read 600 × 750, not as a diameter), and "24 x 24 inch height 30 inch" takes the second 24 for a height and so reads 24 × 30. One real line each.
- **Proof.** `planSize.test.ts` +3 tests. Also stamps #817 as merged in this file.

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing: a unit glued to its number ("180cm", "250mm") is read as the unit, not as inches (PR #817, squash-merged 16:15 IST as `82a446dc`, production deploy succeeded 16:17 IST; not seen signed in) · no SQL
- **Found** while re-reading the size reading after #811: the unit check needed a space, so "L 180cm W 90cm" gave 16,200 square inches instead of 2,511. The old code and #811 had the same fault. Owner: "Yes, fix units".
- **Changed.** `MM` / `CM` in `src/lib/pricing/features.ts`, used by `dimensionsInches()` and `footprint()`: a digit before "mm" / "cm" counts like a space. A word that ends in "mm" is still not a unit.
- **Measured on the real PI files** (`C:\BOE New\Inquiry`: 255 PI-named workbooks, 3,876 priced rupee lines after copies, 3,177 of a product type; a smaller set than the 4,874 lines of 14 Nov because the files were chosen again, so the figures are not comparable with that entry). 158 sizes have a glued "mm" or "cm", but 156 are millimetres already caught by the "over 300 can only be millimetres" rule or by a spaced unit elsewhere in the text. **Only 2 lines read differently** (both "N X Ncm"). Footprints found stay 1,599; the fit's overall error moves from 53.8% to 53.7% (Coffee table 42.5% to 41.0%, every other type unchanged). It is a correctness fix, not an accuracy gain.
- **Known limit.** A size in inches with a thickness in glued millimetres ("72 x 36, 18mm top") would now be read wholly as millimetres. No real line is written that way (0 of 3,876).
- **Not fixed.** Heights alone ("Seat height 18, arm height 24") still give a footprint; labelled feet ("Length 6 ft Width 3 ft") give none.
- **Proof.** `planSize.test.ts` +1 test (8 cases). Also stamps #811 as merged in this file, dossier 24 and the module table.

## 2026-11-15 — Pricing: sizes read by their labels, and the reason for an unseen finish worded right (PR #811, squash-merged 15:12 IST as `cebad468`, production deploy succeeded 15:15 IST; not seen signed in) · no SQL
- **Found by the owner's first real blind test** (10 PIs, 52 lines, average miss 24.3%): the misses showed sizes written as "Length - 72 Breadth - 24 Height - 30" read as 72 × 30 (the two largest numbers), a sofa's "Seating Height - 18" read as its depth, typos ("Lentdh", "Diamension") unread, and "Powder finish not seen before: priced as Acacia" naming a material for a finish.
- **Changed.** `planAreaOf()` in `src/lib/pricing/features.ts` replaces `planArea(dimensionsInches(...))` for every line the model reads: labelled sides (length, breadth / width / depth, diameter; typos listed in `ROLE`) give length × breadth, a seat height or a lone length gives NO footprint, a number marked as the height ("30(H)", "24 x 18 x 30h") is dropped, an "L" written first ("L 72*24*30") names L x B x H, millimetres and centimetres are converted. Plain numbers keep the old rule (the two largest). `predict()` words each unseen kind on its own ("Powder finish not seen before: priced as Natural").
- **Measured on all of the owner's old PI lines** (4,874 in rupees, 17 product types, run on the files in `C:\BOE New\Inquiry`): a footprint is found on 2,072 of 3,998 lines of those types (1,771 before) and the in-sample typical error of the fit falls from 44.1% to 42.5% (Table 53.7% to 50.0%, Bedside 12.6% to 11.7%, Bar stool 32.7% to 28.7%, Sofa 54.1% to 52.3%; Wardrobe 26.4% to 29.1% and Mirror 13.5% to 16.3% a little worse). Reading plain numbers as the template's "W x D x H" (first two) was tried and fitted no better (44.5%, Desk 26.2% to 37.6%), so it was not kept.
- **What it does NOT fix.** The fit's typical error is still about 42% overall (Table 50%, Sofa 52%, Chair 36%): PIs name many different products just "Table" or "Chair" (1,438 and 1,254 lines). That is piece 2 (the design as a parameter), not size reading.
- **Proof.** `planSize.test.ts` (labelled, typo, seat height, diameter, mm / cm, plain, marked height, feet, a lone "L", a word that only looks like a label, the finish wording), the existing pricing tests unchanged. Already-learned batches need nothing: sizes are stored as written and re-read every time the model is fitted.
- **Brought up to date 2026-11-15** (decision D10 of the Price Brain plan: finish this before that work, which builds on the same files). The branch was cut from `ae4040b9` and moved onto `origin/main` `4e866120`, 46 commits later (three times: `main` took #807, #808 and #809 while it was being checked). `src/lib/pricing` had not changed on `main` in that time (last touched by #760), so the code applied with no conflict. The #805 suggested-price block and every other screen that shows the model's reasons print them as plain strings, and nothing pins the old "priced as ..." wording (`model.test.ts` matches only "Walnut material not seen before"). The 14 Nov measurements above were not re-run: the code that was measured is unchanged.

## 2026-11-14 — Quotations: three layout fixes in the premium document (PR #787 squash-merged 2026-11-15 09:25 IST as `80091824`, deployed 09:28 IST, verified live) · no SQL
- **Found by** rendering a sample of the live PDF (15 pages) for the owner; fixed after measuring the real render in headless Edge.
- **Half-empty product page.** The planner treated a product name over 27 characters as two lines; a mixed-case name of up to 31 characters prints on one (a card is 41 units wide, the name 2.85 units; capitals are about 30% wider). `nameLines()`: 30 characters per line, capitals count 1.3, never fewer lines than the browser drew over 43 measured names. The sample's Deluxe Room (8 products) packs 4 + 4, was 2 / 4 / 2.
- **No picture, no frame.** A product with no picture prints its text only; `cardUnits` leaves the picture out (31.7 units, measured 46.2 vs 14.5), so text-only cards pack more rows per page. Checked at 390 and 320 px.
- **Public areas are not rooms.** `roomsOf` counts only room types whose unit is a room word (rooms, suites, villas, ...); "Lobby and Restaurant" on the unit "area" no longer makes "Forty-seven rooms" of 46 or moves the lead-time band at 30 / 31 and 100 / 101. When no room type uses a room word, all count, as before. A lobby left on the unit "rooms" still counts (an explicit "counts as rooms" tick needs a column: the owner's call). Weeks already stored in a version stay; the headline count is recomputed from the stored room types.
- **Proof.** `documentLayout.test.ts` 14, three new document tests; sample 15 pages -> 14, "Forty-seven" -> "Forty-six rooms", no product page overflows (95 to 102 of 109.2 units). Not seen signed in or with real pictures.

## 2026-11-15 — Page speed, part 12: a saved copy of the launcher, profile and task lists, so a revisit paints at once (branch `perf/persisted-cache-a`, PR pending) · no SQL
- **Owner decisions (15 Nov).** "Go with option 1" (a persisted cache) and, asked which data may be kept on a staff PC or phone, ALL FOUR areas: the launcher and own profile, task lists, Orders and PI lists, Sales leads and quotations; the rules offered and accepted: Finance, Payroll, Attendance and salary data never; each person's copy kept separate; wiped on sign-out or when somebody else signs in; expires after 12 hours. THIS PR is phase A only: the launcher, the profile and the person's own open task list. Phase B (Sales leads and quotations) and phase C (Orders and PI lists, which load in effects and must first be converted to the shared query cache) are NOT built.
- **Built.** `src/lib/queryPersist/persistedCache.ts` (core `dehydrate`/`hydrate`, no new dependency; localStorage key `boe-qcache:v1`; a Map round-trips): an ALLOWLIST of query families (`permission-context`, `profile`, `app-modules-visibility`, `module-order`, `task-order`, `top-tasks`, and `tasks` only as `[tasks, assigned-to, <id>]`), saved only when the key carries the owner's id, plus a second wall that refuses any key mentioning finance, payroll, attendance, salary, payment, credit, punch, bank, expense, reimbursement, payslip or wage. Only successful results under 12 hours old are written (biggest dropped first above 1.5 MB), throttled to one write per 2 s and only when a saveable result changed. Restore happens once, after the stored session says who is signed in (`AuthIdentityBoundary` in `Providers.tsx`): it is restored only if the copy's owner is that person, otherwise wiped; a copy that is corrupt, over 12 hours old, from another build or from another person is wiped. Sign-out and a different person wipe it. Kill switch: set `localStorage['boe-qcache-off'] = '1'` (no deploy).
- **Display only, never a decision.** A restored result keeps the time it was fetched, so it is older than this page session. `usePermissionContext` now has `ready` (an answer fetched in THIS session; every guard keeps reading it, so a saved copy cannot open a module or admit anybody) and `displayReady` (any answer; used only by the launcher to draw its cards). While View As is previewing somebody, nothing is painted from a saved copy. The launcher redirects to /login, fetches counts and reads For you only on `ready`; the card list is drawn on `displayReady`. A card the person has since lost disappears when the fresh answer arrives, and the module's own guard re-checks access on opening.
- **What it helps.** The launcher on a revisit (from two waits to none before the cards show), and the first paint of the task lists once their guard has passed (one wait saved). Guarded pages still wait one fresh permission answer, by design. NOT YET MEASURED live: a revisit's first paint, before and after.
- **Not done (phases B and C).** Sales leads and quotations; Orders, PI Drafts and Orders dashboard lists (they fetch in effects, so they must first be moved to the shared query cache); other task lists (assigned-by-me, completed archive).
- **Proof.** New `src/lib/queryPersist/persistedCache.test.ts` (19: the allowlist and the forbidden words, owner-only, a Map and the original timestamp survive, only saveable successful results are written, 12-hour limit, another person's copy wiped, corrupt or tampered copy refused, oversize and full-disk safe, kill switch, no storage, throttled saving, no save for unsaveable changes, `ready` refuses a saved copy, the launcher's gates, the identity wiring). Full suite, `tsc --noEmit`, eslint and `next build`: see the PR.
- **Rollback.** Set the kill switch on a device, or revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

## 2026-11-15 — Page speed, part 11: the Payment Requests badges in one call (PR #807, squash-merged 2026-11-15 13:47 IST as `bb407054`, production deploy success 13:49 IST) · migration 20270719000000 APPLIED 15 Nov ~13:46 IST
- **Owner ask (15 Nov).** "Fold the Finance counter reads", then "Go with option 1" (a read-only database function rather than counting rows in the browser).
- **Built.** Migration `20270719000000_finance_payment_request_tab_counts.sql`: `finance_payment_request_tab_counts(timestamptz)`, SECURITY INVOKER (row-level security decides the rows, as for the four requests it replaces), STABLE, read-only, granted to `authenticated` only, with a self-check. It returns the four badges (pending, clarification, rejected, archive) under the SAME rules as `paymentRequestsQuery.ts`: scope = the three request-stage statuses; archive = `coalesce(rejected_at, updated_at) <= cutoff`; rejected = its complement. The Payment Requests page (`src/app/finance/page.tsx`) calls it when no search is typed; with a search, or on ANY failure (a database without the function included), it keeps the four head-only requests.
- **Effect (counted, not timed).** Four badge requests become one on the Payment Requests page when no search is typed.
- **Apply.** Read-only function: no table or data change, so by the owner's rule no backup beyond a verified same-day one. The page works before and after the apply (it falls back), so merge order is safe; apply, then merge, then check.
- **Applied (15 Nov).** Verified backup first (13:44 IST: 263 tables, 466,913 rows, 0 errors); applied with `supabase db query --linked -f`, then `supabase migration repair --status applied 20270719000000` (recorded locally and remotely). Checked: the function exists, SECURITY INVOKER, `authenticated` may execute it, `anon` may not.
- **Verified live (13:52 IST, owner`s signed-in Chrome, resource timing, `/finance`):** one `rpc/finance_payment_request_tab_counts` replaced the four badge requests; the page made 10 requests (15 in the first trace). The badges read 0 pending and the page`s own list agrees ("No pending payment requests"); the 5 pending rows a database-administrator count sees are hidden from the owner by row-level security, as before.
- **Proof.** New `src/app/finance/tabCountsOneCall.test.ts` (the rule matches the page's own `tabMatches` row by row, including the archive boundary; invoker, read-only, anon refused; the page falls back). Full suite and build: see the PR.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; `drop function public.finance_payment_request_tab_counts(timestamptz)`; no data changed.

## 2026-11-15 — Page speed, part 8: the launcher's attendance answers join the one request (PR #796, squash-merged 2026-11-15 09:48 IST as `27deb4f7`, production deploy success 09:50 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "Yes, do it" to batching the launcher's punch, machine-status and attendance-request calls.
- **What it was.** The punch-in strip (`/api/attendance/my-punch`, everybody) and, for an administrator only, the machine's health (`/api/attendance/machine-status`) and the pending requests (`/api/attendance-requests?scope=queue&status=pending`) were three more server functions on the launcher. All three sign the caller in from the bearer token.
- **Built.** They are three more parts (`punch`, `machine`, `queue`) of part 6's `/api/day-close/launcher`. Each part calls its route's UNCHANGED `GET` handler with the caller's own headers and the address it always had, so sign-in and the administrator-only refusals are the route's own. `batchedGet` knows the three addresses exactly (only the pending queue is batched; the Requests screen's other statuses are not). `usePunchStrip`, `useMachineHealth` and `usePendingApprovalQueue` ask through it. A lone caller still goes to its own route, and ANY batch failure falls back to the routes, which stay.
- **Effect (counted, not timed).** An administrator's cold launcher: server calls 5 -> 3 (counts, and one call for Day Close, Scoreboard, punch, machine and queue). Anybody else's: the punch strip rides with the Day Close parts, 3 -> 2. **Verified live (09:52 IST, administrator, cold `/modules`):** 17 requests, 3 server calls: `/api/notifications/counts`, `/api/day-close/launcher?parts=punch,prompt,me,vote,starSeller` and a SECOND `/api/day-close/launcher?parts=queue,machine` (the administrator-only hooks start a moment later, after the profile, so they miss the 10 ms window and form their own batch). Still 5 -> 3; one request for all seven would need those hooks to start earlier. No ms claimed.
- **Proof.** New `src/components/dayClose/batchedGetAttendance.test.ts` (7: the exact addresses, the administrator's seven answers in one request, staff's five, a lone punch goes direct, an administrator-only refusal keeps its status, the fallbacks keep the queue's query) and 4 more in `launcherBatch.test.ts` (the parts, the three unchanged handlers with the caller's headers, the routes still exist, the hooks ask through the batcher). Full suite 21,258 tests, 0 failures, 9 skipped; `tsc --noEmit`, eslint and `next build` clean.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

## 2026-11-14 — Page speed, part 7: the launcher's badge counts in one call (PR #789, squash-merged 2026-11-14 22:41 IST as `1e80e23c`, production deploy success 22:46 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "Yes, do it" to batching the three notification-count calls the live recount found on a cold Modules page.
- **What the live recount showed.** A cold launcher asked for its unread badges one server function at a time: Task, Finance and Orders from `/api/notifications?count=1&category=...` (through `useUnreadCountState`) and Sample Tracking from `/api/samples/notifications?count=1` (the launcher's own effect): four function calls from India to Tokyo, each signing the caller in, for four small numbers. A warm launcher shows none of them (the persisted counts are fresh for 30 s), so this is the first visit of the day and every return after about a minute.
- **Built.** (1) `GET /api/notifications/counts?parts=task,finance,order,sample[&subjectUserId=...]` calls the UNCHANGED `GET` handlers of the two count routes, in parallel inside one function call, with the caller's own cookies and exactly the query string each caller used to send, and returns each answer with the status and body its own request would have had (`src/lib/notifications/countBatch.ts`, the route imports both handlers). Who may read which count, the view-as rule (the `subjectUserId` goes to the categories only) and the hidden-rows rule are the handlers' own; a part that throws becomes a 500 for that part alone. (2) `batchedCountGet` (`src/lib/notifications/batchedCounts.ts`, the sibling of part 6's `batchedGet`): counts that ask in the same 10 ms share one request, grouped by preview subject; ONE count alone is fetched at its own URL exactly as before; if the batch fails for ANY reason (network, error status, an answer that does not name the part) every caller is answered by its own route; only the two count routes with exactly their known parameters are batched. The hook and the launcher keep their own URLs (the old source pins still match); only `fetch(url)` became `batchedCountGet(url)`.
- **Effect (counted, not timed).** On a cold launcher: function calls 8 -> 5 and browser requests 22 -> 19 (four count requests become one). The batch still does the per-part sign-in and count reads inside the function, next to the database, but the three extra trips from India and their cold starts are gone. **Verified live after the deploy (about 22:50 IST, owner's signed-in Chrome, resource timing, a COLD load of `/modules` after 40 s idle so the stored counts were stale):** ONE `/api/notifications/counts?parts=task,finance,order,sample` request replaced the four count requests; the launcher made 18 requests and 5 server-function calls (counts, my-punch, day-close/launcher, attendance-requests, machine-status), against 22 and 8 on the previous cold load. A direct call to the batch route answered 200 for all four parts, each with `unreadCount`. A WARM load (counts under 30 s old) asks for no category count at all, and the launcher's lone sample count goes to its own URL, as designed. Times in that window are not reliable, so no speed-up in milliseconds is claimed.
- **Not changed.** No badge, card or number; no SQL; the two count routes and every other use of them. NOT done: the other notification categories' pages (a module shell asks for its own category once, so there is nothing to share), `my-punch`, `machine-status`, `attendance-requests`, the For you reads.
- **Proof.** 23 new tests (the browser batcher: four counts make one request and each gets its own status and body, one count alone goes to its own URL, the preview subject travels and a different subject is its own group, every failure ends with each caller answered by its own route; the server batch: the exact legacy paths, each part unchanged, parallel, one failing part isolated and its database message kept in the log, the route runs the two unchanged handlers and only reads); two older pins now read the batcher (the hook still throws on a non-2xx so a failed count keeps the last badge; the launcher still has exactly one place that asks for a count and no bare fetch). Full suite, `tsc --noEmit`, eslint and `next build` clean (see the PR).
- **After the merge.** Recount a COLD launcher on production (clear the stored counts or wait a minute): expect one `/api/notifications/counts` instead of four count requests.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

## 2026-11-14 — Page speed, part 6: the Modules launcher asks for less (PR #785, squash-merged 2026-11-14 21:37 IST as `e4ba4410`, production deploy success 21:39 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "Start Modules launcher": the busiest page, 25 requests in the admin's waterfall (7 in the first step, about 17 in the second), nine of them server functions.
- **What the code showed.** The launcher made nine calls through Vercel functions on every visit (notifications, samples notifications, my punch, machine status, attendance requests, and four more for Day Close and the Scoreboard), each checking the caller and reading the database. The four Day Close / Scoreboard calls (`/api/day-close/prompt`, `/api/scoreboard/me`, `/vote`, `/star-seller`) are paused in production (`day_close_settings.team_live` is false and has never been on), so each answered "nothing" after its own caller check and settings read: four function calls and about a dozen database reads for nothing, and in the earlier waterfall the slowest requests on the page (0.5-1.3 s each, in a slow window). The page also read the signed-in row twice for different columns: `payroll_active, is_deleted` (the attendance request button) in the first step and `position, designation_level` (the account menu) in the second.
- **Built.** (1) `GET /api/day-close/launcher?parts=prompt,me,vote,starSeller` runs the SAME four loaders (`loadMyPrompt`, `loadMyScore`, `loadVote`, `loadStarSeller`) for the same caller after ONE caller check, in parallel, and returns each answer with the status and body its own route would send; a part that throws becomes that route's own 500 and does not take the others with it. The four routes stay. (2) `src/components/dayClose/batchedGet.ts`: callers that ask in the same 10 ms (the strip, the score card, the vote card, the Star Seller card, through `dayCloseFetch` and `useDayPrompt`) share one request; a single caller goes to its own route; if the batch fails for any reason (network, error status, an answer that does not name the part) every caller is answered by its own route, as before. POSTs are never batched. (3) `useOwnRowExtras` (`src/hooks/queries/useOwnRowExtras.ts`): ONE primary-key read of `payroll_active, is_deleted, position, designation_level` shared by the launcher's attendance rule and the account menu, each with its own freshness (30 s and 5 minutes). It is a separate cache entry; the shared permission profile still does not carry designation_level.
- **Effect (counted, not timed).** Function calls on a launcher visit 9 -> 6; the Day Close / Scoreboard group, 4 requests, 4 caller checks, 4 settings reads -> 1, 1, 1 (while paused the whole group costs one function call and about three reads); users reads of the signed-in row 2 -> 1. Browser requests on the admin's launcher 24 (after part 5) -> about 20. By the 22 Oct model (a request beside n-1 others finishes at about 230 + 70 x (n-1) ms) the second step is shorter by about 0.2-0.3 s, and the database serves about 10 fewer reads per visit; **Verified live after the deploy (about 21:45 IST, owner's signed-in Chrome, resource timing, cold load of `/modules`):** the four separate Day Close / Scoreboard calls are gone and `/api/day-close/launcher` appears once; the two 2-column `users` reads are one 4-column read (`users[4c]`); `/tasks/my` shows the same single `users[4c]`. The launcher made 22 requests, 8 of them server functions; 3 of those are `/api/notifications` (one per category: task, finance, order, all fired on a COLD start; not changed by this PR, and the earlier warm-start trace showed one), so like for like it is 20 requests and 6 function calls, against 25 and 9 in the first waterfall. Times in that window are not reliable, so no speed-up in milliseconds is claimed. NEW FOLLOW-UP: the three `/api/notifications` calls on a cold launcher are three function calls for three badge counts.
- **Not changed.** No card, strip or panel; no SQL; nothing about who sees what: the loaders are the routes' own. NOT done, on purpose: `/api/attendance/my-punch`, `machine-status` and `attendance-requests` (three more function calls), the two notification badge calls, the For you panel's six reads (cash journeys, flagged and returned PIs, payment requests, two settings tables) and `my_announcements`: each is its own follow-up and the For you reads would need a database function.
- **Proof.** 26 new tests: the batcher (four callers make one request and each gets its own status and body; one route alone goes direct; the same route twice is fetched once; every failure ends with each caller answered by its own route; no session sends nothing), the server batch (each part unchanged, only the parts asked for, parallel, one failing part isolated and its database message kept out of the answer, the route uses the same four loaders as the four routes), and the shared row (the four columns are selectable, the shared profile still lacks them, the page and the menu have no users read of their own). Full suite 21,191 tests, 0 failures, 9 skipped; `tsc --noEmit`, eslint and `next build` clean (see the PR).
- **After the merge.** Recount the launcher's requests on production; the batch route is public API surface behind the same caller check as the four it replaces.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.


## 2026-11-14 — Page speed, part 5: fewer requests at the start of every page (PR #782, squash-merged 2026-11-14 20:54 IST as `4c215ba9`, production deploy success 20:56 IST) · part 3 dropped, measured · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "Start part 3", then "Fold start-up reads". Part 3 (`fetchAllRows` loading its pages in parallel) was DROPPED after counting the real rows: the busiest person's long reads return 46 to 728 rows (team-response tasks 46, completed tasks 639-728, quotation requests 167, attendance in the busiest month 239, Meta insights 148 in 90 days), so every fetch-all read is one request today; only bank lines (1,079) need two, and parallel waves cannot help a second page that is only discovered after the first. The audit's "fetch-all loops in 36 pages" is a pointer, not a cost.
- **What the real waterfall showed** (the browser's own resource timing, five pages, in the owner's signed-in Chrome; the window was slow, so the order and the counts are reliable and the times are not). Every module page started with three requests (permissions RPC, the profile row, and a separate `users` read of `role` made by the floating Login-as-member widget), then a second step with a 2-column `users` read (the sidebar's position), `/api/notifications` and the page's own reads. The Orders pages read the caller's profile and permissions a second time beside their own data, although the guard above them had just resolved both: PI Drafts 5 requests in its second step, the PI draft and the Confirmed Order 4 and 5.
- **The model that makes this worth doing.** From the 22 Oct measurements: a database call costs about 230-270 ms, of which the network to Tokyo is only about 45-65 ms (measured again today: TCP connect 40-65 ms, a warm call 60-70 ms), the rest is the Supabase side on a Micro-sized database; a request that waits beside n-1 others finishes at about 230 + 70 x (n-1) ms. So fewer concurrent requests finish a step sooner, and moving the database to Mumbai would save about 50 ms per step, not the 250 ms assumed when the move was first listed (part 7). The app already preconnects to the data origin (`src/app/layout.tsx`).
- **Built.** (1) The Login-as-member widget takes the admin flag from the shared profile (`callerProfile`) instead of reading `users.role` itself: one request fewer in the first step of EVERY page. (2) The permission context waits for a profile read that is already running (`profileReadInFlight`) instead of starting a second one, so the widget and the guard share one read whichever starts first. (3) `callerProfile` and `callerModulePermissions` (`src/hooks/queries/usePermissionContext.ts`): the guard's answer when it is under 30 s old, the database otherwise (the rule `cachedModulePermissions` already used). (4) PI Drafts, the PI draft record and the Confirmed Order read their profile and permissions through them: PI Drafts 4 requests -> 2, PI draft 31 -> 28 and Confirmed Order 48 -> 45 call sites (`orderStartupShape.test.ts` counts them). The three pages use only id, full name, role and team of the profile, which the shared row has.
- **Not changed.** No screen, no feature. The permission rule is the same resolver; a role change still reaches an open page within 30 s; a failed permission read still gives no capability; the salespeople list on the PI draft stays inside the group the page waits for, because the "Saved salesperson (not in the list)" text is drawn from it. NOT done, on purpose: the sidebar's position/designation read stays its own request (the shared profile must never carry `designation_level`, see AdminViewModeControls); `/api/notifications`, the Finance counters and the other 9 Orders pages and 13 Attendance pages that still re-read their own profile are follow-ups.
- **Proof.** 16 new tests in `src/hooks/queries/sharedReads.test.ts` (the cached row and permissions are reused only while fresh; stale, failed and invalidated rows are read again; the running read is joined, a failed one is not trusted; the widget and the three pages contain no read of their own); the old source pins that quoted the old call text now quote the new one (7 files), and the startup-count test records DETAIL 48 -> 45, DRAFTS 4 -> 2, PI_DETAIL 31 -> 28. Full suite, `tsc --noEmit` and eslint clean (numbers in the PR). **Verified live after the deploy (about 21:00 IST, owner's signed-in Chrome, the browser's resource timing, cold loads):** `/orders/drafts` 8 -> 5 requests (the 1-column `users` read, the second `resolve_effective_permissions` call and the 20-column `users` read are gone; its second step went from 5 requests to 3); `/finance` 15 -> 14, `/tasks/my` 13 -> 12, `/orders` 16 -> 15 (the widget's role read is gone). The request counts are exact; the times in that window are not reliable (slow, throttled test window), so no speed-up in milliseconds is claimed: expect tens of milliseconds to about 0.2 s per page, not seconds.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

## 2026-11-14 — Backups: the pg_dump step's `docker run` is stopped after 5 minutes (PR #780, squash-merged 2026-11-14 20:17 IST as `33813806`; backup tools installed 20:18 IST, byte-equal to merged main) · no SQL
- **Why.** During the pre-migration backup for #776, Docker Desktop hung on `docker run` (even `docker run --rm ... echo` never returned) while `docker info` still answered, so `backup-database.mjs` step 2b sat 20 minutes: its `spawnSync('docker', ...)` had no time limit. Docker Desktop was restarted by hand and works again.
- **Built.** `spawnSync` gets `timeout: PG_DUMP_TIMEOUT_MS` (5 minutes, `lib.mjs`) and `killSignal: 'SIGKILL'`; `pgDumpFailure()` names a timeout as "Docker Desktop is probably hung: restart it and run backup-all again" and still hides the database URL. The tables then stay NOT COVERED and `verify-backups` fails that layer, as before. Tests: `reliability.test.mjs` (+2; a real hanging child process is cut off), all 34 backup tests pass.
- **After the merge.** Run `node scripts/backup/install-backup-system.mjs` so the installed copy in `BOE-Development\backups\tools` gets the fix.
