## 2026-11-14 — The PI review chain: every hop between Operations and Sales passes through the approver (PR #776, squash-merged 2026-11-14 19:42 IST as `73a9cc30`, production deploy success 19:44 IST) · migration 20270718000000 APPLIED 14 Nov 2026, about 19:27 IST, before the merge
- **Owner ask (14 Nov, Order 547).** Operations flagged PI V1; the owner sent it to the salesperson; the salesperson's reply went straight back to Operations and the owner heard nothing. "For all to and fro, it has to go via me. I decide whether to pass it on or send it back. The interactions and changes should be tracked."
- **Root cause (read in the code).** `respond_order_handoff_clarification` (20270422000000) put the flagged handoff back to `awaiting` and told only the operations reviewer, and worked from the moment Operations flagged, so the approver's send-back was optional. A second direct leg: `order_operations_handoff_notify` (20270321000001) told the Order's salesperson the moment Operations flagged. `my_flagged_pis()` (20270611000000) showed Operations' reason to the salesperson at once. A remarks-only reply could say "revised the rates" while the PI was still V1. Nothing stored who held the item.
- **The rule now.** One holder at a time, stored on the live handoff: `chain_holder` = `operations` | `coordinator` (the approver) | `sales` (null once accepted or replaced), `chain_since`, `chain_round` (times Operations flagged this version). Operations and Sales can only hand it to the approver, who hands it on: `send_order_handoff_to_sales` (now also sets Sales as holder) or the NEW `forward_order_handoff_to_operations(handoff, note)`. The salesperson's reply door works only while Sales holds it, stores the reply, leaves the version flagged, puts it with the approver and tells the approvers (holders of `orders.approve_order`), not Operations. Operations' flag tells the approvers, not the salesperson (an acceptance still tells the salesperson). A BEFORE UPDATE trigger (`order_operations_handoffs_chain_sync`) moves the holder on every status change, so `decide_order_operations_handoff` is unchanged. A revised PI (Edit PI) already went through the approver's approval; unchanged.
- **Migration `20270718000000_order_handoff_review_chain.sql` (3 columns, 1 trigger, 1 new function, 4 re-issued).** Backfills live handoffs from the history (newest of created / flagged / sent to Sales / answered): Order 547 (answered under the old door) stays with Operations. Re-issues `respond_order_handoff_clarification`, `send_order_handoff_to_sales`, `order_operations_handoff_notify` and `my_flagged_pis` (an approver or administrator sees every flagged PI; anybody else sees only a PI the approver sent them, with `holder`, `holder_since`, `round`, `mine` and the reply). Rollback in its header. **Table-changing: backed up, rehearsed in a rolled-back transaction, then applied alone 14 Nov 2026, about 19:27 IST (before the merge, because the queues select `chain_holder`).**
- **Screens.** Order page: the strip says who holds it and the round (title suffix "Round 2" from the second flag), shows the salesperson's reply with "PI unchanged: still V1", and offers the approver **Forward reply to Operations** / **Return to Operations** (a note is required unless a reply is being passed on) beside **Send back to salesperson**; the salesperson's button is **Reply to the approver**, offered only while Sales holds it. A collapsible **PI review thread** lists every hop with its round (`lib/orders/piReviewThread.ts`, `PiReviewThread.tsx`). The approver's Needs-your-decision row names the holder (flagged / Sales replied / With Sales) with the round; the salesperson's strip lists only what was sent to them; For you shows only the reader's own work; the queues read the stored holder (`ordersWithSales`, replaces the history read); a "Sales replied" notification badge.
- **Proof.** `supabase/tests/order_handoff_review_chain_pglite.mjs` (24 checks on PGlite over the real guard trigger, the real `order_notify_users` and the real Edit-PI door, including the Order 547 flow end to end and every refusal), `operationsHandoffChain.test.tsx`, and the existing tests updated to the new rules.
- **Limits, said.** `order_notify_users` never rings the same person twice within two minutes for one Order and type, so a reply sent within two minutes of the flag notice is recorded but does not ring the bell. Order 547 is already with Operations and is not moved. A PI sent to Sales before Operations decided it (not flagged) is held by Sales but does not appear in the flagged lists; it is on the Order page and in the notice. No automatic reminders (the ageing colours on Needs your decision are the existing 1 day amber, 3 days red).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; for the database see the migration header.

## 2026-11-14 — Page speed, part 1: every record page prepared and cached, static files kept for a day, and a page-load audit (PR #775, squash-merged 2026-11-14 19:05 IST as `39fadbcf`, production deploy success 19:07 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "The delay in loading of pages is not acceptable, it should be less than a second but is around 4-6 seconds. Reanalyze every page for the smallest details that slow it, divide into parts, fix them all, learn from Notion, Monday and the best apps, change the process if needed; the only condition is that the front end and the features stay intact."
- **First finding, before any code.** Most of the 4-6 s was the owner's PC, not the app: CPU 100%, paging about 4,800 pages a second, 0.3 GB of RAM free, C: dipped to 0.10 GB free, other sessions' type-check and lint running, Windows Update. From that PC with curl the app is fast: HTML 0.15-0.5 s from the Mumbai edge, the database gateway 0.05-0.08 s on a warm connection, ping 3-50 ms. Twenty-seven finished worktrees were removed to free about 20 GB (standing rule). Measure on a healthy machine before blaming code.
- **Audit.** `scripts/audit-page-load.mjs` reads the source, follows each page's imports and writes `docs/app-context/PAGE_LOAD_AUDIT.generated.md`: 199 pages, with how each is served, its data calls, reads with no limit, fetch-all loops and polling. It cannot see run time, so its numbers point at where to look. Headlines: 147 of 199 pages are client pages; 162 of 179 data-reading pages already use the shared query cache; 360 reads have no row limit in 62 pages; fetch-all loops in 36 pages; the heaviest pages are `/orders/[id]` (147 call sites), `/orders/drafts/[id]` (134), `/finance` (100).
- **Built.** (1) 26 server layouts, one per dynamic segment, each an empty `generateStaticParams` (the recipe of `orders/[id]` from 22 Oct), so a record page is prepared the first time an address is asked for and then served from the edge cache instead of by a Tokyo server function on every open. Next's own build report: dynamic page routes prepared went from 6 to 35 of 36; the one left is the retired `customer-reviews/[id]` redirect. Measured from the owner's PC: about 0.27 s from the cache against 0.46 s through the function for an address already opened. A first open of a new address costs the same as before; a nested dynamic segment needs its own layout (a parent's export does not cover it). (2) `/brand/*` and `/manifest.json` are sent with `max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800` instead of `max-age=0`, so the browser stops asking the server about each one on every page load (the manifest on every move between pages).
- **Proof.** `src/lib/performance/recordPagesCached.test.ts` (8) scans every dynamic route: each has the layout, the layout is a server component with an empty `generateStaticParams`, nothing under a cached route reads cookies or headers, and the two Cache-Control rules and the never-cached service worker are pinned; full suite 21,046 tests, 0 failures, 9 skipped; `next build` passes; eslint clean. **Verified live after the deploy (about 19:10 IST, from the owner's PC, curl):** `/manifest.json`, `/brand/svg/favicon.svg` and `/brand/icons/icon-192.png` now answer `max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800` (were `max-age=0`); `/tasks/x`, `/meetings/x`, `/quotations/x`, `/vendors/x` and `/handover/x` answer `x-vercel-cache: HIT` and 0.23-0.28 s on repeat requests (were MISS and about 0.46 s); the first request of a new address is still 0.8-1.6 s (the page is prepared then). Not proven: the effect on a real person's 4-6 s, which was mostly the overloaded PC.
- **Not changed.** No page, no feature, no layout of any screen; no SQL. The owner's earlier decisions stand (nothing is prefetched; the protected page never mounts before access is known).
- **Next parts (each its own PR; see dossier 08 trap 16).** Part 3 `fetchAllRows` reads later pages in parallel (it reads them one after another today); Part 4 the biggest pages read what is below the fold after first paint; Part 5 fold the duplicate start-up reads; Part 2 persisted cache and Part 7 database region need the owner's decision.

## 2026-11-14 — Quotations: Import brings a BOE PI's prices and customization (PR #773, squash-merged 2026-11-14 13:25 IST as `111f4ef6`, deployed 13:28 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov).** "When we are importing a PI to the quotation module, it is not fetching price, which it should" and "take prices and all other details of products mentioned in the uploaded PI". Before, Import never brought a price (the 12 Nov decision, made for a client's BOQ) and also dropped a PI's "Customization" text.
- **Built.** `importBoq.ts` reads a price column headed as BOE's PIs head it ("Cost per piece", "Original price", "Unit price", "Price per piece", bare "Cost"), whole rupees, never a second price column or a dollar one; a column headed only "Price", "Rate", "Net rate", "BOE - Price" is read too but shaded (a client's BOQ can head its own rate that way); "Customization(s)" joins the line's Remarks. The review table gets an editable **Price ₹** column when the file had prices (a missing one is shaded); the builder says how many came with a price. Every PDF or photo still brings none. Decision 12 of dossier 29 amended. The owner never picked between "PI headings only" and "any price column"; the shaded middle path was chosen after real files showed about 1 in 20 PIs head the price plainly.
- **Pictures.** A PI row can hold several pictures (the representative image in column E, customization pictures further right); a row now keeps the LEFTMOST, not the first the file lists. Measured on 548 products of 35 real PIs: 497 have a picture anchored on their own row, 8 only one row off (not taken), 43 none near; where column E holds one it is the leftmost 360 times of 362.
- **Not imported (said to the owner, who also said "no header or footer").** The PI's Code, internal "BOE Remarks", fabric and packing columns, and the footer and header (discount, fabric, packing, GST, client, phone, project); a PI that is a PDF or a photo; the PI's own "Terms and Conditions" block: the quotation already prints the same standard terms on every quotation (32 of 37 sampled PIs carry that identical text, 3 versions in all).
- **Proof.** `importBoq.test.ts` (7 new: a PI grid shaped after 80 sampled real PIs, "Original Price" and bare "Cost", plain "Price"/"Rate" shaded, what is not a price, rounding, the quotation line, a real .xlsx with its picture; the AI reader never brings a price); the real reader over 157 of the owner's real PI files (counts only: 154 read, 132 with prices, 2,213 of 3,069 products priced; Σ quantity × price equals the PI's own Total Cost for 119 of 123); tsc and eslint clean. Not proven: a real PI file through the real dialog on a screen.
- **Live check (14 Nov).** The owner merged PR #773 at 13:25 IST (`111f4ef6`); the GitHub production deployment 7784669281 for that commit reached `success` at 13:28 IST (Vercel). Not done: the dialog seen signed in with a real PI, or on a phone. The full `npm test` passed (21,012 tests, 0 failures, 9 skipped) before the rebase onto the then-current `main`; after it, tsc, eslint and the two Import and builder test files were re-run, not the whole suite.

## 2026-11-14 — A slow wait shows the BOE One loader over the whole screen (PR #770, squash-merged 12:36 IST as `3dd0cc93`, deployed 12:38 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask (14 Nov, a console screenshot: `manifest.json` `net::ERR_TIMED_OUT`, pages slow).** "In case the page is taking longer to load, show a loading screen covering the full screen with a nice BOE One logo animation where the logo is being formed, in a loop. It should look premium." The timeout itself is not BOE code: from outside, `manifest.json` answered in 0.13-0.40 s (HTTP 200, 1.2 KB, cached at Vercel) and pages in 0.13-0.55 s; it matches the 11 Nov `ERR_CONNECTION_RESET` (the owner's PC connection). The loader makes a slow wait look deliberate; it does not make the network faster.
- **Built, piece 1: the page loader.** `LoadingScreen` (`components/ui/atoms.tsx`, about 190 call sites in 115 files, none edited) is now `BoeOneLoader` (`components/ui/BoeOneLoader.tsx`): the brand logo path, unchanged, is drawn as an outline, floods to terracotta, takes one sweep of light, lets go and repeats (3.6 s). It covers the whole screen (fixed, z-index 10000), is transparent for its first 0.8 s (a CSS delay, so a quick page never flashes it and it also works in the server-rendered HTML before any script runs), and, from 1 s after it shows, a creative one-liner under the logo to lift the mood ("Good things take a moment. This one's worth it.", "A sip of chai, and we're right back."; 8 lines in `CHEER_LINES`, one every 4.5 s, pure CSS, fixed order, hidden from screen readers). The owner asked for these instead of a "taking longer than usual, check your internet" notice, so the loader no longer says anything about the connection. Reduced motion shows the finished logo and the first line, still. `cheer={false}` for the one screen that is not a wait (Task not found).
- **Built, piece 2: slow clicks.** Pages load on click (#682), so a slow click showed nothing. `AppLink` now holds a render-nothing `NavPendingProbe` (`useLinkStatus`) that marks `<html data-boe-nav-pending>` (`lib/navigation/navWait.ts`, a counter so two waiting clicks cannot hide each other's loader) while a link to a different page waits; a hidden copy of the loader in the root layout shows after 1.2 s and lets clicks through. Links that only change the query or hash (filters, tabs) do not trigger it.
- **Left as it was.** The skeleton screens of Orders, Finance and Control Center; the old `.boe-loading` and `.boe-loading-spinner` rules (the payroll saving overlay and the task-load error box still use them); `AppLink`'s `prefetch={false}` (the guard test now pins `<Link {...props} prefetch={false}>`, since the link has children).
- **Proof.** `BoeOneLoader.test.tsx` (18, including that the line list and the CSS cycle cannot drift apart) and `navWait.test.ts` (8, the counter against a real DOM) plus the updated `noBackgroundPrefetch.test.ts`; tsc and eslint clean. Seen in a browser from the real component and the real stylesheet (static stand-in page): transparent until 0.8 s, full 1280x800 cover, outline then fill then sweep, the hidden copy appears only when `<html>` is marked and after 1.2 s, the lines start at 1.8 s and change every 4.5 s with exactly one visible, each fits two lines at 375x812. Not proven: on the live site, on a throttled connection, signed in, a real phone; the reduced-motion look (CSS read and pinned, not seen).
- **Not covered.** A page opened by code after a save (`router.push`); a first request whose HTML never arrives (the browser's own blank page).
- **Live check (14 Nov, after the deploy, from outside, no sign-in).** The served `/login` page carries the hidden click-wait loader with its 8 lines (the markup of every page, since it sits in the root layout), and the live stylesheet has the loader rules (`boe-loader-line` keyframes, the `html[data-boe-nav-pending]` reveal). Not seen: the animation running live, on a throttled connection or on a real phone; a slow click through the real app.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

A forgiving PI reader that finds the table by column names (the strict PI reader opened 0 of 60 sampled old PIs), proves itself against the file's Sub Total, flags what it worked out or doubts, leaves out US-dollar files, quantities-only lists, sample lines, older versions and copies. ## 2026-11-14 — Sales: the lead popup laid out again, board cards with no buttons, and the Lead Board and popup made fast (PR #761, squash-merged 06:02 IST as `8b64c4d3`, deployed 06:04 IST) · `20270716000000` → APPLIED by the owner 2026-11-14 (run by hand after a verified backup, recorded with `migration repair`)

The same open and close through `window.history.replaceState` took ~50 ms.

- **Proof.** PGlite suite 141/141, including a new step that compares `sales_board_summary` with `sales_board` key by key for 6 roles x 8 filters (579 stuck leads counted); `boardSpeed.test.ts` (new), the card and board tests changed on purpose; the real `WorkPanel` and `Card` rendered with the real stylesheet matched the approved preview. Not proven before the merge: the speed after the deploy (measured below), a phone.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; `drop function public.sales_board_summary(text, text, text, text, text, text);` (it is applied).
- **Live measurement (the owner's signed-in Chrome, read-only, 2026-11-14 after the deploy).** A click on a card to the popup showing: 3.5 to 3.9 s -> 35 to 100 ms, with no `/sales/board?_rsc` request. Escape to the popup gone: about 1.1 s with a 0.7 s freeze -> 32 to 37 ms, no long task (the first open of a session had one 155 ms task). Column pages 0.29 to 0.62 s -> 0.2 to 0.5 s. After the SQL was applied, `20270716000000-compare.sql` on the real database (14,860 leads): old `sales_board` 343 ms and 308 KB, new `sales_board_summary` 43 ms and 1.0 KB, the same answer; in the browser the totals call took 0.51 s and 0.74 s on two loads (it was 1.19 s and 0.88 s). Between the deploy and the apply the page asked for the missing function first (a 404) and the totals arrived about 0.3 s later than before; that is gone now.
- **Still slow, not changed.** The board does not start reading until the Sales layout has finished its own checks (the permission read and three `users` reads, about 0.4 to 0.5 s after the page's first request); letting the queries start before the permission read finished changed nothing because of that gate. Next candidate: find and shorten that gate. Not seen on a phone.

- **Done outside the app (read-only, verified).** A local backup of all 521 orders and 1,064 files (4.3 GB) in `~/BOE-Development/backups/clickup-running-orders/2026-11-13/`, with the owner's one-off ClickUp token (to be deleted in ClickUp).
- **Built.** Dossier 01 "Draft Orders (ClickUp)": Administrators-only sidebar entry; list with status tabs, search and Excel; an order page with every field, files with upload dates, comments and history; a browser import of the backup folder that sends files straight to private storage.
- **Database.** New closed table `clickup_draft_orders` and private bucket `clickup-draft-files`; nothing existing is touched. It creates a table, so by the owner's rule back up first. Applied by hand (`db push` would also push 20270624-0628).
- **Proof.** PGlite 17/17, 23 unit tests, the real backup through the import's validator (521 of 521). Not proven: a signed-in screen, the folder import end to end, the project's Storage size limit.
- **Rollback.** Empty the bucket, drop the table, delete the bucket row (dossier 01).

## 2026-11-13 — Confirmed Order on a phone: the Pendencies card no longer wraps into hidden side-by-side columns (PR #753, squash-merged 19:03 IST as `e9a5f6bd`, deployed 19:05 IST) · no SQL
- **Seen** (Order 546, a 390 px wide frame, real data): "Pending from client" (its list and the add box) and the Update button / read-only note were drawn to the right of "Fabric / Finish" at x=298..677 in a 375 px column and cut off by `.boe-main-content { overflow: clip }`. No scrollbar showed, so the clipping hid the fault. At 755 and 1425 px wide the card was fine.
- **Cause.** `.order-ff` is `display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap`. In the summary panel the card is switched to `flex-direction: column` but keeps `wrap`. The 560 px phone rule gives `.order-ff-items` and `.order-ff-end` `flex-basis: 100%` (written for the old one-line strip); in a column that resolves against the grid-stretched height, so each child fills a whole column and the card wraps into four.
- **Fix.** CSS only, five lines in `src/app/globals.css` after `.order-sum-groups .order-ff-end .order-status-action`: `flex-wrap: nowrap` on the stacked variants (`.order-sum-groups > .order-ff`, `.order-docs-row > .order-ff`) and `flex-basis: auto` on their items and action rows. Measured in the live page at 770 and 1440 px: every block has the same position and size before and after.
- **Checked.** At 390 px with the rules injected the card stacks (title, Fabric, Finish, Pending from client, Update) and the inline Approve form opens inside it. A script listing every visible element outside the content column found 22 on the Order page before, 3 after (all inside the closed Details popover, see the dossier), and 0 on `/orders`, `/orders/all`, `/orders/drafts` and `/orders/drafts/<id>`.
- **Test.** `orderDetailPolish.render.test.tsx` pins the two rules.
- **Live check after the deploy (19:05 IST, no injected CSS).** Order 546 at 390 px: `flex-wrap: nowrap`, all four blocks stacked at x=33 inside the 375 px column; at 770 and 1440 px every block has the same position and size as before the fix. Not yet seen on a real phone.
- **Not fixed (own follow-ups).** The Main PI "Details" popover (`.order-pi-details-pop`, 270 px wide, anchored at the left edge of its button) runs about 17 px past the column when opened at 390 px. The Approve / Remove text buttons in Pending from client are 12 px text with no padding, well under the 44 px touch target.

## 2026-11-13 — Day Close and the Scoreboard are paused for the team behind one Control Center switch (PR #746, squash-merged 2026-11-13 as `22d9f772` at 13:43 IST, deployed 13:45 IST) · `20270704000000` → APPLIED 2026-11-13 by the owner (backup of 13:30 IST, verified, used as the rollback copy)
- **Owner ask.** The team had not been introduced to Day Close or the Scoreboard but already saw the red "Your plan was due at 10:30 am" strip and the Scoreboard card. Hide them "till I introduce them", and (owner's choice) pause the rules behind them too.
- **Built.** Control Center > Day Close & Scoreboard > Go live: one switch, starting PAUSED. Paused hides the Modules strip, the red / strike / proof banner on the Today card, the Scoreboard card and evening line, the vote card and the Star Seller card, and stops the nightly strikes, the nightly score record, the proof rule and the leave lock. Switching ON asks for a second click and counts from that day.
- **Fails closed.** Anything but a stored `true` is paused, and an unreadable switch (migration not applied) is paused, so the code can deploy first and hide everything at once. The proof rule is a database trigger, so it keeps asking for proof until the migration is applied.
- **Database.** Two columns on `day_close_settings` (`team_live` default false, `live_since`) and the proof-check function restated with one extra condition. The table stays closed to the browser. Alters a table: back up before applying.
- **Not touched.** Strikes already written, who takes part, Team Today, the Monthly Review, ratings.
- **Proof.** PGlite 7 checks on a stand-in database (`day_close_team_switch_pglite.mjs`); `teamSwitch.test.ts` (15), `dayCardTeamSwitch.render.test.tsx` (3), `leaveLock.test.ts`, `navModel.test.ts`, both migration ledgers.
- **Applied.** The owner ran a rolled-back rehearsal on the live database ("REHEARSAL OK", nothing kept), then the migration with `supabase db query --linked --file`, then `supabase migration repair 20270704000000 --status applied`. Read-only check afterwards: one settings row, `team_live = false` (paused), `live_since` null, the proof function reads the switch, `authenticated` cannot read the settings; `migration list` shows `20270704000000` in `remote`. A backup started at 13:45 IST was cut off when the owner's terminal window closed (stale lock file, partial export folder `20261113T081539Z`; the lock is ignored once its process is dead); the verified 13:30 IST export (258 tables, 464,682 rows) was the rollback copy. **The team is NOT switched on:** it stays paused until an administrator turns it on in Control Center > Day Close & Scoreboard > Go live. Not seen signed in.
- **Overlap.** Open PR #744 hides only the Top 5 behind its own switch (another column of the same table, same Scoreboard files); independent, expect a mechanical merge-in for whichever lands second.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR, then drop the two columns and restore `day_close_task_proof_check()` from `20270624000000` (header of the migration).

## 2026-11-13 — Bank Statements: a reversed card debit no longer stops the upload (PR #743, merged 2026-11-13 as `48b0cd83` at 12:40 IST, production deployment succeeded 12:43 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner report.** Uploading the HDFC PRI statement to 13 Nov failed with "Line 2 (2026-11-03) must have exactly one of withdrawal or deposit."
- **Cause.** HDFC wrote a Vercel reversal as a NEGATIVE withdrawal (`-47.64`, balance up). The parser refused any negative amount.
- **Fix.** `parseHdfcStatement` stores a negative withdrawal as a deposit; the summary totals check subtracts reversals from both sides (the bank counts them inside Debits). Balance chain and database rules unchanged. A negative deposit is still refused.
- **Proof.** `bankStatement.test.ts` (4 new cases); the real 14-line statement now parses with opening 186,970.15, closing 167,181.80, debits 19,788.35, credits 0. Not uploaded signed in.
- **Known gap.** The reversal lands in Money in as Not matched; mark it "Refund / reversal" by hand.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database changed.

  - **Owner's rating:** 1–5 with a reason the person reads, from the 25th. It is worth its number of points, on Team Today for the reviewer only.
  - Both close at 23:00 IST on the last day. The vote counts in the month's final record.
- **Database.** `scoreboard_votes` (not self, at most 3 a voter, never changed) and `scoreboard_ratings`, both closed to the browser.
- **Proof.**
  - `vote.test.ts` and the new `score.test.ts` cases: 21 tests with the existing ones.

## 2026-11-12 — Confirmed Payments: search by any order number, the order's confirmed total, correct an old order's client (PR #719, squash-merged 2026-11-13 as `ebfc7df8`, deployed 08:39 IST; PR 2 of 2, built on PR 1) · `20270701000000` → APPLIED 2026-11-13 alone (db query --file + migration repair), after a verified backup and a rolled-back rehearsal
- **Owner ask.** Search should find a payment by the order number or PI number it is recorded against and, once it shows the result, show the total confirmed amount of that order; and the client of an old order must be addable and editable.
- **Built.**
  - **Search** also matches `order_search_text` (a computed field on `finance_received_payments`): every Order number, PI Draft reference or reserved number, the PI an Order came from, and old order number and client of the payment's active allocations. Before, only the one Order the projection names was searchable.
  - **Order total banner** above the list when the term (3 characters or more) names an order: `Order 0425 · ₹12,50,000.00 confirmed · 3 payments`, from `finance_order_confirmed_totals`. It is the money allocated to the order on every page, ignoring the date and status filters, and never the sum of the visible Amount cells (a split payment would count twice). Several matches: up to five, with a note to type the full number.
  - **Old order client:** Edit Received Payment lists each old order of the payment with its client; a change calls `correct_legacy_order_client` (`finance.manage`), is recorded on the payment's history (new event `allocation_legacy_client_corrected`), and never writes the payment row, so it works while a cash handover locks the payment.
- **Database.** Three functions; the allocation guard restated (20270407 §2 plus a marker clause for the client name of one allocation); the activity CHECK widened by one event. No table, no row, no amount changes.
- **Numbering and apply (13 Nov).** Numbered `20270624000000` first (taken by #721 while this was open), then `20270626000000` (Scoreboard slice 2, #726), then `20270627000000` (Scoreboard slice 3, #727); now `20270701000000`, deliberately clear of the Scoreboard and Quotations sequence (`28` and `29` are claimed by open PRs). Applied to the live database ALONE (`db query --file`, then `migration repair --status applied`), after a verified backup, a rolled-back rehearsal as a real admin and a dry-run; a read-only check as a real admin passed afterwards. It was applied while still numbered `20270626000000`; its ledger row was moved to `27` and finally to `20270701000000` (two `migration repair` commands each time), so the Scoreboard files `26` and `27` are not hidden from a later `db push`. The push of both pending migrations had failed: Day Close's `20270624000000` stops on its own dependency check because the live database has no Day Close tables (the ledger rows for 22 and 23 both belong to quotation versions), and the Scoreboard tables are missing too. A plain `db push` now needs `--include-all` for `24` to `27`.
- **Proof.** `confirmed_payments_order_search_pglite.mjs` runs the real migration in a throwaway Postgres: 38 checks, and 14 deliberate breaks of the migration each make it fail. Finance, orders, navigation, Day Close, Scoreboard and hooks tests, 0 failures; `tsc`; eslint on the changed files.
- **Not proved.** PostgREST accepting the computed field inside the search filter was proved on PGlite only, not on the live PostgREST. Not seen signed in. No browser test (owner's rule).
- **Rollback.** In the migration header: drop the three functions, re-emit the 20270407 guard; the event value may stay in the CHECK. Code first reverts cleanly (the search falls back by reverting the one column).

## 2026-11-12 — Payroll and the My pages read your own row once (PR open, branch `perf/context-reuses-profile`) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "Yes, fix it", after the live recount of #711/#712 found one repeat left.
- **The repeat.** On Payroll, My Attendance, My Credits and My Payroll the page is not behind a ModuleGuard, so it reads the 8-column row first. The shell's `usePermissionContext` started about 1.2 s later and always read the identical row again: 4 own-row reads instead of 3.
- **Changed.** New `profileForFirstResolve(qc, userId)`: on the context's FIRST resolve, a profile row read in the last 30 s (and not marked stale) is reused instead of read again. A refetch always reads, so a role change still reaches an open page within 30 s. A null row (failed or missing) is never reused, so a failed read still denies.
- **Tests.** Three new checks in `sharedReads.test.ts` (reused on the first resolve; a refetch reads; missing, null, older than 30 s or stale reads). Full suite: 20,687 tests, 0 failed, 9 skipped; `tsc` and eslint clean.

## 2026-11-12 — Permissions looked up once in Meetings, Review Workflow, Sample Tracking and Assets (PR #712, squash-merged 2026-11-12 22:13 IST as `3e26083e`, deployed 22:16 IST) · no SQL
- **Owner ask.** "check other modules for duplicate requests too", then "Yes, build both". This is PR 2 of 2; PR 1 (#711) reads your own row once.
- **Measured live before.** Each module's gate already loads the profile and the permissions; these screens loaded them again.
  - Review Workflow: the profile 3 more times and the permissions 3 more. The gate asked twice, and the landing page started `useCustomerReviews` twice.
  - Meetings: the role read twice, and the permissions looked up by its hand-written gate.
  - Assets & Access: the profile once more and the permissions twice more (start-up, then an immediate re-resolve of the same view).
  - Sample Tracking: the profile once more and the permissions once more.
- **Changed.**
  - The Meetings and Review Workflow gates read the signed-in person's permission context (`usePermissionContext`: the profile plus one `resolve_effective_permissions_for_user` call for every module), as the Orders gate has since `202cbaf8`.
  - Same rules. Meetings: admin, or `view`, or `manage`. Review Workflow: an active account with `use` or `verify`, no admin short-circuit. A failed profile read or a failed permission lookup still refuses.
  - New `cachedModulePermissions(qc, userId, moduleKey)` returns the gate's answer for one module only while it is under 30 seconds old; otherwise the caller asks the database as before. `useMeetings`, `useCustomerReviews`, the Samples page and the Assets page use it on first load. Focus refreshes still ask the database.
  - The Review Workflow landing page renders `CustomReviewsScreen` directly instead of running `useCustomerReviews` a second time only to wait for it.
  - Assets does not re-resolve the view it was just opened on; switching views later still re-resolves.
  - The four screens read the profile through `profileQueryOptions`. Assets used to add `position`, which nothing on the page read.
- **Tests.**
  - New: three checks of `cachedModulePermissions` in `sharedReads.test.ts` (a fresh copy; an empty map is no grant; stale or missing asks again).
  - Restated to the new code with the same rules: `adminBypass` (the gate reads no role; a failed resolve is an empty map), `testCardPurge`, `customerReviewOutreach` (the route guard), `enforcement` (Meetings still resolves permissions).
  - Full suite: 20,640 tests, 0 failed, 9 skipped. `tsc` and eslint are clean.
- **Live recount 2026-11-12 ~22:30 IST** (production `3e26083e`, full page loads in Chrome, counted once the page was quiet for 3 s). Every page reads your own row 3 times (profile, role, designation) and looks permissions up once (`_for_user`), with 0 exact repeats and 0 failed requests.
  - Meetings 7 requests (before: the role read twice and a separate lookup).
  - Review Workflow 13 (before: the profile 3 more times and 3 more lookups). Its three `customer_review_custom_submissions` reads are three different queries, not a repeat.
  - Sample Tracking 15, Assets & Access 13.

- **Why.** The owner asked: "Is there anything which can generate similar kind of errors in future then do work on it and solve it". A read-only sweep of about 38 pages in the owner's Chrome found:
  - pages covered: every module home, plus the Orders, Finance, Tasks and Sales sub-pages;
  - 0 console errors or warnings from BOE, 0 failed requests and 0 unused stylesheets;

  - `next/link` outside AppLink when written with double quotes or loaded by `import()`/`require()`;

## 2026-11-12 — Pages load on click: no page is loaded in advance, anywhere (branch `perf/load-on-click`) · no migration
- **Owner decision.** "Yes, load on click" (2026-11-12), after the console kept filling with "preloaded but not used" on every page ("this has really become too common").
- **Why the earlier fixes could not end it.** The owner's screenshots held 72 and then 173 warnings but only 2-3 distinct files (Control Center `0mn.s1bn975w.css`, Orders `0vpo57o72-4do.css`, Attendance & Payroll `0-se8gau-3wku.css`, Pricing's `01yo0_929s3t8.css`, the Dashboard's `0w~i7isy3ru4c.css`). Chrome re-reports every still-unused preloaded file as the person moves around a long-open tab, so any page loaded in advance and not opened (a For-you card, a menu item, a card the pointer rested on) multiplies. #649, #651, #656, #658 and #659 narrowed WHEN pages loaded; this removes it.
- **Changed.** New `src/components/ui/AppLink.tsx` (`next/link` with `prefetch={false}` forced after the caller's props). All 113 files that imported `next/link` now import `AppLink as Link` (one line each, by script; nothing else in them changed). Every `router.prefetch` is gone: Dashboard (`/tasks/my`, `/notifications` on mount), Modules cards (250 ms rest), My Tasks and Assigned By Me rows, the shell's Home button, the Orders/Finance switch, the Finance/Orders menus and rail Home link, Quick Actions, Notifications task titles, Confirmed Orders rows, PI Drafts rows, the Orders dashboard sections, the showroom editor's neighbours. Deleted: `src/hooks/useIntentWarm.ts` and the local `useWarmOnRest`. React Query data warm-ups (`prefetchQuery`) stay: they load data, not pages, and bring no stylesheet.
- **Cost, accepted by the owner.** The first click to a page waits for it (its loading skeleton shows) instead of being ready in advance.
- **Tests.** New guard `src/lib/navigation/noBackgroundPrefetch.test.ts`: AppLink is the only `next/link` importer, it forces prefetch off, nothing calls `router.prefetch`. Removed the superseded guards `intentPrefetch.test.ts`, `warmOnIntent.test.ts`, `notificationsIntentPrefetch.test.ts`; restated the pins in `orderStartupShape`, `taskNavigationPerformance`, `taskReturnPath`, `taskDetailOpenFromDrawer`, `controlCenterNav`, `finalApprovalScope` (one import line).
- **Supersedes.** PR #679 (For-you links on intent), closed unmerged.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; no data involved.

## 2026-11-12 — Backup: the copy to the external drive now updates moved branches (PR #676, squash-merged 2026-11-12 10:20 IST as `26e8e2f1`) · no migration
- **Found.** The owner asked for a backup on the hard disk (D:) on 2026-11-12. After the copy, 20 of 2,398 refs in the drive's git mirror (`main` included) still named their old commit. The second-copy step skipped any file already on the drive with the same size, and every git ref is a 41-byte file, so a branch that moved was never updated. Objects were always copied, so no commit was missing, but a restore from the drive alone would have opened on old branch tips. The nightly task would have repeated it every night.
- **Fixed.** `copyGitMirror` (`scripts/backup/lib.mjs`) copies objects first (skipped when already there: they never change), then refs, HEAD and the rest always, so a ref never lands before its commit. `backup-all.mjs` uses it for `git/boe-task-management.git`; the other layers copy as before (storage stays incremental by size).
- **Proof.** Two new tests in `lib.test.mjs` (a moved ref with the same size reaches the drive; an object already there is left alone). The old rule, run on the same kind of fixture, leaves the stale ref. All 32 backup tests pass. Run on the real mirror against D:: 2,398 refs equal to local, no tip missing.
- **Installed.** 2026-11-12 10:20 IST, from the merged `main`: all 16 tools and 4 hooks in `BOE-Development\backups` are byte-equal to the repo, and the 32 backup tests pass from the installed folder. The old tools are kept in `BOE-Development\temporary\backup-tools-before-pr676`. The first nightly with the fix is 9:30 pm on 2026-11-12, and it copies to D: only if the drive is plugged in.
- **Rollback.** Revert the commit and reinstall the tools.

## 2026-11-12 — Quotations, phase 1: build by room type, price, print, share a client link (PR #672, squash-merged by the owner 2026-11-12 05:35 IST as `7f491957`, deployed 05:37 IST) · `20270614000000` → APPLIED 2026-11-12 (about 06:00 IST, after the merge and the 05:35-05:59 IST backup, alone; recorded with `migration repair`; 20270610000000 stays pending on purpose)
- **Owner ask (2026-11-12).** A quotation maker inside BOE One to replace the Base44 tool: import any client file, clean up client pictures, easy to fill, share and reshare, connected to the other modules, and the base of the Pricing module. Planned the same night (plan + 15 mockups); the owner delegated every decision and asked for phase 1 to be built, tested and merged.
- **Built.** New module `quotations` (view, create, edit, view_all; Admin only until granted). `/quotations` list, `/quotations/[id]` builder (room types × rooms, products with pictures, sizes in feet and inches shown in inches, per-room or fixed quantity, price, GST rate, optional lines, discount, fabric, packing, notes), `/quotations/[id]/print` (Print or save as PDF, the PI sheet's terms word for word), `/q/[token]` client link (no login) and a WhatsApp message with the link. History of every save, status change, share and client open.
- **Database.** Four tables closed to every client role; all reads and writes through SECURITY DEFINER functions; `quotation_public(token)` is the only one open to anon and returns client-safe fields only. Public picture bucket `quotation-images`. Totals are never stored; the same arithmetic in `math.ts` and `quotation_value_before_gst`.
- **Safe either way round.** Before the migration is applied the tile shows for Admin only and every page says "being set up"; no other module is touched.

Not seen signed in.
- **Rollback.** In the migration header: drop the four tables, the sequence and the quotation_* functions, the two storage policies and the bucket, and the registration rows for `quotations`. Revert the code commit.

## 2026-11-11 — Sales: a WhatsApp chat is judged before it becomes a lead (PR #668, squash-merged 2026-11-11 20:56 IST as `20395a5f`) · `20270613000000` → APPLIED 2026-11-11 (about 19:30 IST, before the merge, alone; 20270610000000 stays pending on purpose)
- **Owner ask (2026-11-11).** "We get all our leads on the company's WhatsApp number" (+91 87691 13712); many chats are job seekers, suppliers or transport companies; the system should tell from the message whether it is a lead and only then move it to the Lead Master with a tag. Plan approved with its defaults.
- **Changed.** The WhatsApp route asks the database which numbers are already leads (`sales_chat_context`, service role), sends a NEW number's profile name and words (never the number) to Claude (`src/lib/salesLeads/classify.ts`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `WA_CLASSIFY_MODEL` optional), and passes the verdict to `capture_sales_chats` (replaced, same signature). Lead: as before, tagged `wa_tag` detected + `wa_reason`. Not a lead: kept in `sales_whatsapp_held` (last 5 messages, 30 days) and shown in Filtered chats (`/sales/lead-master/filtered-chats`, Sales `assign`) with This is a lead (`make_whatsapp_chat_a_lead`) and Clear (`clear_whatsapp_held_chat`). Unclear or any failure: a lead tagged Check, not allotted automatically, allotters told "WhatsApp chat to check"; its next messages are judged again (an ad on such a chat is kept). An ad chat is always a lead; a known lead is never judged. `sales_lead_row` re-issued with `wa_tag`, `wa_reason`.
- **Order is safe either way round, but migration first is the rule.** With the migration applied and the old code live, rows arrive without a verdict and behave exactly as before. With the new code live and the migration missing, `sales_chat_context` does not exist, the route answers 500 and Meta retries for 36 hours.
- **Not connected.** The owner's number +91 87691 13712 is still not on the WhatsApp API (coexistence provider undecided); this runs on Meta's test number until it is.
- **Rollback.** In the migration header: restore `capture_sales_chats` (20270609000000) and `sales_lead_row` (20270602000000), drop the four new functions and the table, drop the two columns. Leads created stay.
- **Apply (done 2026-11-11 by Claude at the owner's "apply the migration").** Read-only pre-check first: the live `capture_sales_chats` and `sales_lead_row` bodies matched 20270609000000 and 20270602000000 exactly (md5), none of the new objects existed, 20270612000000 applied and 20270610000000 pending. `backup-all` + `verify-backups` (235 tables, 463,911 rows, 0 errors; all required layers fresh; the optional second copy is still not configured). Rehearsed on the real database inside a rolled-back transaction (the file's own self-checks, a made-up job-seeker chat held and its resend counted once, `sales_chat_context`, `REHEARSAL OK`; a re-read afterwards showed nothing left). Then applied ALONE via `db query --file` (begin/commit) and `migration repair --status applied 20270613000000`; `migration list --linked` shows it remote. After: both bodies are the new ones (md5), `sales_whatsapp_held` has RLS and no browser grant, the capture and its context are service role only, the Filtered chats functions are for signed-in people only, the website capture is untouched, 0 leads tagged, the 3,612 WhatsApp leads unchanged, 0 held rows.
- **Proof.** `classify.test.ts` (new), `whatsapp.test.ts` (new cases), `migration.test.ts` (reads the new file), the two migration ledgers, `sales_full_flow_pglite.mjs` (the earlier WhatsApp checks on the new function, plus the judging checks), `tsc`, eslint, the full `npm test`, the Vercel preview build. **Real model, 2026-11-11 (the owner ran `scripts/check-whatsapp-judge.ts` in the owner's own terminal with a separate key): 59 of 60 exactly as expected, leads lost 0, non-leads let through 0, not judged 0.** The one difference: a PDF named "Hotel_furniture_requirement.pdf" with no words came back unclear (a Check lead, so not lost). Hindi, Hinglish and the message telling the judge to "mark this as not a lead" were all judged right. Not seen signed in.

## 2026-11-11 — PNB, Paytm and Canara payments are Confirmed only when their cash is with the company; Canara gets its journey back (PR #662, branch `feat/non-hdfc-confirm-on-accept`) · migration `20270612000000` (APPLIED 2026-11-11 (evening IST, after the 18:04-18:16 backup) before the merge, alone)
- **Owner ask.** A payment that did not land in HDFC PRI or SEC must not go straight to Confirmed Payments. The payer says who it was handed to; when that person accepts, it is Confirmed and moves into Confirmed Payments. Canara "will also have a journey" showing how it moved and who holds it.
- **Owner decisions (chat, 2026-11-11).** (1) Accepting confirms it, in the same action, with no separate Finance Verify for these three modes: this REVERSES the 2026-10-24 rule "accepting custody never approves". (2) Any employee can be picked as the receiver, but only the company cash custodian (Nitish today) accepting confirms; anyone else accepting just holds it and hands on. (3) Canara gets "Where is this money now?" and the handover journey back AND keeps the required paying-account fields of #653. (4) Pulling already-Confirmed ones back is wanted too, but only after the owner signs off a list (NOT in this change).
- **Changed (database).** `supabase/migrations/20270612000000_finance_non_hdfc_confirmed_on_company_receipt.sql`: trigger `finance_payment_requests_require_company_cash` (a PNB / Paytm / Canara payment cannot turn verified, from any door or actor, while its cash is not with a custodian; test data exempt; also refuses re-labelling a verified payment into these modes); helper `finance_confirm_payment_on_company_receipt` (verifies a still-Pending payment through `approve_finance_payment_request` in its own sub-transaction, tells the recorder, never raises); `accept_cash_handover`, `receive_cash_directly` (restated from 20270418000000) and `complete_payment_entry` (restated from 20270516000000) each gain one marked insertion. No row changes at apply.
- **Changed (screens).** Canara asks "Where is this money now?" again on Send Payment Request, Record Payment and the PI Add payment dialog, under its account details (steps 3 and 4). #653's Canara special cases are removed from `cashJourney.ts`, `cashJourneyBoard.ts`, `handover.ts`, `CashPaymentActions.tsx` and the Cash journeys page. A payment whose cash is known to be elsewhere shows "Confirmed when the cash reaches the company" instead of Verify / Approve (Payment Requests popup via `awaitsCash`, PI and Order payment rows via `ApproveUnlessCashPending`); database refusals read as sentences through `bankVerifyRefusal`; the three accept dialogs say a custodian's acceptance also confirms.
- **Not changed.** HDFC PRI / SEC (verified only against the bank), the payment lock, "Not received" (final), the Order cash exception (it can no longer arise for new payments), permissions, notification types.
- **Known gaps.** The Order team is not sent the "payment landed" notice when a payment is confirmed by acceptance (the recorder is). A custodian without finance.approve receives the cash but the payment stays Pending for an approver (WARNING only, no screen text). The pull-back of already-Confirmed payments is not built. Not seen on screen.
- **Proof.** `supabase/tests/non_hdfc_confirmed_on_company_receipt_pglite.mjs`: 18 of 18 on in-memory Postgres with the REAL migrations 20270410 → 20270418 → 20270612 (the migration's own apply-time assertions run), including the negative control that Finance and an admin are refused before the cash is in. `src/lib/finance/nonHdfcConfirmedOnCompanyReceipt.test.ts` pins the restated bodies against their sources (only additions) and the screens.
- **Apply / rollout (done 2026-11-11 at the owner's yes).** backup-all + verify-backups first (235 tables, 463,891 rows; all required layers fresh; the optional second copy is still not configured). The file was rehearsed on the real database inside a rolled-back transaction (marker row correct, a re-read after the rollback showed nothing left), then applied ALONE via `db query --file` (begin/commit) + `migration repair --status applied`; `migration list --linked` shows it remote. `20270610000000` (#652) is STILL PENDING on purpose. After the apply: the trigger is live, the helpers are not callable by clients, anon cannot call accept, the 43 payment rows are unchanged, and ONE verified PNB / Paytm / Canara payment has its cash elsewhere (it stays Confirmed: the pull-back candidate). Rollback: drop the trigger and re-emit the three bodies from their sources (the helpers can stay).

- **Owner ask (2026-11-11, from a screenshot of the admin's panel).** The right-hand panel showed Review cards the owner could not act on. ## 2026-11-11 — Canara payments: the paying account instead of "Where is this money now?" (PR #653, squash-merged 2026-11-11 14:38 IST as `06167f61`; its "no handover" half was reversed the same day, see the entry above) · no migration
- **Owner ask.** A Canara payment is money that landed in a bank account, so there is no handover journey. The person adding it (a salesperson, a manager or the owner) gives the account it came from; Nitish (the company cash custodian) then accepts it, meaning the money reached the company. Decisions in chat: the three fields describe the paying account and Bank is any bank (Canara is only BOE's account name); account holder and account number are required, bank, payment details and the screenshot are optional; Nitish's Accept means "reached the company" only (Finance still verifies, rule 3c of the Finance dossier); stored in the existing note, no SQL.
- **Changed.** Send Payment Request, Record Payment and the PI "Add payment" dialog no longer ask "Where is this money now?" for Canara (PNB and Paytm still do; `modeAsksWhereMoneyIs`); they ask for **Account holder name\***, **Account number\*** and **Bank** (`CanaraAccountFields`), and open the proof / reference / screenshot section. The three answers are saved as labelled lines at the top of the note to Finance (`composeCanaraNote`, `src/lib/finance/canaraAccount.ts`), so the payment popup shows them wherever it shows the note. Edit Payment Request no longer shows the "use Hand over cash" line for Canara.
- **After saving.** A Canara payment reads "Waiting for the company to accept" (not "With <recorder>"); no screen offers a Hand over for it (Cash journeys, the unclosed-money card, the modules attention panel, the Hand over cash picker); Nitish's Receive button is called **Accept** with a Canara sentence. The payment stays tracked, so an Order's payments check still lists it until he accepts.
- **Not changed.** The database: `payment_mode_requires_custody` still includes canara, `create_cash_handover` still allows a handover of one (no screen offers it), `receive_cash_directly` is the Accept, nothing is enforced server-side for the two required fields (a form rule). Payments already in the old Canara flow keep their history and open handovers.
- **Proof.** `canaraAccount.test.ts` (20 new), three pin tests restated (`paymentFormsRecordEdit`, `paymentRequestForm`, `custodyTrail`), the Finance and Orders suites (8,759 tests) pass, `tsc` and eslint clean. NOT seen on screen or signed in.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; nothing in the database was touched. Payments saved meanwhile keep their account lines in the note.

## 2026-11-11 — Tasks: automatic escalation removed (PR #652, squash-merged 2026-11-11 14:22 IST as `8ff4dafd`) · migration `20270610000000` → APPLIED 2026-11-12 13:00 IST (see the 2026-11-12 entry)
- **Owner ask.** Remove the escalation feature: "not solving any purpose and just sends comments and messages on repeat after certain hours".
- **What it was.** The hourly pg_cron job `run_task_health_check()` added two lines to a task's Activity, written under the task creator's name: "Auto-escalated: overdue with no action for 24 hours" and "Auto-escalated: no update for 72 hours" (action `escalated`, once per task each). - **Owner ask.** After #649 the console still logged "preloaded but not used" on task pages and the Dashboard ("it never seems to be ending"), and the owner said it happens when clicking View Task Page in the task preview drawer.

Every page using `DashboardLayout` prefetched `/modules` on mount, so the launcher's stylesheet was preloaded and never applied: one warning per page opened. Opening the drawer on `/tasks/my` had already requested every task page in the list (13 tasks, 26+ requests; `/tasks/assigned-by-me`: 8 tasks, 16). The red "message channel closed" error is a Chrome extension (no `chrome.runtime` in the repo or in 30 live bundles). NOT found: what prefetches `/orders` (the `adminDashboard` stylesheet in the owner's last screenshot); not reproducible in that Chrome.
- **Changed.** New `src/hooks/useIntentWarm.ts` (`warmOnRest` after 250 ms, `warmNow` for focus and touch, `cancel`). `DashboardLayout` drops its mount effect `router.prefetch('/modules')`; its Home button warms the launcher on intent. `/tasks/my` and `/tasks/assigned-by-me` drop the fifteen-row warm-up (and `warmedHrefs`); each row warms its own `?returnTo` href when the pointer rests on it. The drawer's View Task Page link still prefetches when the drawer opens, so a quick click stays warm.
- **Not changed.** The Dashboard's load-time warm-up of `/tasks/my` and `/notifications` (same module, no unused stylesheet seen), `NotificationsView`, Quick Actions.
- **Proof.** The 872 tests that read these files pass; `tsc` and eslint clean. NOT checked live yet: after deploy, open My Tasks and then a task drawer and count `_rsc` requests (expect 0-2, not 26+), and open a task page (no `/modules` prefetch at load).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; no data involved.

## 2026-11-11 — Modules launcher: a card warms its route only after the pointer rests 250 ms (#649 merged as 19250a90 at 11:53 IST, 2026-11-11) · no migration
- **Owner ask.** The console again showed 56 "resource ... was preloaded using link preload but not used within a few seconds" warnings (source `modules:1`, owner on `/sales/my-day`), and they asked how to avoid them.
- **Cause, reproduced in the owner's Chrome.** Loading `/modules` alone leaves no unused style preload. Hovering four cards without clicking left four (two were the owner's `0mn.s1bn975w.css` and `0vpo57o72-4do.css`): the 2026-10-19 fix warmed a module's route on pointer-enter, so every card the pointer crossed on the way to Sales preloaded a stylesheet the launcher never applies.
- **Changed.** `src/app/modules/page.tsx` `ModuleCard`: pointer-enter starts a 250 ms timer (`INTENT_DWELL_MS`) that calls the intent handler; pointer-leave and unmount cancel it. Focus and touch-start still warm at once. Edit mode still warms nothing. Guard `src/app/modules/intentPrefetch.test.ts` now fails on raw `onPointerEnter={onIntent}`, requires the leave/unmount cancel and a dwell of 150-500 ms.
- **Not changed.** Quick Actions (`QuickActions.tsx`) still warm on pointer-enter: not seen warning, so not touched. The warning is harmless and DevTools-only; resting on a card and then leaving it will still log it, so it becomes rare, not zero.
- **Proof.** `intentPrefetch.test.ts` and the other tests that read the Modules page source (457) pass; `tsc` and eslint clean. Live check after deploy (production deployment 7703384539 for `19250a90`, success 11:55 IST), in the owner's Chrome with real mouse input and a log of how long the pointer stayed on each card: resting about 1 s on Performance and Meetings prefetched their routes (4 requests each); crossing Attendance & Payroll and Review Workflow in about 30 ms made no request for either route; resting on Admin Control Center prefetched it and preloaded its stylesheet, which is intent, so by design. A rest-then-leave can therefore still log the warning once.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR; no data involved.

## 2026-11-08 — Minop feed: the pull can no longer skip a punch the nightly sync repairs (branch `fix/minop-feed-pull-no-gap`, PR open, NOT merged) · no migration
- **Found while answering the owner's "no data missed in any case".** The pull's cursor followed `first_seen_at`. The nightly sync (`syncMachineAttendance`) adds a punch the webhook failed to record with its OLD receipt time, so an ERP whose cursor had already passed that time never pulled it. Only a punch whose push ALSO failed could be lost, but the promise is no loss in any case.
- **Changed.** The pull now runs in insert order (`created_at`, then `id`), and the cursor carries `created_at`. It lists a punch only once it has been in the table 2 minutes (`PARTNER_SETTLE_MS`), so a write still finishing cannot land behind a cursor just handed out. The push brings fresh punches within seconds, so nothing waits on that. The reply shape, the push and every other part are unchanged. An older cursor still decodes and at worst re-sends punches, which the ERP skips by `id`.
- **Proof.** All 247 Minop tests pass, including a repaired punch with an old receipt time that still comes after the cursor, the settle bound, and the exact query (`created_at=lt.<now - 2 min>`, `order=created_at.asc,id.asc`, the cursor `or` filter on `created_at`). Type check and lint are clean. No index: the table grows by a few thousand rows a year.
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR.

- **Proof.** The two feed test files updated and a test added for a receipt late in the UTC evening (next day in India).
- **Rollback.** Revert the PR.

## 2026-11-07 — Payment Requests: merge a duplicate Sales request into the payment already confirmed (PR #639 squash-merged as 4981893b, 10:21 UTC, on the owner's word; Production deployment 7619898821 success 10:23 UTC) · 20270608000000 → APPLIED 2026-11-07 by the owner, before the merge (the screen calls the new door)
- **Owner request.** A payment is often recorded by the owner and then sent again by Sales (the screenshot: P-AA-0093, ₹13,38,565.00, already the verified Suspense payment P-AA-0089, linked to its credit). The window must say plainly that it is a request, compare it with the confirmed payment, and let the owner merge it: not rejected, nothing for Sales to do, the old record stays and its activity shows who sent the request and that it was merged as a duplicate. Plan, a mockup (Design canvas, five boards), then "Yes, build it" with all four recommended choices: find the earlier payment by its details as well as by the credit it holds; apply Sales' destination to the old payment; the request leaves Sales' list with a notice; HDFC PRI / SEC only.
- **Changed (screens).** In the payment window of a pending HDFC request the bank panel opens with "Sent by <name>", a note that this may already be in Confirmed Payments, and the request beside the confirmed payment (Added by, Amount, Payment date, Account, Bank credit, UTR, Client, For, Proof, a verdict on each) with **Merge into P-AA-xxxx**. Merge asks first and lists what will happen (optional note). When a merge is not allowed the window says why in words and draws no button. The old "unlink, delete, then verify" advice stays only as the fallback when the look-alike cannot be read. New `src/app/finance/bankDuplicate.tsx` and `src/lib/finance/paymentDuplicates.ts`; small edits to `bankMarkUi.tsx`, `page.tsx`, `paymentRequestsDetails.tsx` (the notice), `paymentActivityLabels.ts` + `PaymentRequestActivity.tsx` (two events), `notify.ts` and `/api/finance/notify` (`finance_merged`, sent as the existing `finance_linked` enum value, so no enum change), `globals.css` (new `.bank-dup*` block; stacks on a phone).
- **Changed (database).** `20270608000000_finance_merge_duplicate_payment_request.sql`: `merge_payment_request(request, into, note)`, definer, fixed `search_path`, no anon; needs `finance.reconcile` and `finance.approve`, never on your own request unless you may verify your own. Needs a pending request and a VERIFIED payment of the same HDFC account and the EXACT amount; refused when the old payment is already allocated somewhere else. One transaction: the request's intent (or PI-page allocation) is cancelled / reversed and placed on the old payment through the canonical allocators; its bank links are released and its proof files move; the request is soft-removed with the `delete_own_payment_request` mechanism (not rejected, no new status); `request_merged` and `request_merged_in` are written. The activity `event_type` check is restated with 16 values.
- **Not changed.** No status, permission, Order or PI total, payment ID or amount. A merged request keeps its ids and history; nothing is deleted.
- **Proof.** `supabase/tests/payment_request_merge_pglite.mjs`: 30 checks on the real allocators, freeze trigger and bank unlink, and 15 deliberate breaks of the migration all caught (`MUTATE=<name>`); `paymentDuplicates.test.ts` (23), `bankDuplicate.dom.test.tsx` (17), activity label tests, 18 deliberate breaks of the code all caught; both migration-ledger lists in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts` name the migration. Final full suite on the rebased tree: 20,235 tests, 20,226 passed, 0 failed, 9 skipped. Type check, whole-project lint and `next build` clean. The rehearsal file also ran on PGlite, with and without a seeded P-AA-0093 / P-AA-0089 pair, and on the REAL database in a rolled-back transaction (the owner ran it: `REHEARSAL_OK`, including a real merge of P-AA-0093 into P-AA-0089 as an admin; both rows read back untouched). Not covered: a merge by a non-admin verifier on the real database; not seen signed in or on a phone.
- **Steps taken, in this order.** Backup-all by hand (the one case it is still run by hand; database export 20261107T085716Z, 234 tables, 462,955 rows); the rolled-back rehearsal `supabase db query --linked --file <rehearsal.sql>` (`REHEARSAL_OK`); `db push --dry-run` (only 20270608000000 pending); the owner applied it with `supabase db push` from the worktree and `supabase migration list --linked` showed it in both columns; merge on the owner's word. No backup after the merge (owner decision: the 9:30 pm task covers the day). Limit 300 rows × 50 columns per sheet with a notice; hidden sheets, rows and columns are not shown. `.xls`, `.csv` and unreadable files keep the plain SheetJS table (100 rows). The iframe is gone, so the file link is no longer sent to Microsoft. Download and Open in Tab unchanged.
- **Lockfile trap.** Adding the dependency on Windows made npm drop four optional `@emnapi/*` entries (also with `--package-lock-only`); CI `npm ci` then failed "Missing: @emnapi/runtime@1.11.3". The entries were copied back from main's lock; `npm ci --dry-run` and CI accept it.
- **Not changed.** No database, route or permission. Not addressed: the extension error, and a 10 s pre-request stall seen once in Chrome (owner to test in Incognito / bisect extensions).
- **Proof.** `excelPreview.test.ts` builds a real workbook and reads it back; `attachmentDownload.test.ts` forbids an outside viewer (22 tests). Full suite 20,191 / 20,182 pass / 0 fail / 9 skipped; tsc and lint clean; CI verify and Vercel green. A quotation-shaped workbook rendered with the real `ExcelSheetView` was screenshotted in headless Edge and matches Excel's layout. Production console sweep (15 loads and in-app moves): 0 BOE errors, rejections or `console.error`. NOT seen in the signed-in modal on a real file.

## 2026-11-07 — Modules page: a punch-in strip for every employee (PR #635 squash-merged as 5aa5448f 2026-11-07 05:51Z, deployed 05:53Z) · no SQL
- **Owner request.** Everyone sees their punch-in on the Modules page for 30 minutes after it; anyone not punched in sees a highlighted notice for the rest of the day that goes only if they remove it, and never carries to the next day. The aim is that nobody can say they missed the punch. Plan, then a mockup built from the app's real styles (checked at 1440, 1280 and 390 wide), then "Yes, build it". Owner choices: a strip at the very top (not a For you card); red from 10:15; "Hide for today" remembered on that device only; "On time" / "Late by N minutes" on the green and amber strips.
- **Changed.** New `GET /api/attendance/my-punch` (the caller's OWN punch, no id accepted), `src/lib/minop/punchStrip.ts` (the rules and reads), `src/app/modules/PunchStrip.tsx` + `punchStrip.module.css`, `usePunchStrip.ts` (asks every 60 s; never on window focus, which the app forbids), `punchStripHidden.ts`; one line in `src/app/modules/page.tsx`. Green or amber for 30 minutes from the later of the punch and its arrival at BOE; red from 10:15 until they punch or press Hide for today. The server decides and counts the time left, so a wrong phone clock changes nothing. Says nothing for: no Machine ID, inactive, a Sunday or any holiday, an approved request, a stopped machine, before 1 Nov 2026, a lone punch after 14:00. Not drawn while previewing with View As or arranging cards. Detail: module 04 section 6.
- **Not changed.** No database object, migration, notification, permission or payroll rule. Nothing is written anywhere by this feature.
- **Proof.** `punchStrip.test.ts` (rules, every silent case, own-punch isolation on `MemorySupabase`), `punchStrip.test.tsx` (what each answer draws, route and page wiring), `punchStripHidden.test.ts`; type check and lint clean on the changed files. Full suite on the first build of the code: 20,174 tests, 1 failed, 9 skipped. The failure was mine: `financeRefreshPolicy.test.ts` forbids any query under `src/hooks/queries` from refetching on window focus and the strip's hook had asked for it; removed. That file, the new tests and the Modules page tests were re-run after the fix (166 pass); the whole suite was not run a second time. `npm run build` passes and lists the new route. `permissions:check` was not run (it needs production credentials the worktree must not hold; no permission or module changed). NOT seen signed in or on a real phone.
- **Deploy, checked.** Production deployment 7617307740 succeeded at 05:53Z; `GET /api/attendance/my-punch` with no sign-in answers 401 on boeone.vercel.app. All three PR checks passed. The nightly backup covers the day (owner decision: none per merge).
- **Owner steps.** Link every payroll employee's Machine ID first (a person with none never sees the strip); look at it signed in and on a phone. Rollback: revert the PR; nothing else holds state.

A backup-all with the D: copy followed on 2026-11-07.

## 2026-11-06 — BOE Credits: a review month that is still open follows the saved monthly target (PR #627 squash-merged as ad44cccd by the owner 2026-11-06 05:04Z, deployed 05:06Z) · 20270605000000 → APPLIED 2026-11-06 about 05:12Z on the owner's yes (the migration list shows it in both columns)
- **Owner report.** The admin lowered "Minimum Approved Reviews Per Month" from 3 to 1 on `/payroll/credits`. October's Month close table still read "2 of 3" and "1 of 3", "Below target", and closing October would have lapsed those credits. Cause: each month row copied the target in force when its first review was approved (`minimum_reviews_snapshot`), the guard trigger refused any later change, and the recount qualified only against the copy, so a change to the saved target never reached a month that already had a row. Plan agreed ("Yes, build it"); the owner's intent: each month's qualified-or-not status follows the target they saved.
- **Changed (database).** `20270605000000_boe_credits_open_months_follow_target.sql`: the months guard lets the minimum move only while the month is open and not finalized; `refresh_boe_credit_review_month()` reads the newest saved target for such a month and qualifies against it (closing a month recounts first, so it is judged against the target in force when closed); new `refresh_open_boe_credit_review_months()`; new trigger `boe_credit_settings_refresh_open_months` (AFTER INSERT on `boe_credit_settings`), so saving a target on the BOE Credits page applies it at once; and a one-time recount at the end of the file. Qualified months stay qualified when the target is raised; closed and lapsed months keep the minimum they were closed under and are not reopened. No ledger row is written.
- **THE MONEY STEP.** The one-time recount qualifies every open month that already meets the saved target and so turns its pending credits into spendable ones. With the target at 1 and October as it stood on 2026-11-05 that is two employees (2 reviews = 2 credits, 1 review = 1 credit, about Rs 150). The file's own checks run in the same transaction and abort the apply if the result is not exactly the rule.
- **Changed (screens, wording only).** Month close: "1 verified review" (singular), plus "A month still open follows the target saved now"; the settings form hint and footer say the monthly minimum reaches open months and that qualified or closed months keep theirs; the employee guide line says the same. "x of N" is the month row's own minimum, so it follows the database.
- **Rollout, as it actually went.** Planned: backup, apply, then merge. The owner merged #627 first (05:04:53Z, deployed 05:06:42Z) while the backup was still finishing; harmless, because the merged code is wording only, but for about ten minutes the screens said open months follow the target while the database did not. Backup `database\20261106T045007Z` (234 tables, 462,536 rows, pg_dump OK for the 3 restricted finance tables, `verify-backups` all required layers fresh; the second copy failed because D: was not plugged in). Then, on "Yes, apply it": the file in the worktree was byte-identical to merged main (sha-256 58f317be…); a read-only preview of production showed the saved target at 1 and exactly two open months that would qualify (October, 3 reviews, 3.00 credits), plus one September month with 0 reviews that would stay open; `supabase db push --dry-run` listed only `20270605000000`; `db push` applied it (exit 0). Rollback in the migration header restores behaviour only; a qualified month stays qualified by design.
- **Result read back from production after the apply (no names).** October: 2 months now `qualified` under minimum 1 (3 reviews, 3 credits), 1 month still `qualified` under 3 (4 reviews, 5 credits, untouched). September: 2 months `qualified` under 3 (7 reviews, 7 credits, untouched), 1 month `open` with 0 reviews now carrying minimum 1. Total provisional credits 0.00 (it was 3.00). The settings trigger exists; `anon` and `authenticated` cannot execute the recount. The credit balance view was not used to total spendable credits: it is read under a login with no `auth.uid()`, so that figure is not trusted. Not seen signed in by the owner: Month close should now read "2 of 1" and "1 of 1", Qualified.
- **Proof.** `supabase/tests/boe_credits_open_months_target_pglite.mjs` (real chain on PGlite, 29 checks: reproduces the reported defect before the file, applies it twice, lower / raise / approve / reverse / close / guard / privileges, and four deliberately broken copies each stopped by the file's own check), `openMonthsTarget.test.ts`, the two migration ledgers, type check, lint, full suite (see the PR). Not seen signed in; production data was not read (reads refused), so the real October months were not rehearsed, only the same numbers on a replayed schema.

## 2026-11-05 — Payroll: the payslip lists the month's review credits (PR #625 squash-merged as aa1f4b46, 2026-11-05 19:04Z, deployed 19:07Z) · no SQL
- **Owner request.** Jasvi's October payslip showed one "BOE Credit Addition" of ₹75 (1.5 credits) although she had four approved reviews that month, each with its own credit. Cause: a review's credit is only EARNED on the ledger; it reaches salary when the employee applies it from their own payslip, and the payslip showed only the applied amount. Plan agreed ("Yes, build it"): show the breakdown, do not change how credits become salary.
- **Changed.** The payslip detail payload (admin and employee) gains `review_credits`; a new card "BOE Credits · October reviews" sits under Salary Calculation and Adjustments: one row per approved review (day, Text or Image review, reference, credit; a reversed credit is struck through and not counted), then Earned from the month's reviews (with the rupee value at the current rate), Applied to this salary, Spendable now (across all months), the month's target sentence (pending / reached / lapsed) and, for the admin only, that credits reach salary only when the employee applies them. No card for an employee with no review credit that month. New `src/lib/payroll/reviewCredits.ts` (pure), `reviewCreditsStore.ts` (the read) and `src/components/payroll/ReviewCreditsCard.tsx`; `PayrollDetailView` and `resultDetailPayload.ts` pass it through. "Applied" is read from the settlement and the live balance from the employee page's own figure, because that page refreshes those after Apply / Change / Remove and would otherwise show the card stale until a reload. A read error drops the card and logs; the payslip still opens.
- **Not changed.** No salary figure, no settlement arithmetic, no engine input. Auto-applying a month's credits (so nobody has to press Apply) was NOT built: it changes the rule decided in Phase 1D and moves money for every employee, so it waits for an owner decision.
- **Database.** None (reads five existing tables). Rollback: revert the PR.
- **Proof.** `reviewCredits.test.ts`, `resultDetailPayload.reviewCredits.test.ts`, `ReviewCreditsCard.test.tsx` (see the dossier); full suite 20,036 tests, 0 failed; type check, lint and build clean. Not seen signed in, on a phone, or against Jasvi's real October data (production reads were refused to the agent).
- **Merge.** On the owner's yes ("Yes, merge it"), after they thought they had merged it: GitHub still showed #625 open with every check green, so the agent squash-merged it with `gh pr merge --squash`, pinned to head `5262a15e` and run only while the state read `OPEN CLEAN`. Production deployment `7582756337` (GitHub deployments API) succeeded at 19:07:06Z, about 2 minutes after the merge. The live bundle was not grepped. No backup was run after the merge (owner decision: the 9:30 pm task covers the day).
