## 2026-10-21 — Salesperson dashboard + notification: PIs sent back for changes / rejected (PR #383, merged 6be85e43) · migrations 20270328000100 + 20270328000200 (APPLIED to production 2026-10-21 with `db push --include-all`, because 20270328000300 from main was already applied)
- **Owner request.** When a reviewer presses Needs Changes or Reject on a PI, the salesperson must be told and see it on the Orders dashboard, with how many PIs are still pending.
- **Notification (20270328000100):** `order_submission_review_notify()` (20270321000001) already wrote `pi_review_decided` to submitted_by/created_by with the reviewer's note; it now also reaches `salesperson_id`. App: the badge follows the title, amber "Changes requested" / red "Rejected" (`notificationMeta.ts`).
- **Dashboard (20270328000200):** `salesperson_orders_dashboard()` restated with `returned` (every needs_changes PI, no age limit) and `rejected` (last 7 days), each with note, reviewer name and age; owners = salesperson_id OR created_by OR submitted_by. "Pending approval" is unchanged (status submitted, salesperson_id only). UI: "Needs your attention" block on top, plus two cards (Sent Back for Changes, Rejected). Parser treats a payload without the lists as none, so code is safe before the migration.
- Rollout (done): both migrations applied, then merged; live function bodies verified afterwards. Rollback: re-apply the 20270321000001 function body and the 20270228000000 function (and keep 20270328000000 orders_dashboard_overview intact).
- Tests: new `salespersonDashboardReturned.test.tsx`; `salespersonDashboard.test.tsx` card count 3 → 5. SQL not executed against a database (no Docker rehearsal yet). Not verified on screen.

## 2026-10-21 — Order page summary: "Total product value" row replaced by a Shipping and billing details link; due-date rule no longer overlaps its label (PR open) · no migration
- **Owner request.** On `/orders/[id]`, "Client and value" lost the **Total product value** row; its place is a link, **Shipping and billing details**, that opens a dialog with the PI's bill-to and ship-to (name + address each, `Not provided` when absent, never merged). Data is the already-loaded `piHandoff.client` (`ClientDetails.billTo/shipTo`) — no new read, no schema. The value is still in the Commercial breakdown and the Orders list; `orderSummaryFields` still builds it (unchanged), `orderSummaryView` just no longer draws it.
- **Overlap bug:** the overdue Due date row's red inset rule sat on top of its label. Cause: the Notion-pass reset `.order-sum-row { padding: 7px 0 }` (end of globals.css) beat the flagged rows' earlier `padding-left`. Fixed by restating `padding-left: 12px` for `--amber`/`--red` last; a render test pins that it comes after the reset.
- Tests: `orderWorkspace.render.test.tsx` (link, dialog, no-amount header, CSS order). Not verified on screen in a signed-in session.

## 2026-10-21 — Notifications: task pages warmed on intent only, not on load (PR open) · no migration
- **Owner report:** Chrome's "preloaded using link preload but not used" for the Task page stylesheet on every Notifications visit, and a slow-feeling open. Cause: `NotificationsView` prefetched the first 12 tasks in an effect and each card title `<Link>` also viewport-prefetched; measured on production 12 RSC requests for 4 tasks (3 per task).
- **Now:** `prefetch={false}` on the title link; `onWarmTask` (page-owned `router.prefetch`) fires on pointer-enter / focus / touch-start. Tests: new `notificationsIntentPrefetch.test.ts`; `taskReturnPath.test.ts` follows the move.
- **Not proven:** the 5-6 s hang did not reproduce (about 1 s warm on production); the fix removes the warning's cause and the request burst.
- Unrelated, found on clean main: `ordersFinanceUsability.test.tsx` "the target link is a real link" fails (reads `ReceivedPaymentsView.tsx`; the handler now lives in `receivedPaymentsTable.tsx`).

## 2026-10-21 — PI Drafts list trim: Grand total column and the date under Salesperson removed, header "Advance %" (PR open) · no migration
- **Owner request after seeing the live list.** Removed the **Grand total** column (table and cards; Product value stays — `grandTotal`/`grandTotalMissing` stay on the row model and the select, the PI detail page still states the total), the workbook "Date of creation" printed under the salesperson's name (it is G20 of the PI file, typed by the preparer, not an upload or status date; `authoredOn` stays on the model), and shortened the column header "Advance % received" → **"Advance %"** so it is not truncated. Column widths re-balanced for 8 columns. Tests: `draftsAccess` pins.

## 2026-10-21 — PI Drafts list: Uploaded by / Submitted out; Confirmed date, Due date, Advance % received in (PR open) · migration 20270327000000 (APPLIED to production 2026-10-21)
- **Owner request.** `/orders/drafts` table (and the narrow-screen cards) lost **Uploaded by** and **Submitted**; gained **Confirmed date** (`order_confirmation_date`), **Due date** (`due_date`) and **Advance % received** after Salesperson. Both dates are the app's Internal-details dates (blank = "—"), read by adding the two columns to `PI_DRAFT_LIST_COLUMNS`. Search no longer matches uploader/submitter names; the list's `users` name lookup (and `reviewerRef`) is gone, `describeDraftListEntry` lost `uploader`/`uploadedAt`/`submitter` and gained `confirmedOn`/`dueOn`/`advancePercent` (`formatAdvancePercent`).
- **Advance % received** = verified + awaiting-verification payments ÷ Total before GST, truncated to 2 dp (the detail page's `attached_percent`, owner choice over verified-only). New read-only RPC `pi_submissions_advance_percent(uuid[])` returns it for every listed PI in ONE call (≤ 500 ids), `SECURITY DEFINER`, filtered through `can_view_order_submission` (unviewable ids return no row), `authenticated` only. Mirrors the allocation filter and base test of `pi_submission_payment_summary` (20270227). Called from `load()` after the rows arrive; a failed call or no usable base prints "—", never 0%. Rollback: `drop function public.pi_submissions_advance_percent(uuid[]);`.
- **DEPLOY ORDER (done): 20270327000000 was applied before merge.** Without it the RPC errors and the whole column shows "—" (the list itself still loads). SQL not executed locally (no Docker); pinned by text in `piDraftsListAdvancePercentSchema.test.ts` — execute it once applied.
- Tests updated: `draftsAccess`, `submissionWorkflow`, `submissionDeletion`, `orderStartupShape` (PI Drafts count stays 4: one `users` read swapped for one `rpc`).
## 2026-10-21 — Admin can permanently delete a meeting with all its data (PR open) · migration `20270326000000_meetings_admin_delete.sql` (UNAPPLIED)
- **Owner request:** dummy/test meetings could not be removed (no delete UI; triggers refuse any meeting with orders/discussion; evidence FK has no cascade).
- **Now:** administrators see **Delete** on the meeting page and a trash icon on desktop list rows. A dialog shows counts and wants the word DELETE typed. `POST /api/meetings/delete` runs `admin_delete_meeting(uuid)` AS THE CALLER (function re-checks active admin), then erases the Storage files with the service role (evidence + product photos no other line references).
- **Goes:** meeting, attendees, orders/SKU lines, update history, activity log, evidence, its agenda appearances and per-meeting issue-trail rows. **Stays:** tasks (link nulled), discussion issues (open ones with no appearance return to the Inbox), issue-level trail rows. One row per deletion in `meeting_deletion_audit` (admin-readable).
- Both delete refusals (`meetings_prevent_delete_with_content/_with_discussion`) keep their behaviour for everyone else; they skip only when a transaction-local marker (`app.admin_deleting_meeting`) names that meeting, set by the function. Mobile cards have no trash icon; delete from the meeting page.
- Tests: `src/lib/meetings/adminDelete.test.ts`. Migration rehearsed in BEGIN…ROLLBACK against the linked DB (plain delete still refused, employee refused, admin deletes, repeat = not found, zero residue).

## 2026-10-20 — Admin can edit any payment inside the payments popup, PI Draft and Order pages (PR open) · no migration
- **Owner request:** fix a wrong date/amount/mode/reference without leaving the order for Finance. The Order page's "Payments awaiting verification" / "Verified payments" popup (`OrderPaymentListDialog`) had no Edit at all (that is why the owner saw none); the PI Draft popup (`PiPaymentDetailsModal`) had Edit only on awaiting rows.
- **Now:** an administrator (PI page `canAdminAmend`, Order page `actingAsAdmin`) sees **Edit** on every live payment row — awaiting verification AND confirmed (not rejected, not a reversed allocation). `PiPaymentEditDialog` reads the row and picks Finance's form by status: awaiting → `paymentRequestsEdit` `EditPaymentModal` (`edit_payment_request` RPC, unchanged); confirmed → `finance/received/receivedPaymentsEdit` `EditPaymentModal` (imported, not copied; its database guards decide and its error banner shows a refusal). After saving the PI page runs `loadPayments()`, the Order page runs `loadOrder()`, so the list shows corrected values. New helpers `isConfirmedPayment`, `isCorrectablePayment` in `lib/finance/piPaymentView.ts`.
- No client write added to the pages or the dialog. Tests: `piPaymentDetails.render` updated/extended. Run `orderWorkspace.render` with `src/app/orders/*id*/…`.
- Not browser-checked as a signed-in admin.
## 2026-10-20 — Edit Received Payment: "Received in" removed, quiet Notion-style form (PR open) · no migration
- **Owner request.** Finance > Confirmed Payments > Edit Received Payment no longer asks "Received in": Payment mode (HDFC PRI / HDFC SEC / PNB / Paytm / Canara) already names the account. `finance_payment_requests.received_in` is nullable and unwritten by every other path since 20261013, so the edit no longer sends it — the stored value is left untouched (no derivation, no migration). The other payment forms already had no such field.
- Redesign: customer shown as a plain value (not a disabled input), a "Payment" section, small sentence-case muted labels over larger darker values, calmer spacing. `PaymentDetailsFields` gained an opt-in `quiet` prop (other forms keep their bold capital labels). `RECEIVED_IN_OPTIONS` in `receivedPaymentsModel.tsx` is now unused.
- Tests: `paymentForms.dom.test.tsx` (no select, `received_in` absent from the payload, customer is text).

## 2026-10-20 — Payment verification modal redesign (PR open) · no migration
- **Visual only; every RPC, decision, note rule and notification is unchanged.** Labels are small, regular, muted; values larger and dark (figure band: Amount 28px / Payment Mode 20px / Payment Date 16px). Client, Against, Submitted by, Reference and Note are a Notion-style property list (`PropertyRow`, label left, value right). Decision is a quiet radio list (outline + tint + filled dot on the chosen item); Activity moved to the full-width `bottom` zone so a phone reads details, decision, history; Cancel/Confirm footer wraps cleanly on phones.

Under it one **Download** row of small buttons: **Excel** and **ERP** (blue-tinted, the two people use), **Pictures (N) · ZIP**, then a quiet **PDF** last. The PDF button now downloads (`onOpenPdf(id, true)`).

## 2026-10-20 — Quotation completed / cancelled now tells the sales person who raised it (PR not yet opened) · no migration
- **Owner rule (amends the Sept 2026 "quotations are silent" rule):** when the assignee marks a quotation request complete, its creator is notified ("<name> completed task"); when an admin cancels one, its creator is notified ("<name> cancelled a task"). Only the creator; never the assignee; the creator cancelling their own request tells nobody; comments, status moves, reopening stay silent.
- Write side: `shouldNotifyTaskStatusEvent(task, action, recipientId)` (quotation: `completed` to `created_by` only) used by `/api/notify-status-update`; `cancellationRecipient()` used by `/api/cancel-task`. No client change (Mark Complete already announces through `sendStatusNotice`).
- Read side: the Task feed hid every quotation row; it now shows the two ending notices. PostgREST cannot OR across the task join, so the feed is two queries merged (counts added, lists merged newest-first, bulk ids unioned). Old pre-Sept completion notices on quotations may reappear (owner accepted). Tests: `taskNotificationPolicy`, `taskFeedExclusion`, `routeBehaviour` (fake PostgREST now ANDs repeated `or`), five source pins updated.
- Not changed: Restore/Reopen of a quotation stays silent.

- **ERP button (owner):** the Product pictures block on the Confirmed Order's Documents card has an **ERP** download button. It saves `<order no> ERP line items.xlsx`, built in the browser (`lib/orders/erpLineItems.ts`, `xlsx` imported on demand) with the production team's exact 23-column header (`line_items_template.xlsx`, sheet `Sheet 1 - line_items_template`). Filled: Item Name (= Name), Description, Quantity, Unit Price (= Cost per piece). Inventory Code is blank on purpose. Size, material and customization are ONE cell in Description (`Dimensions: …`, `Material: …`, `Customization: …`, each on its own line in the cell, empty parts omitted). Inventory Code, Item Type, the dimension columns, remarks, approvals and PO-allowed columns are left empty on purpose.
- **Picture names (owner):** "Download all (ZIP)" now names each picture by its product's Order Product Code: `524-BE0001.jpg` for a single picture; `524-BE0001A`, `B`, `C` … when a product has several (representative first). A row with no code keeps the old descriptive name.
- Proof: `erpLineItems.test.ts` (new), `productPictures.test.ts` (2 new); tsc and eslint clean.

## 2026-10-20 — PI upload accepts the shorter "Dimension in inches" heading (PR open) · no migration
- Complaint: every PI upload was refused with `TEMPLATE_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH` ("D31 holds "Dimension in inches""). Nothing in the app had changed: the parser has required "Dimension in inches & Shape" since 2026-08-16; the sheets Sales were now building carried the shorter heading (a different/edited template copy).

The canonical name is still what messages and the preview report. Any other dimension heading is still refused; both headings on one row are still ambiguous and refused.

## 2026-10-20 — Product code BE0001, Pending-from-client pendencies, Excel-style Commercial breakdown (PR open) · migration 20270325000000 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-20 (rehearsed in BEGIN…ROLLBACK first, then `db push`; only this migration was pending)
- **Product codes (owner):** every product code is now `<order no>-BE0001` — four digits, no dash after BE (`534-BE0001`, `524-BE0010`) — for ALL Orders, old and new. Display-only: `order_product_codes.boe_sequence` is an integer and the string is built in `lib/orders/orderProductCodes.ts` (`BOE_ITEM_CODE_DIGITS = 4`), so no data was rewritten and no migration is involved. The PI-revision line-matching dialog still receives the old 3-digit label from SQL (20270116/20270120) and re-pads it with `normalizeBoeItemCode`. Order products column widened 88 → 108px; PDF/Excel documents use the same helper.
- **Pendencies:** new `order_pendencies` + `add_/clear_/reopen_/remove_order_pendency` + `can_manage_order_pendencies` (`20270325000000_order_pendencies.sql`; RLS select-only, RPC writes, self-assertions). The convert dialog (`PiApproveOrderModal`) takes any number of free-text pendencies; they are saved right after the Order exists via `saveNewOrderPendencies` (approve_order_submission is NOT restated, so a failure never undoes the Order — the PI page says so). They show on the Order page inside Fabric & Finish (`OrderPendencies.tsx`, self-loading) as open reminders with Mark cleared / Remove, cleared ones under a disclosure with Reopen. Without the migration the Order page simply hides the block and the convert dialog's save reports the failure.
- **Commercial breakdown:** Excel-style bordered label|amount grid, subtle alternate-row tint, final row keeps its blue tint; the "fabric provided by BOE / client" sub-label is a small italic tag. CSS block appended at the end of globals.css.
- Proof: tsc clean; new `orderPendencies.test.tsx`; `orderProductCodes.test.ts`, `piVersionPdf.test.ts`, `piLineReview.render.test.tsx` updated to the new format. Migration replayed in a scratch Postgres 16 against STUBS of its dependencies (orders, users, auth.uid, the two authority functions): applies, re-applies idempotently, self-assertions pass; add/clear/reopen/remove, duplicate and blank handling, outsider refusal, approver permission, direct-insert refusal and cancelled-Order refusal all behaved. NOT proven against the real migration chain or production — run `supabase db push --linked --dry-run` and apply before merging.

## 2026-10-20 — Lead source is now required to submit a PI (PR open) · no migration
- Owner rule: Sales must choose the lead source before submitting, so management is no longer asked for it at approval (the workbook has no lead source to preselect from). Salesperson stays optional at Submit (the approval dialog preselects the workbook's exact match, PR #364).
- `ORDER_DETAILS_FIELDS.lead_source` need is now `submission`; `orderDetailsSubmissionGaps` names "Lead source" (blank or not one of the five); the Internal details field carries the red star; `buildPiCompletion` no longer lists it under "needed later".
- Enforcement is client-side (checklist + disabled Submit), like the other internal-detail gaps' display; the database authority for lead source remains `approve_order_submission`. PIs already submitted without one are unaffected and are asked once at approval. A PI returned for changes must have one before it is resubmitted.
- Tests: `salesOrderDetails.test.tsx`, `piCompletion.test.ts`, `piFormRedesign.render.test.tsx` updated.

## 2026-10-20 — Approval dialog preselects the salesperson the PI workbook names (PR open) · no migration
- Complaint: Create Confirmed Order asked for the BOE salesperson although the PI page showed "Salesperson named in PI workbook: <name>". Cause: that line is the workbook's typed author name; a PI Sales submitted without choosing a salesperson has `salesperson_id` NULL (and `lead_source` NULL), so the dialog opened empty. Dates were already prefilled and shown "From the PI".
- Change: when Sales saved nobody, the dialog preselects the BOE user whose name EXACTLY matches the workbook name (case/spacing ignored, whole name, exactly ONE active user: `matchWorkbookSalesperson` in `lib/orders/orderConfirmation.ts`). Shown as "From the PI workbook" with a Change link; no match, a partial name or two people with the same name still select nobody and show the name as a hint. Reverses the earlier "never match by name" rule on purpose (owner request); never the viewer or submitter; the database still validates the id.
- NOT changed: lead source has no source on a PI Sales left blank, so it is still asked at approval. Salesperson and lead source remain optional at Submit (`piCompletion.ts` "later" list) — making them required at Submit is a separate decision.
- Tests: `piApprovalModals.render.test.tsx` (2, 2b, 3) and `salesOrderDetails.test.tsx` updated.

## 2026-10-20 — Two HDFC accounts: HDFC PRI and HDFC SEC (PR open) · migration 20270324000000 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-20 (applied alone; the two 20270321 migrations were left unapplied)
- BOE holds two HDFC accounts but a payment could only say "HDFC". Payment modes are now `hdfc` (shown **HDFC PRI**, primary) and new `hdfc_sec` (shown **HDFC SEC**, secondary). No row is rewritten: every existing `hdfc` payment reads as HDFC PRI; correcting one that really went to the secondary is an ordinary payment edit.
- `20270324000000_payment_mode_hdfc_secondary.sql`: widens the `payment_mode` CHECK and `payment_mode_is_current()` (the one list every entry door asks — the entry/edit RPCs are NOT redefined) and restates only the enforce-trigger's refusal sentence. Additive, so the old code keeps working once it is applied. Custody rule unchanged (HDFC SEC has no custody trail).
- Code: `PAYMENT_MODES` / helper lines / error sentences in `lib/finance/paymentEntry.ts` and `splitPaymentEntry.ts` (every Payment Request, Record Payment, Edit and PI payment picker reads that list); legacy `(bank_transfer, company_account)` pair and `RECEIVED_IN_*` labels now read "HDFC PRI". Expenses are untouched on purpose: they have their own seven outgoing routes and no HDFC value (pinned by `expenseForm.render.test.tsx`).
- Proof: migration + its executed self-assertions run against the linked DB inside BEGIN…ROLLBACK (constraint restored after); tests updated and `paymentModeHdfcSecondaryMigration.test.ts` added; migration added to both ledger lists in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`.

## 2026-10-19 — Console "preloaded but not used" warning: other modules are warmed on intent, not on load (PR open) · no migration
- Root cause: the Modules launcher prefetched every module route in an effect on load, and Quick Actions prefetched /exhibition-leads/add and /finance/expenses/new. A prefetched route brings its stylesheet, which the browser preloads; the current page never applies it, so Chrome logged "was preloaded using link preload but not used within a few seconds" for Control Center, Inventory and Exhibition Leads CSS, while the burst of requests competed with the page's own data.
- Fix: Module cards (not in edit mode) and Quick Action buttons now call router.prefetch on pointer-enter, focus and touch-start only. Same-module warm-ups (Dashboard -> /tasks/my, /notifications, /modules) are unchanged.
- Guard: src/app/modules/intentPrefetch.test.ts. Not verified in a browser against production; the owner should check the console after deploy.

## 2026-10-19 — Payment proof drop zone: click, drag-and-drop, paste a screenshot; admin can attach on Edit (PR open) · migration 20270323000000 → UNAPPLIED (apply BEFORE merging)
- One control, `ProofDropZone` (`finance/components/ProofDropZone.tsx`), replaces the small "📎 Attach" button on Send Payment Request, Record Payment and the PI "Add payment" dialog, and is NEW on Edit Payment Request (which never had an upload). Click to choose, drop a file, or press Ctrl+V anywhere in the dialog to paste a screenshot (a paste with no file is left to the text box). Shows a thumbnail/PDF icon with size, Replace and Remove. Pasted `image.png` is renamed `payment-proof-<date>-<time>.png`.
- Other image formats: `lib/finance/proofIntake.ts` redraws BMP/AVIF/TIFF etc. as JPEG in the browser (white background), or refuses in plain words if the browser cannot read it; SVG is never accepted. The bucket's allowed types (JPEG, PNG, WEBP, GIF, PDF) and 10 MB limit are unchanged.
- Edit form: saves the correction first, then uploads (`attachPaymentProof`, same helper as the other forms); if the upload fails the correction stays saved and the button becomes "Retry proof upload" (nothing saved twice).
- `20270323000000_finance_payment_proof_admin_attach.sql`: until now only the submitter, only while pending, could attach a proof, so an admin correcting someone else's payment could not add the screenshot. New `admin_may_attach_payment_proof(text)` + two extra INSERT policies (proof row, proof file): an ACTIVE role admin, on a payment that is unverified (pending / needs clarification / rejected) and not deleted, creator = themselves. Submitter policies untouched; a verified payment still takes no new proof from anyone. Proof: executed on a scratch Postgres (self-assertions pass) plus a 14-case allow/refuse probe (admin vs submitter vs stranger × pending / clarification / verified / deleted) — all as intended.
- Tests: new `proofIntake.test.ts`, `paymentProofAdminAttachMigration.test.ts`; `proofReferenceSection.test.ts` updated deliberately (every form, Edit included, now uses the drop zone); migration added to both ledger lists in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`. Full offline suite: only the live-DB suites fail (no database offline), as on main.

## 2026-10-19 — Correct a pending payment that is already attached to a PI (#360) · migration 20270322000000 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-19 (applied alone; the two 20270321 migrations were left unapplied)
- Follow-up to #357. Its Edit buttons opened the form, but saving a payment recorded from a PI failed ("could not be submitted"): that payment carries an ALLOCATION (written by `record_pi_submission_payment`), and `edit_payment_request` only knew the intent model — it refused it (`PAYMENT_EDIT_ALLOCATED`, or `PAYMENT_BELOW_ALLOCATED` / `INTENT_EXCEEDS_PAYMENT` when the amount moved), and the form drew it as a Suspense Entry.
- New `20270322000000_finance_payment_edit_reallocates.sql` restates `edit_payment_request` (same signature and grants). An UNVERIFIED payment with EXACTLY ONE active allocation is now correctable: the destination is locked to the allocation's target (`PAYMENT_EDIT_DESTINATION_LOCKED`); an allocation covering the whole payment follows a changed amount — reversed with a reason ("Payment corrected before verification: X changed to Y.", row kept with who/when) and re-created on the SAME target through `allocate_payment_to_target_internal`, one transaction; a partial allocation stays and the amount may not fall below it; several allocations are refused by name (`PAYMENT_EDIT_SPLIT_ALLOCATED`). The PI is locked before the payment (project lock order). Verified payments are still refused. Permission unchanged: the submitter or an admin.
- Edit form (`paymentRequestsEdit.tsx`): reads the active allocation when there is no intent (`loadPaymentAllocation`), shows the real destination locked, says when the attached amount will follow, blocks a split payment. `paymentEntryErrorMessage` now says these refusals in words.
- Proof: `supabase/tests/run_payment_edit_reallocates_suite.sh` (disposable Postgres; production allocation guards installed verbatim by `_payment_edit_reallocates_extra_schema.sql`; defect reproduces before, 8 groups of assertions pass after, migration applies twice in one transaction); `run_payment_entry_edit_race.sh <host> --with-20270322` (edit vs approval, both directions); `payment_custody_and_modes_assertions` still pass with the migration applied.

## 2026-10-19 — Orders notifications from the database (step 2, PR open) · migrations 20270321000000 + 20270321000001 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-20 (code re-landed, see the entry above)
- `20270321000000_order_submission_notification_types.sql`: enum values `pi_correction_requested/resolved/rejected` (the route inserted these; every insert failed until now), `pi_review_requested`, `pi_review_decided`, `order_change_requested`, `order_change_decided`, `order_update_approval`. Its own file: a new enum value cannot be used in the transaction that adds it.
- `20270321000001_order_submission_notifications_complete.sql`: notices written IN THE SAME TRANSACTION as the activity — trigger on `order_activity_log` (status, amendments, production, payment verified/rejected/linked/unlinked — fixes "payments never reach the Order" because the verify trigger already writes one activity row per allocated Order — sample changes; advance hold / Order exception to the salesperson), `order_approval_events` (Fabric & Finish), `order_change_requests` (raised → admins, decided → requester), `order_submissions` (sent for approval → approve_order holders, deferred to commit so a self-approved PI pages nobody; returned/rejected → owner), `orders` insert from a PI (`order_converted` → PI owner + salesperson), `order_operations_handoffs` decision → salesperson. Shared helper `order_notify_users`: never the actor, never inactive/deleted, no repeat of the same type+record to the same person within 2 minutes (so the existing browser routes cannot double a notice); recipients from `order_notification_recipients` (`order_update_recipient_ids`). Every trigger swallows its own error (WARNING) so a notice never undoes an action. Rehearsed on the linked DB inside BEGIN…ROLLBACK (found and fixed a text-typed NULL in task_id).
- App: the new types in `ORDER_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` + `notificationMeta` (PI review/correction open the PI; change requests and Fabric & Finish open the Order); the PI page and the split-payment modal now send `finance_submitted` like the Finance form (the PI page sends only the payment id; `/api/finance/notify` reads the number under the caller's own session, so the PI page still touches no Finance table). Tests: `hiddenOrderTypes`, new `ordersNotificationsMigration`, migration allowlists (`participantAndOrderTotalSecurity`, `notificationSystemActivity`), and `designationLevels` allows this one migration to read `designation_level` for the Super Admin RECIPIENT category only (it grants nothing). **Apply both migrations before merging.**

## 2026-10-18 — App-context system and backup system introduced
- Added `docs/app-context/` (this folder): `00_README_FIRST.md`, `01_APP_CONTEXT.md`, `02_MASTER_INSTRUCTION_SHEET.md`, `03_BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md`, `04_NEW_MODULE_CHECKLIST.md`, `modules/*.md` dossiers, and the generated `APP_INVENTORY.generated.md` (`scripts/generate-app-inventory.mjs`).
